diff --git a/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/program/aggregate_facts.rs b/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/program/aggregate_facts.rs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000..10002e01ee
--- /dev/null
+++ b/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/program/aggregate_facts.rs
@@ -0,0 +1,166 @@
+//! Exact non-empty facts established by successful count guards.
+
+use super::*;
+use std::collections::BTreeSet;
+
+pub(super) const NONEMPTY: &str = "$aggregateNonempty";
+/// Capture marker for a local whose current value is an exact `count(domain)`
+/// observation. It lives on the result and every domain root: ordinary
+/// rebinding, a write, an authority edge, or a control-flow meet therefore
+/// destroys stale provenance without repurposing diagnostic `origins`.
+pub(super) const COUNT_CAPTURE: &str = "$aggregateCountCapture";
+
+impl Binder<'_> {
+    /// A non-empty observation survives evaluation of its guard only when the
+    /// complete condition is write-free. Unlike point facts, aggregate facts
+    /// deliberately admit filters and seeks because those are part of the
+    /// exact domain identity recorded below.
+    pub(super) fn aggregate_guard_safe(&self, value: &A) -> bool {
+        match value {
+            A::Name { .. } | A::Focal { .. } | A::Literal { .. } => true,
+            A::Array { items, .. } => items.iter().all(|item| self.aggregate_guard_safe(item)),
+            A::Object { fields, .. } => fields.iter().all(|field| field.value.as_ref().is_none_or(|value| self.aggregate_guard_safe(value))),
+            A::Unary { value, .. } | A::Assert { value, .. } | A::ShapeUse { base: value, .. } => self.aggregate_guard_safe(value),
+            A::Binary { left, right, .. } => self.aggregate_guard_safe(left) && self.aggregate_guard_safe(right),
+            A::Member { base, .. } => self.aggregate_guard_safe(base),
+            A::Call { name, args, .. } => self.catalog.functions.get(name).is_none_or(|function|
+                self.pure_functions.get(&function.name) == Some(&true))
+                && args.iter().all(|arg| self.aggregate_guard_safe(arg)),
+            A::Read(read) | A::Exists { read, .. } | A::Created { read, .. } => {
+                read.computed.values().all(|value| self.aggregate_guard_safe(value))
+                    && read.keys.iter().all(|key| self.aggregate_guard_safe(&key.value))
+                    && read.seeks.iter().all(|seek| self.aggregate_guard_safe(&seek.value))
+                    && read.filter.as_deref().is_none_or(|filter| self.aggregate_guard_safe(filter))
+                    && read.matching.as_deref().is_none_or(|matching| self.aggregate_guard_safe(matching))
+                    && read.range.as_ref().and_then(|range| range.cursor.as_deref()).is_none_or(|cursor| self.aggregate_guard_safe(cursor))
+            }
+            A::Shape { base, fields, .. } => self.aggregate_guard_safe(base)
+                && fields.iter().all(|field| match field {
+                    ShapeField::Field { value, .. } => self.aggregate_guard_safe(value),
+                    ShapeField::Spread { .. } | ShapeField::NamedSpread { .. } | ShapeField::Bare { .. } => true,
+                }),
+            A::If { then, condition, otherwise, .. } => self.aggregate_guard_safe(then)
+                && self.aggregate_guard_safe(condition)
+                && self.aggregate_guard_safe(otherwise),
+            A::Merge(_) | A::Walk { .. } | A::Write { .. } => false,
+        }
+    }
+
+    /// Record only implications that follow from count's nonnegative range.
+    /// The common authored form is `require n > 0`, where `n` was bound from
+    /// `count(domain)`; direct count calls use the same identity.
+    // @ref LLP 3000.013.001#4-non-empty-domain-proof — the fact belongs to the exact domain roots and transaction state.
+    pub(super) fn record_aggregate_nonempty(&mut self, condition: &A, truth: bool) {
+        if !truth { return; }
+        let A::Binary { op, left, right, .. } = condition else { return };
+        let (candidate, threshold, op) = if let Some(n) = literal_int(right) {
+            (left.as_ref(), n, op.as_str())
+        } else if let Some(n) = literal_int(left) {
+            let reversed = match op.as_str() { "<" => ">", "<=" => ">=", ">" => "<", ">=" => "<=", other => other };
+            (right.as_ref(), n, reversed)
+        } else { return };
+        let proves_positive = match op {
+            ">" => threshold >= 0,
+            ">=" | "=" => threshold >= 1,
+            "!=" => threshold == 0,
+            _ => false,
+        };
+        if !proves_positive { return; }
+        let Some((key, roots)) = self.count_key(candidate) else { return };
+        for root in roots {
+            let Some(mut binding) = self.binding(&root).cloned() else { return };
+            binding.fields.insert(key.clone(), Ty::Bool);
+            self.scopes.last_mut().expect("a scope").insert(root, binding);
+        }
+    }
+
+    pub(super) fn aggregate_nonempty(&self, read: &ReadExpr) -> bool {
+        self.aggregate_key(read).is_some_and(|(key, roots)| roots.iter().all(|root|
+            self.binding(root).is_some_and(|binding| binding.fields.get(&key) == Some(&Ty::Bool))))
+    }
+
+    /// Resolve count provenance before rebinding `name`: a count domain may
+    /// itself refer to the old value of that local. Such a self-dependent
+    /// capture cannot be represented by the post-assignment binding and is
+    /// therefore deliberately not narrowed.
+    pub(super) fn count_capture(&self, name: &str, value: &A) -> Option<(Vec<String>, BTreeSet<String>)> {
+        let A::Call { name: call, args, .. } = value else { return None };
+        if call != "count" || args.len() != 1 || self.catalog.functions.contains_key(call)
+            || !self.aggregate_guard_safe(value) { return None; }
+        let read = args.first().and_then(|arg| self.aggregate_domain(arg))?;
+        let (aggregate, roots) = self.aggregate_key(&read)?;
+        if roots.contains(name) { return None; }
+        let capture = vec![COUNT_CAPTURE.into(), aggregate[1].clone()];
+        Some((capture, roots))
+    }
+
+    /// Attach pre-resolved capture-time provenance after assigning `name`.
+    /// `origins` remains diagnostic history; this field fact is the reaching
+    /// definition proof used by a later `require name > 0`.
+    pub(super) fn record_count_capture(&mut self, name: &str, capture: Option<(Vec<String>, BTreeSet<String>)>) {
+        let Some((capture, mut owners)) = capture else { return };
+        owners.insert(name.to_owned());
+        for owner in owners {
+            let Some(mut binding) = self.binding(&owner).cloned() else { return };
+            binding.fields.insert(capture.clone(), Ty::Bool);
+            self.scopes.last_mut().expect("a scope").insert(owner, binding);
+        }
+    }
+
+    fn count_key(&self, value: &A) -> Option<(Vec<String>, BTreeSet<String>)> {
+        let (count_arg, captured_name) = match value {
+            A::Call { name, args, .. } if name == "count" && args.len() == 1
+                && !self.catalog.functions.contains_key(name) => args.first(),
+            A::Name { name, .. } => self.binding(name)?.origins.iter().rev().find_map(|(_, origin)| match origin {
+                A::Call { name, args, .. } if name == "count" && args.len() == 1
+                    && !self.catalog.functions.contains_key(name) => args.first(),
+                _ => None,
+            }),
+            _ => None,
+        }.map(|arg| (arg, match value { A::Name { name, .. } => Some(name.as_str()), _ => None }))?;
+        let result = self.aggregate_key(&self.aggregate_domain(count_arg)?)?;
+        if let Some(name) = captured_name {
+            let capture = vec![COUNT_CAPTURE.into(), result.0[1].clone()];
+            if self.binding(name)?.fields.get(&capture) != Some(&Ty::Bool)
+                || !result.1.iter().all(|root| self.binding(root)
+                    .is_some_and(|binding| binding.fields.get(&capture) == Some(&Ty::Bool)))
+            {
+                return None;
+            }
+        }
+        Some(result)
+    }
+
+    fn aggregate_key(&self, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<(Vec<String>, BTreeSet<String>)> {
+        if read.range.is_some() || read.matching.is_some() { return None; }
+        let mut roots = BTreeSet::new();
+        let keys = read.keys.iter().map(|key| Some(format!("{:?}:{}", key.column,
+            self.aggregate_value(&key.value, &mut roots)?))).collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>()?;
+        let seeks = read.seeks.iter().map(|seek| Some(format!("{}:{}:{}", seek.column, seek.op,
+            self.aggregate_value(&seek.value, &mut roots)?))).collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>()?;
+        let filter = match read.filter.as_deref() {
+            Some(value) => Some(self.aggregate_value(value, &mut roots)?),
+            None => None,
+        };
+        if roots.is_empty() { roots.insert(pure_calls::CONSTANTS.into()); }
+        let identity = format!("{:?}:{:?}:{:?}:{keys:?}:{seeks:?}:{filter:?}", read.table, read.index, read.order_keys);
+        Some((vec![NONEMPTY.into(), identity], roots))
+    }
+
+    fn aggregate_value(&self, value: &A, roots: &mut BTreeSet<String>) -> Option<String> {
+        match value {
+            A::Focal { .. } => Some("focal".into()),
+            A::Member { base, name, .. } if matches!(base.as_ref(), A::Focal { .. }) => Some(format!("focal.{name}")),
+            A::Literal { .. } | A::Name { .. } | A::Member { .. } => self.stable_value(value, roots).map(|value| value.key()),
+            A::Unary { op, value, .. } => Some(format!("unary:{op}:{}", self.aggregate_value(value, roots)?)),
+            A::Binary { op, left, right, .. } => Some(format!("binary:{op}:{}:{}", self.aggregate_value(left, roots)?, self.aggregate_value(right, roots)?)),
+            A::Call { name, args, .. } => {
+                let function = self.catalog.functions.get(name)?;
+                if self.pure_functions.get(&function.name) != Some(&true) { return None; }
+                Some(format!("call:{name}:{:?}", args.iter().map(|arg| self.aggregate_value(arg, roots)).collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>()?))
+            }
+            A::Array { items, .. } => Some(format!("array:{:?}", items.iter().map(|item| self.aggregate_value(item, roots)).collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>()?)),
+            _ => None,
+        }
+    }
+}

@@ -125,6 +125,7 @@ impl Binder<'_> {

                     return Err(unknown(site, "delete does not yield a value; return a confirmation value after the write", "delete the row, then `return null`"));
                 }
+                let count_capture = self.count_capture(name, value);
                 // @ref LLP 3000.007#5.1 — rebinding preserves the local slot and resets its flow refinement.
                 if let Some(mut binding) = self.binding(name).cloned() {
                     let mut origins = binding.origins.clone();
@@ -147,6 +148,7 @@ impl Binder<'_> {
                     self.scopes.last_mut().unwrap().get_mut(name).unwrap().origins.push((ty, value.clone()));
                     out.push(Stmt::Let { name: runtime, value: expr });
                 }
+                self.record_count_capture(name, count_capture);
                 Ok(())
             }
             S::Require { condition, code, message, site } => {
@@ -462,6 +464,14 @@ impl Binder<'_> {
 
     /// After `require x != null` or inside `if x != null:`, `x` is present.
     pub(super) fn narrow(&mut self, condition: &A, then_branch: bool) {
+        // Aggregate facts may describe filtered domains, so they have a
+        // slightly wider write-free admission check than point facts. Record
+        // them once for the whole, already-evaluated condition: recursively
+        // recording a safe left operand would be stale when a later operand
+        // calls a writeful helper.
+        if self.aggregate_guard_safe(condition) {
+            self.record_aggregate_nonempty(condition, then_branch);
+        }
         self.narrow_inner(condition, then_branch, self.point_guard_safe(condition));
     }

commit b081f96054340034b6b771e239abeb4d7a3f94d7
Author: Charlie Cheever <ccheever@firewoodagent.com>
Date:   Thu Oct 15 00:10:38 2026 +0200

    Add the email text format contract

diff --git a/snapback4/crates/snapback4-core/src/engine/validate.rs b/snapback4/crates/snapback4-core/src/engine/validate.rs
index 69b5b1f66c..65bbe5e14a 100644
--- a/snapback4/crates/snapback4-core/src/engine/validate.rs
+++ b/snapback4/crates/snapback4-core/src/engine/validate.rs
@@ -139,6 +139,7 @@ pub fn validate_text(label: &str, text: &str, min: u32, format: Option<TextForma
         return Err(RuntimeError::Input(match format {
             TextFormat::Handle => format!("{label} must be a handle: lowercase letters, digits and underscores"),
             TextFormat::Url => format!("{label} must be an http or https URL"),
+            TextFormat::Email => format!("{label} must be an ASCII email address with one @, a dot-safe local part, and valid domain labels"),
         }));
     }
     Ok(())
diff --git a/snapback4/crates/snapback4-core/src/ir.rs b/snapback4/crates/snapback4-core/src/ir.rs
index 8869eab1ec..bccb38fb01 100644
--- a/snapback4/crates/snapback4-core/src/ir.rs
+++ b/snapback4/crates/snapback4-core/src/ir.rs
@@ -598,7 +598,8 @@ fn deserialize_text_format<'de, D: serde::Deserializer<'de>>(deserializer: D) ->
     match String::deserialize(deserializer)?.as_str() {
         "handle" => Ok(Some(crate::schema::TextFormat::Handle)),
         "url" => Ok(Some(crate::schema::TextFormat::Url)),
-        _ => Err(serde::de::Error::custom("text format must be handle or url")),
+        "email" => Ok(Some(crate::schema::TextFormat::Email)),
+        _ => Err(serde::de::Error::custom("text format must be handle, url, or email")),
     }
 }
 
diff --git a/snapback4/crates/snapback4-core/src/schema.rs b/snapback4/crates/snapback4-core/src/schema.rs
index f7db1da6d1..3a29a617dc 100644
--- a/snapback4/crates/snapback4-core/src/schema.rs
+++ b/snapback4/crates/snapback4-core/src/schema.rs
@@ -965,6 +965,7 @@ pub enum Constraint {
 pub enum TextFormat {
     Handle,
     Url,
+    Email,
 }
 
 impl TextFormat {
@@ -975,6 +976,24 @@ impl TextFormat {
                 .strip_prefix("https://")
                 .or_else(|| text.strip_prefix("http://"))
                 .is_some_and(|rest| !rest.is_empty() && rest.chars().all(|c| !c.is_whitespace() && !c.is_control())),
+            // @ref LLP 3000.013.001#1-decision — one deterministic ASCII mailbox grammar is shared by arguments, writes and devices.
+            Self::Email => {
+                if text.len() > 254 { return false; }
+                let Some((local, domain)) = text.split_once('@') else { return false };
+                if domain.contains('@') || local.is_empty() || local.len() > 64
+                    || domain.is_empty() || domain.len() > 253
+                    || local.starts_with('.') || local.ends_with('.') || local.contains("..")
+                {
+                    return false;
+                }
+                let local_ok = local.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_alphanumeric()
+                    || matches!(b, b'.' | b'!' | b'#' | b'$' | b'%' | b'&' | b'\'' | b'*'
+                        | b'+' | b'/' | b'=' | b'?' | b'^' | b'_' | b'`' | b'{' | b'|'
+                        | b'}' | b'~' | b'-'));
+                local_ok && domain.split('.').all(|label| !label.is_empty() && label.len() <= 63
+                    && !label.starts_with('-') && !label.ends_with('-')
+                    && label.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || b == b'-'))
+            }
         }
     }
 }

   143	    fn aggregate_key(&self, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<(Vec<String>, BTreeSet<String>)> {

   145	        let mut roots = BTreeSet::new();

   147	            self.aggregate_value(&key.value, &mut roots)?))).collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>()?;
   148	        let seeks = read.seeks.iter().map(|seek| Some(format!("{}:{}:{}", seek.column, seek.op,
   149	            self.aggregate_value(&seek.value, &mut roots)?))).collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>()?;

   151	            Some(value) => Some(self.aggregate_value(value, &mut roots)?),
   152	            None => None,

   183	/// Source-level builtins whose result is a deterministic function of stable
   184	/// arguments. State-reading operations and allocation never enter this list;
   185	/// malformed calls still receive their ordinary binding error.
   186	fn stable_builtin(name: &str, arity: usize) -> bool {
   187	    match name {
   188	        "count" | "first" | "last" | "lower" | "trim" | "sha256" | "tokens"
   189	        | "month" | "year" | "monthStart" | "yearStart" => arity == 1,

   191	        "slice" | "share" => arity == 3,
   192	        "sum" => matches!(arity, 1 | 2),
   193	        "day" | "hour" => matches!(arity, 1 | 2),
   194	        "bucket" => matches!(arity, 2 | 3),
   195	        "divide" => matches!(arity, 3 | 4),
   196	        "sort" => arity >= 2,
   197	        _ => false,
   198	    }
   199	}

     1	//! Presence facts for identical point reads across proved stable transaction states.
     2	use super::*;
     3	use std::collections::BTreeSet;
     4	const POINT: &str = "$point";
     5	
     6	impl Binder<'_> {
     7	    // A compound condition cannot prove an earlier read after a later effect.
     8	    // Unknown/table-reading helpers are conservative here; direct guards need none.
     9	    pub(super) fn point_guard_safe(&self, value: &A) -> bool {
    10	        match value {
    11	            A::Name { .. } | A::Focal { .. } | A::Literal { .. } => true,
    12	            A::Member { base, .. } | A::Unary { value: base, .. } | A::Assert { value: base, .. } => self.point_guard_safe(base),
    13	            A::Binary { left, right, .. } => self.point_guard_safe(left) && self.point_guard_safe(right),
    14	            A::Call { name, args, .. } => self.catalog.functions.get(name).is_none_or(|f| self.pure_functions.get(&f.name) == Some(&true))
    15	                && args.iter().all(|a| self.point_guard_safe(a)),
    16	            A::Read(read) | A::Exists { read, .. } => read.keys.iter().all(|k| self.point_guard_safe(&k.value)) && read.filter.is_none() && read.matching.is_none() && read.range.is_none(),
    17	            _ => false,
    18	        }
    19	    }
    20	    fn point_key(&self, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<(Vec<String>, BTreeSet<String>)> {
    21	        if read.range.is_some() || read.matching.is_some() || read.filter.is_some() || !read.seeks.is_empty() { return None; }
    22	        let info = self.catalog.tables.get(&read.table)?;
    23	        if info.maintained.is_some() || info.decl.ephemeral.is_some() { return None; }
    24	        let mut binder = self.clone();
    25	        let mut setup = setup::Body::default();
    26	        let (bound, index, _) = binder.select_index(info, read, ReadMode::FilteredPoint, &mut setup).ok()?;
    27	        // Exists over a nonunique key does not prove a point lookup.
    28	        if !index.unique || bound.len() != index.columns.len() { return None; }

    30	        let mut key = vec![POINT.into(), read.table.clone(), index.name];

    32	            key.push(format!("{:?}:{}", part.column, self.stable_value(&part.value, &mut roots)?.key()));

    60	            index: None, range: None, matching: None, site: site.clone(),

   118	                            !fields.iter().any(|field| index.columns().contains(&field.name)
   119	                                || key.iter().skip(3).any(|part| part.starts_with(&format!("Some({:?}):", field.name))))),
   120	                    Some(maintain_types::AUTHORIZATION) => false,
   121	                    Some(aggregate_facts::NONEMPTY) => false,
   122	                    Some(aggregate_facts::COUNT_CAPTURE) => false,
   123	                    _ => true,
   124	                });
   125	            }
   126	        }
   127	    }
   128	
   129	    pub(super) fn ordinary_write_lifetime(&self, table: &str) -> bool {
   130	        self.schema.table(table).is_some_and(|table| table.expire.is_none()
   131	            && table.not_before.is_none() && table.through.is_none() && table.ephemeral.is_none())
   132	    }
   133	
   134	    pub(super) fn row_independent_read_proved(&self, table: &str) -> bool {
   135	        let Some(info) = self.catalog.tables.get(table) else { return false };
   136	        if self.schema.tables[table].rules.read.is_uniformly_readable() { return true; }
   137	        let Some(rule) = info.decl.rules.iter().find(|rule| rule.effect == ast::Effect::Read) else { return false };
   138	        fn proved(binder: &Binder<'_>, table: &str, value: &A) -> bool {
   139	            if let A::Binary { op, left, right, .. } = value && op == "or" {
   140	                return proved(binder, table, left) || proved(binder, table, right);
   141	            }
   142	            let mut value = value.clone();
   143	            maintain_types::normalize_rule_constants(&mut value);
   144	            let mut row_dependent = false;
   145	            super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut value, &mut |expr| {
   146	                if matches!(expr, A::Focal { .. }) { row_dependent = true; }
   147	                if let A::Call { name, .. } = expr && binder.catalog.functions.contains_key(name) {
   148	                    row_dependent = true;
   149	                }
   150	                if let A::Read(read) | A::Exists { read, .. } | A::Created { read, .. } = expr {
   151	                    row_dependent |= read.table == table || binder.catalog.tables.get(&read.table)
   152	                        .is_none_or(|info| info.maintained.is_some())
   153	                        || binder.schema.table(&read.table).is_none_or(|target| !target.maintained.is_empty() || target.through.is_some());
   154	                }
   155	            });
   156	            !row_dependent && binder.point_guard_safe(&value) && binder.authorization_proved(&value, true)
   157	        }
   158	        proved(self, table, &rule.value)
   159	    }
   160	
   161	    pub(super) fn parent_rewrite(&self, value: &A) -> Option<String> {
   162	        let A::Member { base, name, .. } = value else { return None; };
   163	        let A::Read(read) = base.as_ref() else { return None; };
   164	        let info = self.catalog.tables.get(&read.table)?;
   165	        let field = Ty::from_scalar(info.columns.get(name)?);
   166	        if self.point_present(read) || field.is_optional() || info.maintained.is_some()
   167	            || info.decl.columns.iter().any(|c| c.name == *name && c.written_by.is_some() && !c.readable_by_audience) { return None; }
   168	        Some(format!("require exists {} else NOT_FOUND — a membership or required reference does not prove this row exists; alternatively bind {} ! else NOT_FOUND", print::read_expr(read), print::read_expr(read)))
   169	    }
   170	}
   171	
   172	pub(super) fn writes(body: &[Stmt]) -> bool {
   173	    ir::effects::Effects::writes_in(body)
   174	}
   175	
   176	fn rule_probes(rule: &snapback4_core::schema::RuleExpr, target: &str) -> bool {
   177	    use snapback4_core::schema::RuleExpr;
   178	    match rule {
   179	        RuleExpr::Exists { table, .. } | RuleExpr::Created { table, .. } => table == target,
   180	        RuleExpr::And(items) | RuleExpr::Or(items) => items.iter().any(|rule| rule_probes(rule, target)),
   181	        RuleExpr::Not(rule) => rule_probes(rule, target),
   182	        _ => false,
   183	    }
   184	}
   185	
   186	fn rule_dependencies_stable(schema: &snapback4_core::schema::Schema, rule: &snapback4_core::schema::RuleExpr) -> bool {
   187	    use snapback4_core::schema::RuleExpr;
   188	    match rule {
   189	        RuleExpr::Exists { table, .. } | RuleExpr::Created { table, .. } => schema.table(table)
   190	            .is_some_and(|target| target.maintained.is_empty() && target.through.is_none() && schema.maintain_for(table).is_none()),
   191	        RuleExpr::And(items) | RuleExpr::Or(items) => items.iter().all(|rule| rule_dependencies_stable(schema, rule)),
   192	        RuleExpr::Not(rule) => rule_dependencies_stable(schema, rule),
   193	        _ => true,
   194	    }
   195	}

     5	impl Binder<'_> {
     6	    // ----- statements -----
     7	
     8	    pub(super) fn block(&mut self, statements: &[S], out: &mut setup::Body, completes: bool) -> Fallible<()> {
     9	        let before = self.visible_bindings();
    10	        self.push_scope();
    11	        let count = statements.len();
    12	        let mut result = Ok(());
    13	        for (index, statement) in statements.iter().enumerate() {
    14	            if let Err(error) = self.statement(statement, out, completes && index + 1 == count) {
    15	                if result.is_ok() { result = Err(error); }
    16	            }
    17	        }
    18	        let after = self.visible_bindings();
    19	        self.pop_scope();
    20	        for (name, mut old) in before {
    21	            let new = &after[&name];
    22	            if old.runtime == new.runtime && old.revision != new.revision {
    23	                self.scopes.last_mut().expect("a scope").insert(name, new.clone());
    24	            } else if old.runtime == new.runtime {
    25	                // Facts added inside a lexical block remain local, while a
    26	                // write's invalidation must reach the enclosing loop header.
    27	                let surviving_fields: BTreeMap<_, _> = old.fields.iter()
    28	                    .filter(|(path, ty)| new.fields.get(*path) == Some(*ty))
    29	                    .map(|(path, ty)| (path.clone(), ty.clone()))
    30	                    .collect();
    31	                if surviving_fields.len() != old.fields.len() {
    32	                    old.fields = surviving_fields;
    33	                    self.scopes.last_mut().expect("a scope").insert(name, old);
    34	                }
    35	            }
    36	        }
    37	        result
    38	    }

    40	    pub(super) fn append_errors(&mut self, diagnostics: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>) {
    41	        for error in self.errors.drain(..) {
    42	            if !diagnostics.contains(&error) { diagnostics.push(error); }
    43	        }
    44	    }
    45	
    46	    pub(super) fn record_error(&mut self, error: Diagnostic) {
    47	        if !self.errors.contains(&error) { self.errors.push(error); }
    48	    }
    49	
    50	    pub(super) fn statement(&mut self, statement: &S, out: &mut setup::Body, completes: bool) -> Fallible<()> {
    51	        let result = self.statement_inner(statement, out, completes);
    52	        if let Err(error) = &result {
    53	            self.record_error(error.clone());
    54	            if let S::Bind { names, .. } = statement {
    55	                for name in names {
    56	                    if self.binding(name).is_some_and(|binding| binding.parameter) { continue; }
    57	                    let runtime = self.declare(name, Ty::Unknown);
    58	                    let binding = self.scopes.last_mut().unwrap().get_mut(name).unwrap();
    59	                    binding.error = Some(error.clone());
    60	                    out.push(Stmt::Let { name: runtime, value: Expr::Literal(Value::Null) });
    61	                }
    62	            }
    63	        }
    64	        result
    65	    }

   301	                    return Ok(());
   302	                }
   303	                if completes && self.kind != ProgramKind::Job {
   304	                    let prior = std::mem::replace(&mut self.returned_data, self.depth == 0 && self.kind == ProgramKind::Query);
   305	                    let result = self.value_of(value, out);
   306	                    self.returned_data = prior;
   307	                    let (expr, ty) = result?;
   308	                    if self.depth == 0 && self.kind == ProgramKind::Mutation && matches!(value, A::Write { .. }) {
   309	                        self.implicit_write_results.push(expr.clone());
   310	                    }
   311	                    self.returns.push((ty, value.clone()));
   312	                    out.push(Stmt::Return(expr));
   313	                    return Ok(());
   314	                }
   315	                if let A::Write { .. } = value {
   316	                    self.write(value, out, false)?;
   317	                    return Ok(());
   318	                }
   319	                Err(unknown(site, "a non-final expression must be a write; bind it if its value is needed", format!("value = {}", print::expression(value))))
   320	            }
   321	        }
   322	    }
   323	
   324	    pub(super) fn tuple_bind(&mut self, names: &[String], value: &A, site: &Site, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<()> {
   325	        let A::Call { name, args, .. } = value else {
   326	            return Err(unknown(site, "tuple bindings take exactly two names and sorted(a, b) or divmod(a, b)", "(quotient, remainder) = divmod(dividend, divisor)"));
   327	        };
   328	        if args.len() != 2 || (name != "divmod" && name != "sorted") {
   329	            return Err(unknown(site, "tuple bindings take exactly two names and sorted(a, b) or divmod(a, b)", format!("({}, {}) = divmod(a, b)", names[0], names[1])));
   330	        }
   331	        for (position, local) in names.iter().enumerate() {
   332	            if names[..position].contains(local) || self.scopes.last().is_some_and(|scope| scope.contains_key(local)) || self.scopes[0].contains_key(local) {
   333	                return Err(unknown(site, format!("duplicate local {local}"), "give each tuple component a fresh name"));
   334	            }
   335	        }
   336	        let (a, at) = self.expr(&args[0], out)?;
   337	        let ta = self.temp("a");
   338	        out.push(Stmt::Let { name: ta.clone(), value: a });
   339	        let (b, bt) = self.expr(&args[1], out)?;
   340	        if name == "divmod" {
   341	            if !at.is_numeric() || !bt.is_numeric() {
   342	                return Err(type_error(site, format!("divmod takes two present numbers, got {} and {}", at.name(), bt.name()), "narrow both operands first"));
   343	            }
   344	            let tmp = self.temp("divmod");
   345	            out.push(Stmt::Let { name: tmp.clone(), value: Expr::Builtin { builtin: Builtin::Divmod { remainder_sink: "lexicographic-id".into() }, args: vec![ir::var(&ta), b] } });
   346	            let first = self.declare(&names[0], Ty::Int);
   347	            let second = self.declare(&names[1], Ty::Int);
   348	            self.maintenance_local(&first, site, &names[0]);
   349	            self.maintenance_local(&second, site, &names[1]);
   350	            out.push(Stmt::Let { name: first, value: ir::field(ir::var(&tmp), "quotient") });
   351	            out.push(Stmt::Let { name: second, value: ir::field(ir::var(&tmp), "remainder") });
   352	        } else {
   353	            let joined = self.join(&at, &bt, &args[0], &args[1])?;
   354	            if !at.comparable(&bt) || at.is_optional() || bt.is_optional() {
   355	                return Err(type_error(site, format!("sorted takes two present values of one type, got {} and {}", at.name(), bt.name()), "narrow both operands first"));
   356	            }
   357	            let tb = self.temp("b");
   358	            out.push(Stmt::Let { name: tb.clone(), value: b });
   359	            let first = self.declare(&names[0], joined.clone());
   360	            let second = self.declare(&names[1], joined);
   361	            self.maintenance_local(&first, site, &names[0]);
   362	            self.maintenance_local(&second, site, &names[1]);
   363	            out.push(Stmt::Let { name: first, value: Expr::Builtin { builtin: Builtin::Min, args: vec![ir::var(&ta), ir::var(&tb)] } });
   364	            out.push(Stmt::Let { name: second, value: Expr::Builtin { builtin: Builtin::Max, args: vec![ir::var(&ta), ir::var(&tb)] } });
   365	        }
   366	        Ok(())
   367	    }
   368	
   369	    /// Join loop-carried writes with the zero-iteration path before accepting
   370	    /// the body. A refinement from the first iteration cannot bless later ones.
   371	    fn loop_body(&mut self, name: &str, item: Ty, body: &[S], site: &Site, fresh_rows: bool) -> Fallible<(String, setup::Body)> {
   372	        let mut header = self.visible_bindings();
   373	        let saved = self.scopes.clone();
   374	        let (temps, sites, returns) = (self.temps, self.sites, self.returns.len());
   375	        let mut growing_list = None;
   376	        // An acyclic dependency among carried slots stabilizes in at most
   377	        // one pass per slot plus the first literal refinement. A cycle that
   378	        // keeps nesting a value inside itself has no finite Quarry type.
   379	        for _ in 0..header.len() + 2 {
   380	            growing_list = None;
   381	            self.scopes = saved.clone();
   382	            self.scopes.last_mut().expect("a scope").extend(header.clone());
   383	            self.temps = temps;
   384	            self.sites = sites;
   385	            self.returns.truncate(returns);
   386	            self.push_scope();
   387	            let binding = self.declare(name, item.clone());
   388	            if fresh_rows { self.narrow_fresh_row(name, &item, site); }
   389	            self.maintenance_local(&binding, site, name);
   390	            self.loops += 1;
   391	            self.loop_transfers.push(LoopTransfers::default());
   392	            let mut out = setup::Body::default();
   393	            let result = self.block(body, &mut out, false);
   394	            let transfers = self.loop_transfers.pop().expect("loop transfer frame");
   395	            self.loops -= 1;
   396	            let after = self.binding_states();
   397	            self.pop_scope();
   398	            result?;
   399	            let flow = flow::Outcomes::block(&out);
   400	            let carried = transfers.continues.iter().chain(flow.falls_through().then_some(&after));
   401	            let mut changed = false;
   402	            for (local, old) in &mut header {
   403	                for bindings in carried.clone() {
   404	                    let new = bindings
   405	                        .get(&old.runtime)
   406	                        .expect("a loop edge retains every outer binding identity");
   407	                    let (candidate_changed, candidate_grows) = self.join_loop_binding(local, old, new, site)?;
   408	                    changed |= candidate_changed;
   409	                    if candidate_grows { growing_list.get_or_insert_with(|| local.clone()); }
   410	                }
   411	            }
   412	            if !changed {
   413	                let mut exit = header;
   414	                for bindings in &transfers.breaks {
   415	                    for (local, binding) in &mut exit {
   416	                        let edge = bindings
   417	                            .get(&binding.runtime)
   418	                            .expect("a break edge retains every outer binding identity");
   419	                        self.join_loop_binding(local, binding, edge, site)?;
   420	                    }
   421	                }
   422	                self.scopes.last_mut().expect("a scope").extend(exit);
   423	                return Ok((binding, out));
   424	            }
   425	        }
   426	        self.scopes = saved;
   427	        // @ref LLP 3000.007#5.1 — lengths widen to the heap bound, but recursively
   428	        // nesting a carried list has no finite item type. Offer the fixed-item form.
   429	        if let Some(local) = growing_list {
   430	            return Err(recursive_list_error(site, &local));
   431	        }
   432	        Err(type_error(site, "this loop keeps growing a recursive value type", "accumulate items of a fixed type rather than nesting an accumulator inside itself"))
   433	    }
   434	
   435	    /// Meet one loop edge with the zero-iteration path. A field proof survives
   436	    /// only when both paths retain the same fact; assignments additionally
   437	    /// widen the declared type and keep their revision for job carry checks.
   438	    fn join_loop_binding(&self, local: &str, old: &mut Binding, new: &Binding, site: &Site) -> Fallible<(bool, bool)> {
   439	        debug_assert_eq!(new.runtime, old.runtime);
   440	        let surviving_fields: BTreeMap<_, _> = old.fields.iter()
   441	            .filter(|(path, ty)| new.fields.get(*path) == Some(*ty))
   442	            .map(|(path, ty)| (path.clone(), ty.clone()))
   443	            .collect();
   444	        let fields_changed = surviving_fields.len() != old.fields.len();
   445	        if new.revision == old.revision {
   446	            old.fields = surviving_fields;
   447	            return Ok((fields_changed, false));
   448	        }
   449	        let declared = self.join(&old.declared, &new.declared, &joins::local(local, site), &joins::local(local, site))?;
   450	        let ty = self.join(&old.ty, &declared, &joins::local(local, site), &joins::local(local, site))?;
   451	        let type_changed = old.ty != ty || old.declared != declared;
   452	        let grows = type_changed && matches!(ty, Ty::List(..))
   453	            && new.origins.iter().any(|(_, source)| has_concat(source));
   454	        let changed = type_changed || !old.fields.is_empty();
   455	        old.fields.clear();
   456	        old.ty = ty;
   457	        old.declared = declared;
   458	        for origin in &new.origins {
   459	            if !old.origins.iter().any(|(_, source)| *source == origin.1) { old.origins.push(origin.clone()); }
   460	        }
   461	        old.revision = old.revision.max(new.revision);
   462	        Ok((changed, grows))
   463	    }
   464	
   465	    /// After `require x != null` or inside `if x != null:`, `x` is present.
   466	    pub(super) fn narrow(&mut self, condition: &A, then_branch: bool) {
   467	        // Aggregate facts may describe filtered domains, so they have a
   468	        // slightly wider write-free admission check than point facts. Record
   469	        // them once for the whole, already-evaluated condition: recursively
   470	        // recording a safe left operand would be stale when a later operand
   471	        // calls a writeful helper.
   472	        if self.aggregate_guard_safe(condition) {
   473	            self.record_aggregate_nonempty(condition, then_branch);
   474	        }
   475	        self.narrow_inner(condition, then_branch, self.point_guard_safe(condition));
   476	    }
   477	
   478	    fn narrow_inner(&mut self, condition: &A, then_branch: bool, points: bool) {
   479	        // The condition is evaluated before narrowing. If any operand may
   480	        // write, a fact observed by an earlier operand can already be stale.
   481	        if points {
   482	            self.record_maintain_authorization(condition, then_branch);
   483	        }
   484	        match condition {
   485	            A::Exists { read, .. } if then_branch && points => self.narrow_point(read),
   486	            A::Call { name, args, site } if name == super::super::intent_variants::PRESENT => self.narrow_inner(&A::Binary {
   487	                op: "!=".into(), left: Box::new(args[0].clone()), right: Box::new(A::Literal { value: Literal::Null, site: site.clone() }), site: site.clone(),
   488	            }, then_branch, points),
   489	            A::Unary { op, value, .. } if op == "not" => self.narrow_inner(value, !then_branch, points),
   490	            A::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if (op == "and" && then_branch) || (op == "or" && !then_branch) => {
   491	                self.narrow_inner(left, then_branch, points);
   492	                self.narrow_inner(right, then_branch, points);
   493	            }
   494	            A::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "!=" || op == "=" => {
   495	                let candidate = match (left.as_ref(), right.as_ref()) {
   496	                    (A::Literal { value: Literal::Null, .. }, other) | (other, A::Literal { value: Literal::Null, .. }) => other,
   497	                    _ => return,
   498	                };
   499	                if let A::Read(read) = candidate {
   500	                    if points && (op == "!=") == then_branch { self.narrow_point(read); }
   501	                } else if let A::Name { name, .. } = candidate
   502	                    && let Some(mut binding) = self.binding(name).cloned()
   503	                {
   504	                    binding.ty = if (op == "!=") == then_branch { binding.ty.required() } else { Ty::Null };
   505	                    self.scopes.last_mut().expect("a scope").insert(name.clone(), binding);
   506	                } else if let Some((name, path)) = field_path(candidate, self.focal.as_ref().map(|(name, _)| name.as_str()))
   507	                    && !path.is_empty()
   508	                    && let Some(mut binding) = self.binding(&name).cloned()
   509	                    && let Ok((_, ty)) = self.expr(candidate, &mut setup::Body::default())
   510	                {
   511	                    // @ref LLP 3000.007#10 — null guards refine fields of lexical snapshots, through short circuits and surviving branches.
   512	                    let present = (op == "!=") == then_branch;
   513	                    if present { binding.ty = binding.ty.required(); }
   514	                    binding.fields.insert(path, if present { ty.required() } else { Ty::Null });
   515	                    self.scopes.last_mut().expect("a scope").insert(name.to_owned(), binding);
   516	                } else {
   517	                    self.narrow_call(candidate, (op == "!=") == then_branch);
   518	                }
   519	            }
   520	            _ => {}
   521	        }
   522	    }
   523	
   524	    pub(super) fn field_refinement(&self, expr: &A) -> Option<Ty> {
   525	        let (name, path) = field_path(expr, self.focal.as_ref().map(|(name, _)| name.as_str()))?;
   526	        self.binding(&name)?.fields.get(&path).cloned()
   527	    }
   528	
   529	    pub(super) fn loop_domain(&mut self, domain: &A, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(LoopDomain, Ty)> {
   530	        let global = match domain {
   531	            A::Name { name, site } if self.binding(name).is_none() => Some((name, site)),
   532	            A::Read(read) => Some((&read.table, &read.site)),
   533	            _ => None,
   534	        }.filter(|(name, _)| self.catalog.tables.get(*name).and_then(|table| table.maintained.as_ref())
   535	            .is_some_and(|maintain| maintain.by.is_empty()));
   536	        if let Some((name, site)) = global {
   537	            return Err(type_error(site, format!("{name} is one global maintained scalar, not a loop domain"),
   538	                format!("use `{name}` as a scalar value rather than iterating it")).guide("maintains"));
   539	        }
   540	        if let A::Read(read) = domain && let Some(shaped) = self.page_shape(read) { return self.loop_domain(&shaped, out); }
   541	        if let A::Name { name, site } = domain && self.binding(name).is_none() && self.catalog.tables.contains_key(name) {
   542	            let read = ReadExpr { cursor_prefix: None, order_keys: Vec::new(), computed: Default::default(), table: name.clone(), keys: Vec::new(), index: Some("byId".into()), range: None, matching: None, filter: None, seeks: Vec::new(), site: site.clone() };
   543	            let (scan, _) = self.scan_at(&read, out, prefix::Position::Domain)?;
   544	            return Ok((LoopDomain::Page(scan), Ty::Row(name.clone())));
   545	        }
   546	        match domain {
   547	            A::Call { name, args, site } if name == "all" && self.viewer_depth.is_some() => {
   548	                let (value, ty) = self.job_query_call("$jobAll", args, site, out).ok_or_else(|| job_reads::diagnostic(site, "E_JOB_VIEWER_QUERY", "all requires a declared query", "for row in all(queryName(..., null)):"))??;
   549	                let (item, max) = ty.sequence().expect("all returns a list");
   550	                Ok((LoopDomain::DerivedArray { value, max, bound_source: "viewer query pages".into() }, item))
   551	            }
   552	            A::Name { name, .. } if self.binding(name).is_none() && self.catalog.view_reads.contains_key(name) => {
   553	                let mut merge = self.catalog.view_reads[name].clone(); merge.site = domain.site().clone();
   554	                self.loop_domain(&A::Merge(merge), out)
   555	            }
   556	            A::Merge(merge) if merge.index.is_empty() => {
   557	                let (value, ty) = self.planned_view(merge, out, prefix::Position::Domain)?;
   558	                let item = match ty { Ty::Page(table, _) => Ty::Row(table), Ty::Collection(item, _) => *item, _ => unreachable!("planned collection") };
   559	                Ok((LoopDomain::DerivedArray { value, max: merge.take, bound_source: "planned domain".into() }, item))
   560	            }
   561	            A::Read(read) if self.is_page(read, ReadMode::FilteredPoint)? => {
   562	                let (scan, _) = self.scan_at(read, out, prefix::Position::Domain)?;
   563	                Ok((LoopDomain::Page(scan), Ty::Row(read.table.clone())))
   564	            }
   565	            A::Walk { table, seed, column, depth, site } => {
   566	                let (recurse, ty) = self.walk(table, seed, column, *depth, site, out)?;
   567	                Ok((LoopDomain::Recurse(recurse), ty))
   568	            }
   569	            A::Call { name, args, site } if name == "range" || name == "$builtin:range" => {
   570	                if args.len() != 2 { return Err(type_error(site, "range takes two int expressions", "for i in range(0, n):")); }
   571	                let (start, start_ty) = self.expr(&args[0], out)?;
   572	                let mut setup = setup::Body::default();
   573	                let (end, end_ty) = self.expr(&args[1], &mut setup)?;
   574	                let start = if setup.is_empty() { start } else { self.snapshot(start, out) };
   575	                out.extend(setup);
   576	                if start_ty != Ty::Int || end_ty != Ty::Int { return Err(type_error(site, "range takes two present ints", "for i in range(0, n):")); }
   577	                let domain = match (literal_int(&args[0]), literal_int(&args[1])) {
   578	                    (Some(start), Some(end_exclusive)) => LoopDomain::LiteralRange { start, end_exclusive },
   579	                    _ => LoopDomain::ExpressionRange { start, end_exclusive: end },
   580	                };
   581	                Ok((domain, Ty::Int))
   582	            }
   583	            A::Name { name, site } if self.scopes[0].contains_key(name) && self.binding(name).is_some_and(|binding| binding.runtime == *name) => {
   584	                let ty = self.lookup(name).expect("bound argument").clone();
   585	                match ty {
   586	                    Ty::List(item, max) => Ok((LoopDomain::ArgList { name: name.clone(), max }, *item)),
   587	                    Ty::Optional(inner) if matches!(*inner, Ty::List(..)) => Err(type_error(site, "for cannot iterate an optional list", format!("for x in {name} ?? []:"))),
   588	                    other => Err(type_error(site, format!("for cannot iterate {}", other.name()), "iterate a bounded page or a bounded list argument")),
   589	                }
   590	            }
   591	            other => {
   592	                let (expr, ty) = self.expr(other, out)?;
   593	                match ty {
   594	                    Ty::List(item, max) => Ok((LoopDomain::DerivedArray { value: expr, max, bound_source: "list".into() }, *item)),
   595	                    Ty::Page(table, max) => Ok((LoopDomain::DerivedArray { value: expr, max, bound_source: "page".into() }, Ty::Row(table))),
   596	                    Ty::Collection(item, max) => Ok((LoopDomain::DerivedArray { value: expr, max, bound_source: "shape".into() }, *item)),
   597	                    Ty::Optional(inner) if inner.loop_max().is_some() => Err(type_error(other.site(), "for cannot iterate an optional list", format!("for x in {} ?? []:", print::expression(other)))),
   598	                    Ty::Unknown => Err(unbound_loop(other.site(), "for cannot prove a static bound for this value", "iterate a bounded page, a bounded list argument, or tokens(text)")),
   599	                    other_ty => Err(type_error(other.site(), format!("for cannot iterate {}", other_ty.name()), "iterate a bounded page or bounded list")),
   600	                }
   601	            }
   602	        }
   603	    }
   604	
   605	    /// Arguments belong to the caller: bind and snapshot each one before
   606	    /// introducing any parameter. `new` and reads are evaluated once, in
   607	    /// source order, even when the helper uses the parameter repeatedly.
   608	    fn helper_arguments(&mut self, name: &str, declared: &[ast::ArgDecl], args: &[A], site: &Site, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<BTreeMap<String, Binding>> {
   609	        if self.depth >= MAX_EXPANSION_DEPTH {
   610	            return Err(unsupported(site, "helpers nested deeper than eight levels", "flatten the helper"));
   611	        }
   612	        if args.len() != declared.len() {
   613	            return Err(type_error(site, format!("{name} takes {} arguments; received {}", declared.len(), args.len()), format!("{name}({})", declared.iter().map(|a| a.name.clone()).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", "))));
   614	        }
   615	        let mut bindings = BTreeMap::new();
   616	        for (declared, given) in declared.iter().zip(args) {
   617	            if bindings.contains_key(&declared.name) {
   618	                return Err(unknown(&declared.site, format!("duplicate argument {}", declared.name), "rename one helper parameter"));
   619	            }
   620	            let expected = Ty::from_scalar(&declared.type_);
   621	            // Helpers are polymorphic over same-table row snapshots; retain their
   622	            // existing row parameter semantics. Only a literal needs id conversion.
   623	            let (expr, ty) = if expected.required().is_identity() && matches!(given, A::Literal { .. }) { self.expr_as_identity(given, out, &expected)? } else { self.expr(given, out)? };
   624	            // @ref LLP 3000.036.002#8 — call-site expansion preserves a same-table row; references keep their existing meaning.
   625	            let expected = match (expected.required(), ty.required()) {
   626	                (Ty::Ref(table), Ty::Row(row)) if table == row && (!ty.is_optional() || expected.is_optional()) => {
   627	                    if expected.is_optional() { Ty::Row(row).optional() } else { Ty::Row(row) }
   628	                }
   629	                (Ty::Ref(table), Ty::WriteRow(row)) if table == row && (!ty.is_optional() || expected.is_optional()) => {
   630	                    if expected.is_optional() { Ty::WriteRow(row).optional() } else { Ty::WriteRow(row) }
   631	                }
   632	                _ => expected,
   633	            };
   634	            if !ty.assignable_to(&expected) {
   635	                return Err(type_error(given.site(), format!("{name}'s {} is {}; got {}", declared.name, expected.name(), ty.name()), match (ty.required(), expected.required()) {
   636	                    (Ty::Enum(variants), Ty::Text(max)) if variants.iter().any(|variant| variant.chars().count() > max as usize) => format!("change {name}'s parameter to `{}: {}`; not every variant fits text <={max}", declared.name, ty.name()),
   637	                    _ if ty.is_optional() && !expected.is_optional() => self.parent_rewrite(given).unwrap_or_else(|| format!("{} — require this argument once before calling {name}; absent or hidden rows refuse NOT_FOUND", print::expression(&A::Assert { value: Box::new(given.clone()), code: "NOT_FOUND".into(), message: None, site: given.site().clone() }))),
   638	                    _ => format!("pass {} for {}; a table parameter receives an id when called with a reference, or the same-table row snapshot when called with a row. To read fields from an id, bind `{table}[{}] ! else NOT_FOUND` in the helper; multiline fn bodies need `return value`", expected.name(), declared.name, declared.name, table = match expected.required() { Ty::Ref(t) => t, _ => "<table>".into() }),
   639	                }));
   640	            }
   641	            let runtime = self.temp(&declared.name);
   642	            self.maintenance_scopes.insert(runtime.clone(), format!("helper/{name}/parameter/{}", declared.name));
   643	            out.push(Stmt::Let { name: runtime.clone(), value: expr });
   644	            bindings.insert(declared.name.clone(), Binding { runtime, ty: expected.clone(), declared: expected, parameter: true, revision: 0, fields: BTreeMap::new(), error: None, origins: Vec::new() });
   645	        }
   646	        Ok(bindings)
   647	    }
   648	
   649	    pub(super) fn fn_call(&mut self, function: &ast::FunctionDecl, args: &[A], site: &Site, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {
   650	        let bindings = self.helper_arguments(&function.name, &function.args, args, site, out)?;
   651	        let start = out.len();
   652	        let caller = std::mem::replace(&mut self.scopes, vec![bindings]);
   653	        self.declare(pure_calls::CONSTANTS, Ty::Null);
   654	        let focal = self.focal.take();
   655	        let loops = std::mem::take(&mut self.loops);
   656	        // @ref LLP 3000.019#6 — remove the measured helper/inline placement discrepancy.
   657	        // @ref LLP 3000.007#5.2 — placement inherits caller loops; break/continue stay lexical.
   658	        self.caller_loops += loops;

   660	        self.depth += 1;
   661	        self.call_chain.push((function.name.clone(), site.clone()));

   663	        let result = match &function.body {
   664	            ast::FunctionBody::Expression(value) => self.value_of(value, out),

   666	                let mut body = setup::Body::default();
   667	                self.block(statements, &mut body, false).and_then(|()| {
   668	                    let ty = self.return_type()?;
   669	                    let result = self.temp("result");
   670	                    self.maintenance_scopes.insert(result.clone(), format!("helper/{}/result", function.name));
   671	                    let done = self.temp("returned");
   672	                    out.push(Stmt::Let { name: result.clone(), value: Expr::Literal(Value::Null) });
   673	                    out.push(Stmt::Let { name: done.clone(), value: Expr::Literal(Value::Bool(false)) });

   675	                    let code = helper_returns(body.code, &result, &done).0;

   682	            && let Ty::Row(table) | Ty::WriteRow(table) | Ty::Page(table, _) = ty.required()

   684	            self.receipt_returns.push((table, function.site.clone()));
   685	        }
   686	        if let Ok((value, _)) = &result {
   687	            self.pure_functions.insert(function.name.clone(), pure_calls::statements(&out[start..]) && pure_calls::expression(value));
   688	        }
   689	        self.depth -= 1;
   690	        self.call_chain.pop();
   691	        self.returns = returns;
   692	        self.loops = loops;
   693	        self.caller_loops -= loops;
   694	        self.scopes = caller;
   695	        if point_facts::writes(&out[start..]) { self.invalidate_points(); }

   705	    pub(super) fn visible_bindings(&self) -> BTreeMap<String, Binding> {
   706	        self.scopes.iter().flat_map(|scope| scope.iter().map(|(name, binding)| (name.clone(), binding.clone()))).collect()

   708	
   709	    /// Preserve hidden outer locals across loop edges. Runtime identities are
   710	    /// unique to declarations even when their source names are shadowed.
   711	    fn binding_states(&self) -> BTreeMap<String, Binding> {

   713	            .iter()
   714	            .flat_map(|scope| scope.values())
   715	            .map(|binding| (binding.runtime.clone(), binding.clone()))
   716	            .collect()
   717	    }

   719	    fn branch(&mut self, condition: &A, taken: bool, statements: &[S], completes: bool) -> Fallible<(setup::Body, BTreeMap<String, Binding>)> {
   720	        self.push_scope();
   721	        self.narrow(condition, taken);
   722	        let mut out = setup::Body::default();
   723	        let result = (|| {
   724	            let mut result = Ok(());
   725	            for (index, statement) in statements.iter().enumerate() {
   726	                if let Err(error) = self.statement(statement, &mut out, completes && index + 1 == statements.len()) {
   727	                    if result.is_ok() { result = Err(error); }
   728	                }
   729	            }
   730	            result.map(|()| (out, self.visible_bindings()))

   734	    }
   735	}
   736	
   737	/// Lower helper-local returns without extending the serialized IR. Guards
   738	/// skip the remaining statements; a returning loop breaks its own iteration.
   739	fn helper_returns(statements: Vec<Stmt>, result: &str, done: &str) -> (Vec<Stmt>, bool) {
   740	    let mut out = Vec::new();
   741	    let mut may_return = false;
   742	    for statement in statements {
   743	        let (statements, returns) = match statement {
   744	            Stmt::Return(value) => (vec![Stmt::Assign { name: result.into(), value }, Stmt::Assign { name: done.into(), value: Expr::Literal(Value::Bool(true)) }], true),
   745	            Stmt::If { condition, then_body, else_body } => {
   746	                let (then_body, a) = helper_returns(then_body, result, done);
   747	                let (else_body, b) = helper_returns(else_body, result, done);
   748	                (vec![Stmt::If { condition, then_body, else_body }], a || b)
   749	            }
   750	            Stmt::For { binding, domain, body } => {
   751	                let (mut body, returns) = helper_returns(body, result, done);
   752	                if returns {
   753	                    body.push(Stmt::If { condition: ir::var(done), then_body: vec![Stmt::Break], else_body: Vec::new() });
   754	                }
   755	                (vec![Stmt::For { binding, domain, body }], returns)
   756	            }
   757	            other => (vec![other], false),
   758	        };
   759	        if may_return {
   760	            out.push(Stmt::If { condition: Expr::Unary { op: UnaryOp::Not, value: Box::new(ir::var(done)) }, then_body: statements, else_body: Vec::new() });
   761	        } else {
   762	            out.extend(statements);
   763	        }
   764	        may_return |= returns;
   765	    }
   766	    (out, may_return)
   767	}
   768	
   769	pub(super) fn exits(statements: &[Stmt]) -> bool {
   770	    flow::Outcomes::block(statements).exits()
   771	}
   772	
   773	/// Only lexical snapshots have stable fields; repeated database reads do not.
   774	// @ref LLP 3000.019#3.2 — the aggregate's declared row is a lexical snapshot;
   775	// null guards inside its value expression refine `.field` for that row only.
   776	pub(super) fn field_path(expr: &A, focal: Option<&str>) -> Option<(String, Vec<String>)> {
   777	    match expr {
   778	        A::Name { name, .. } => Some((name.clone(), Vec::new())),
   779	        A::Focal { .. } => Some((focal?.to_owned(), Vec::new())),
   780	        A::Member { base, name, .. } => {
   781	            let (root, mut path) = field_path(base, focal)?;
   782	            path.push(name.clone());
   783	            Some((root, path))
   784	        }
   785	        _ => None,
   786	    }
   787	}
   788	
   789	/// A condition needs a `bool`, and the repair depends on what the author has.
   790	///
   791	/// For most optionals `x != null` is exactly right: the question is whether a value
   792	/// is present. For `bool ?` it is a trap — `x != null` is also true when the value is
   793	/// `false`, so an author who follows that advice gets a condition that compiles, is
   794	/// blessed by `check`, and silently inverts their intent for every explicitly-false
   795	/// row. The 0.2.28 calibration hit this class six times.
   796	///
   797	/// `shape` carries `{}` where the comparison belongs.
   798	pub(super) fn bool_condition_rewrite(ty: &crate::types::Ty, shape: &str) -> String {
   799	    if matches!(ty.required(), crate::types::Ty::Bool) {
   800	        format!("compare the value: `{}` — `x != null` is also true when x is false", shape.replace("{}", "= true"))
   801	    } else {
   802	        format!("compare the value: `{}`", shape.replace("{}", "!= null"))
   803	    }
   804	}
   805	
   806	fn has_concat(value: &A) -> bool {
   807	    let mut found = false;
   808	    super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut value.clone(), &mut |value| {
   809	        found |= matches!(value, A::Binary { op, .. } if op == "+");
   810	    });
   811	    found
   812	}
   813	
   814	fn recursive_concat(value: &A, name: &str) -> bool {
   815	    // Follow returned container values, not arbitrary references: count(xs)
   816	    // and even count([xs]) consume the accumulator without embedding it.
   817	    fn embeds(value: &A, name: &str, nested: bool) -> bool {
   818	        match value {
   819	            A::Name { name: source, .. } => nested && source == name,
   820	            A::Array { items, .. } => items.iter().any(|item| embeds(item, name, true)),
   821	            A::Object { fields, .. } => fields.iter().any(|field| field.value.as_ref()
   822	                .map_or(nested && field.name == name, |value| embeds(value, name, nested))),
   823	            A::If { then, otherwise, .. } => embeds(then, name, nested) || embeds(otherwise, name, nested),
   824	            A::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "+" || op == "??" =>
   825	                embeds(left, name, nested) || embeds(right, name, nested),
   826	            A::Assert { value, .. } => embeds(value, name, nested),
   827	            _ => false,
   828	        }
   829	    }
   830	    has_concat(value) && embeds(value, name, false)
   831	}
   832	
   833	fn recursive_list_error(site: &Site, local: &str) -> Diagnostic {
   834	    type_error(site, format!("this loop keeps growing {local}'s recursive list item type"),
   835	        format!("use `{local} = {local} + [x]` with x of a fixed item type; do not nest a carried accumulator inside itself"))
   836	}

   293	            let guarded = ir::scans(&program).into_iter().filter(|scan| scan.site == site).all(|scan| snapback4_core::engine::rules::grant_dominated(schema, &program, scan));

   295	        }

   300	        Some(BoundProgram { maintenance_scopes: binder.maintenance_scopes, statement_ends, page_sources: binder.page_sources, canonical_sources: binder.canonical_sources, constraint_domains: binder.constraint_domains, filters: binder.filters, view_types: binder.view_types, selections: binder.selections, sources: binder.sources, receipt_sources: binder.receipt_sources, receipt_returns: binder.receipt_returns, implicit_write_results: binder.implicit_write_results, timeline_scans: binder.timeline_scans, program, presence, module: module.to_owned(), args: arg_types, result, notes: binder.notes, site: decl.site.clone(), job_bound_helpers: binder.job_bound_helpers })

   303	    fn site(&mut self) -> String {

   342	    fn table(&self, name: &str, site: &Site) -> Fallible<&'a TableInfo> {
   343	        if self.viewer_depth.is_some() && self.schema.table(name).is_some_and(|table| table.ephemeral.is_some()) {
   344	            return Err(job_reads::diagnostic(site, "E_JOB_VIEWER_QUERY", "job viewer reads require durable data", "keep ephemeral reads in a client query; persist the state the job needs"));
   345	        }
   346	        self.catalog.tables.get(name).ok_or_else(|| unknown(site, format!("unknown table {name}"), name_rewrite(name, self.catalog.tables.keys().map(String::as_str), None)))
   347	    }

   349	    // @ref LLP 3000.013.001#3-global-maintained-view-representation — every
   350	    // page admission path, including planned-view lanes, rejects the physical
   351	    // row behind a global maintained scalar.
   352	    pub(super) fn reject_global_scalar_page(&self, read: &ReadExpr) -> Fallible<()> {
   353	        if self.catalog.tables.get(&read.table).and_then(|table| table.maintained.as_ref())
   354	            .is_some_and(|maintain| maintain.by.is_empty())
   355	            && (!read.keys.is_empty() || read.range.is_some() || read.filter.is_some()
   356	                || read.matching.is_some() || !read.seeks.is_empty() || !read.order_keys.is_empty())

   358	            return Err(type_error(&read.site, format!("{} is one global maintained scalar, not a group table", read.table),

   360	        }
   361	        Ok(())
   362	    }

   364	    // ----- reads -----
   365	
   366	    fn read(&mut self, read: &ReadExpr, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {
   367	        if read.matching.is_some() && self.binding(&read.table).is_some_and(|b| matches!(b.ty.required(), Ty::Text(_) | Ty::Enum(_))) {
   368	            return Err(type_error(&read.site, format!("`{}` is text; `matching` searches a table's word index", read.table),

   370	        }
   371	        self.reject_global_scalar_page(read)?;

   373	            let expanded = if let Some(plan) = self.catalog.feed_views.get(&read.table) {
   374	                super::feed_view::read(timeline, plan, read)?
   375	            } else { super::timeline::read(timeline, read)? };
   376	            // @ref LLP 3000.013#14.4 — the scale certificate trusts only the scan this expansion generates.
   377	            let outer = self.expanding_feed.replace((timeline.table("Feed"), read.site.clone()));
   378	            let bound = self.expr(&expanded, out);
   379	            self.expanding_feed = outer;
   380	            return bound;
   381	        }
   382	        if let Some(shaped) = self.page_shape(read) { return self.expr(&shaped, out); }
   383	        // @ref LLP 3000.036#11 — a bound row of this table addresses itself, as in `update t[row]`.
   384	        if let Some(by_id) = self.row_as_key(read) { return self.read(&by_id, out); }
   385	        if self.is_page(read, ReadMode::FilteredPoint)? {
   386	            let (scan, take) = self.scan(read, out)?;
   387	            return Ok((Expr::Scan(scan), Ty::Page(read.table.clone(), take)));
   388	        }
   389	        let info = self.table(&read.table, &read.site)?;
   390	        if let Some(maintain) = &info.maintained {
   391	            if read.keys.len() != maintain.by.len() || read.keys.iter().filter_map(|key| key.column.as_ref()).any(|column| !maintain.by.contains(column)) {
   392	                return Err(super::maintains::refusal(&read.site, format!("{} scalar lookup needs the complete group key: {}", read.table, maintain.by.join(", ")), format!("supply every group key, or page the view: {} first 20 by .id after c", read.table)));
   393	            }
   394	        }
   395	        let value = self.read_value(read, ReadMode::FilteredPoint, out)?;
   396	        if let Some(maintain) = &info.maintained {
   397	            // A maintained point read is the typed value: zero when absent.
   398	            let row = self.temp("countRow");
   399	            let tmp = self.temp("total");
   400	            let zero = Expr::Literal(Value::Int(0));
   401	            out.push(Stmt::Let { name: row.clone(), value });
   402	            out.push(Stmt::If {
   403	                condition: Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::Eq, left: Box::new(ir::var(&row)), right: Box::new(Expr::Literal(Value::Null)) },
   404	                then_body: vec![Stmt::Let { name: tmp.clone(), value: zero }],
   405	                else_body: vec![Stmt::Let { name: tmp.clone(), value: ir::field(ir::var(&row), "value") }],
   406	            });
   407	            let ty = info.columns.get("value").map(Ty::from_scalar).unwrap_or(Ty::Int);
   408	            let ty = self.maintained_result_type(maintain, read, ty);
   409	            return Ok((ir::var(&tmp), ty));
   410	        }
   411	        Ok((value, Ty::Row(read.table.clone()).optional()))
   412	    }

   414	    fn scan(&mut self, read: &ReadExpr, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Scan, u32)> {
   415	        self.scan_at(read, out, prefix::Position::Page)
   416	    }
   417	
   418	    fn scan_at(&mut self, read: &ReadExpr, out: &mut setup::Body, position: prefix::Position) -> Fallible<(Scan, u32)> {
   419	        self.reject_global_scalar_page(read)?;
   420	        let info = self.table(&read.table, &read.site)?;
   421	        let (keys, index, selection) = self.select_index(info, read, ReadMode::Range, out)?;
   422	        let prefix = self.order_keys(info, &index, keys)?;
   423	        // @ref LLP 3000.014#5.1 — one pinned parent authorizes all of its aggregate groups.
   424	        if let Some(m) = self.schema.maintain_for(&read.table).filter(|m| m.groups_by_parent(self.schema)) {
   425	            let parent = self.schema.tables[&m.source].through.as_ref().unwrap();
   426	            if m.parent_key.is_none() || !index.columns.iter().take(prefix.len()).any(|f| f == &parent.reference) {
   427	                let fields = m.by.iter().filter(|f| *f != &parent.reference).map(|f| format!(".{f}")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ");
   428	                let order = if fields.is_empty() { ".id" } else { &fields };
   429	                return Err(Diagnostic::new(&read.site, "E_READ_THROUGH_SCAN",
   430	                    format!("{} must pin {} to one readable {} row before enumerating groups", read.table, parent.reference, parent.parent),
   431	                    format!("inside a page of {}, read `{}[{} = .id] by {order}` in its shape: `{{ *, totals: {}[{} = .id] by {order} }}`; keep the parent page's audience key, order and cursor. For a scalar supply every group key: {}[{}]. Omit an explicit aggregate read rule to inherit parent authorization", parent.parent, read.table, parent.reference, read.table, parent.reference, read.table, m.by.join(", "))).guide("maintains"));
   432	            }
   433	        }
   434	        let (range, bound) = self.prefix_bound(read, &index, position)?;
   435	        if info.maintained.is_some() && let Some(position) = index.columns.iter().position(|field| field == "value") {
   436	            if prefix.len() != position {
   437	                return Err(super::maintains::refusal(&range.site, format!("{} by {} needs every ranking scope field pinned: {}", read.table, index.name, index.columns[..position].join(", ")),
   438	                    format!("pin {} with where equalities, then read {} last {} by .value after c; omit the scope predicate for a global ranking", index.columns[..position].join(", "), read.table, range.take)));
   439	            }
   440	        }
   441	        if range.take == 0 {
   442	            return Err(type_error(&range.site, "a page holds at least one row", print::read_expr(&ReadExpr { range: Some(ast::RangeSpec { take: 50, ..range.clone() }), ..read.clone() })));
   443	        }
   444	        let mut page_source = read.clone();
   445	        page_source.computed.extend(index.computed.iter().map(|(key, value)| {
   446	            let mut value = value.clone();
   447	            if let A::Call { args, .. } = &mut value
   448	                && let Some(A::Name { name, site }) = args.first().cloned() {
   449	                args[0] = A::Member { base: Box::new(A::Focal { site: site.clone() }), name, site };
   450	            }

    10	#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
    11	enum AuthorizationDomain {
    12	    /// Program `exists` observes only rows readable by the current viewer.
    13	    Program,
    14	    /// A table rule's witness tests raw row presence below the target rule.

    17	
    18	impl Binder<'_> {
    19	    /// A private maintained point is nullable: authorization may withhold a
    20	    /// stored value and the logical zero alike. Keep the numeric type required
    21	    /// only when the key or a surviving guard proves the view's read rule.
    22	    // @ref LLP 3000.013#14.7 — hidden maintained hits and misses are null;
    23	    // an admitted miss alone becomes the aggregate identity.
    24	    pub(super) fn maintained_result_type(
    25	        &self,
    26	        maintain: &ast::MaintainDecl,
    27	        read: &ReadExpr,
    28	        ty: Ty,
    29	    ) -> Ty {
    30	        if !self.maintained_parent_is_live(maintain, read) {
    31	            return ty.optional();
    32	        }
    33	        if (self.caller_kind == ProgramKind::Job && self.viewer_depth.is_none())
    34	            || self

    36	                .table(&read.table)
    37	                .is_some_and(|table| table.rules.read.is_uniformly_readable())

    40	        }
    41	        let Some(expected) = substituted_rule(maintain, read) else {
    42	            return ty.optional();
    43	        };
    44	        if self.authorization_proved(&expected, true) {
    45	            ty
    46	        } else {
    47	            ty.optional()
    48	        }
    49	    }
