    51	    /// A system job bypasses read policy, but an inherited maintained point
    52	    /// still disappears with its through parent. A dominating exact-point
    53	    /// witness or the live parent row supplying `.id` proves that lifetime.
    54	    fn maintained_parent_is_live(&self, maintain: &ast::MaintainDecl, read: &ReadExpr) -> bool {
    55	        let Some(parent) = self.schema.maintain_for(&read.table).and_then(|maintain| maintain.parent_key.as_ref()) else { return true };
    56	        let Some(key) = group_keys(maintain, read).remove(&parent.reference) else { return false };
    57	        let site = key.site().clone();
    58	        let mut parent_read = ReadExpr {
    59	            cursor_prefix: None, computed: Default::default(), order_keys: Vec::new(), table: parent.parent.clone(),
    60	            keys: vec![ast::KeyPart { column: None, value: key, site: site.clone() }], seeks: Vec::new(), filter: None,
    61	            index: None, range: None, matching: None, site,
    62	        };
    63	        if self.point_present(&parent_read) { return true; }
    64	        parent_read.keys[0].column = Some("id".into());
    65	        self.point_present(&parent_read)
    66	    }
    67	
    68	    /// Store a truth fact on every lexical root it depends on. Existing branch
    69	    /// intersection and rebinding logic then keeps exactly the facts that
    70	    /// survive control flow and clears facts whose operands change.
    71	    pub(super) fn record_maintain_authorization(&mut self, condition: &A, truth: bool) {
    72	        let Some((key, roots)) = self.authorization_key(condition, truth, AuthorizationDomain::Program) else {
    73	            return;
    74	        };
    75	        for root in roots {
    76	            let Some(mut binding) = self.binding(&root).cloned() else {
    77	                return;
    78	            };
    79	            binding.fields.insert(key.clone(), Ty::Bool);
    80	            self.scopes
    81	                .last_mut()
    82	                .expect("a scope")
    83	                .insert(root, binding);
    84	        }
    85	    }
    86	
    87	    fn authorization_holds(&self, condition: &A, truth: bool) -> bool {
    88	        self.authorization_key(condition, truth, AuthorizationDomain::Rule)
    89	            .is_some_and(|(key, roots)| {
    90	                roots.iter().all(|root| {
    91	                    self.binding(root)
    92	                        .is_some_and(|binding| binding.fields.get(&key) == Some(&Ty::Bool))
    93	                })
    94	            })
    95	    }
    96	
    97	    /// Facts may prove a read rule without restating the whole expression:
    98	    /// one admitted disjunct proves an `or`, while every conjunct proves an
    99	    /// `and`. This is the same propositional narrowing used by control flow.
   100	    pub(super) fn authorization_proved(&self, condition: &A, truth: bool) -> bool {
   101	        if self.condition_known(condition, truth) || self.authorization_holds(condition, truth) {
   102	            return true;
   103	        }
   104	        match condition {
   105	            A::Unary { op, value, .. } if op == "not" => self.authorization_proved(value, !truth),
   106	            A::Binary {
   107	                op, left, right, ..
   108	            } if op == "and" && truth => {
   109	                self.authorization_proved(left, true) && self.authorization_proved(right, true)
   110	            }
   111	            A::Binary {
   112	                op, left, right, ..
   113	            } if op == "and" => {
   114	                self.authorization_proved(left, false) || self.authorization_proved(right, false)
   115	            }
   116	            A::Binary {
   117	                op, left, right, ..
   118	            } if op == "or" && truth => {
   119	                self.authorization_proved(left, true) || self.authorization_proved(right, true)
   120	            }
   121	            A::Binary {
   122	                op, left, right, ..
   123	            } if op == "or" => {
   124	                self.authorization_proved(left, false) && self.authorization_proved(right, false)
   125	            }
   126	            _ => false,
   127	        }
   128	    }
   129	
   130	    fn authorization_key(
   131	        &self,
   132	        condition: &A,
   133	        truth: bool,
   134	        domain: AuthorizationDomain,
   135	    ) -> Option<(Vec<String>, BTreeSet<String>)> {
   136	        let mut roots = BTreeSet::new();
   137	        let key = self.condition_identity(condition, truth, domain, &mut roots)?;
   138	        if roots.is_empty() {
   139	            roots.insert(pure_calls::CONSTANTS.to_owned());
   140	        }
   141	        Some((vec![AUTHORIZATION.into(), key], roots))
   142	    }
   143	
   144	    fn condition_identity(
   145	        &self,
   146	        condition: &A,
   147	        truth: bool,
   148	        domain: AuthorizationDomain,
   149	        roots: &mut BTreeSet<String>,
   150	    ) -> Option<String> {
   151	        match condition {
   152	            A::Unary { op, value, .. } if op == "not" => {
   153	                self.condition_identity(value, !truth, domain, roots)
   154	            }
   155	            A::Exists { read, .. } => {
   156	                let raw = matches!(domain, AuthorizationDomain::Rule)
   157	                    || truth
   158	                    || self.program_absence_is_raw(read);
   159	                let identity = self.read_identity("exists", if raw { "raw" } else { "readable" }, read, roots)?;
   160	                Some(format!("truth:{truth}:{identity}"))
   161	            }
   162	            A::Created { read, .. } => {
   163	                let identity = self.read_identity("created", "raw", read, roots)?;
   164	                Some(format!("truth:{truth}:{identity}"))
   165	            }
   166	            A::Binary {
   167	                op, left, right, ..
   168	            } if matches!(op.as_str(), "=" | "!=") => {
   169	                let mut operands = [
   170	                    self.value_identity(left, domain, roots)?,
   171	                    self.value_identity(right, domain, roots)?,
   172	                ];
   173	                operands.sort();
   174	                let equal = (op == "=") == truth;
   175	                Some(format!("compare:{equal}:{:?}", operands))
   176	            }
   177	            _ => Some(format!(
   178	                "truth:{truth}:{}",
   179	                self.value_identity(condition, domain, roots)?
   180	            )),
   181	        }
   182	    }
   183	
   184	    fn value_identity(&self, value: &A, domain: AuthorizationDomain, roots: &mut BTreeSet<String>) -> Option<String> {
   185	        match value {
   186	            A::Unary { op, value, .. } => {
   187	                Some(format!("unary:{op}:{}", self.value_identity(value, domain, roots)?))
   188	            }
   189	            A::Binary {
   190	                op, left, right, ..
   191	            } => Some(format!(
   192	                "binary:{op}:{}:{}",
   193	                self.value_identity(left, domain, roots)?,
   194	                self.value_identity(right, domain, roots)?
   195	            )),
   196	            // Nested witnesses have no known polarity. Keep their observable
   197	            // program domain separate from the raw witness used by a rule.
   198	            A::Exists { read, .. } => self.read_identity("exists", match domain {
   199	                AuthorizationDomain::Program => "readable",
   200	                AuthorizationDomain::Rule => "raw",
   201	            }, read, roots),
   202	            A::Created { read, .. } => self.read_identity("created", "raw", read, roots),
   203	            _ => self.stable_value(value, roots).map(|value| value.key()),
   204	        }
   205	    }
   206	
   207	    fn read_identity(
   208	        &self,
   209	        kind: &str,
   210	        domain: &str,
   211	        read: &ReadExpr,
   212	        roots: &mut BTreeSet<String>,
   213	    ) -> Option<String> {
   214	        if read.range.is_some()
   215	            || read.matching.is_some()
   216	            || read.filter.is_some()
   217	            || !read.seeks.is_empty()
   218	        {
   219	            return None;
   220	        }
   221	        let keys = read
   222	            .keys
   223	            .iter()
   224	            .map(|key| {
   225	                Some(format!(
   226	                    "{:?}:{}",
   227	                    key.column,
   228	                    self.value_identity(&key.value, AuthorizationDomain::Rule, roots)?
   229	                ))
   230	            })
   231	            .collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>()?;
   232	        Some(format!("{kind}:{domain}:{:?}:{:?}:{keys:?}", read.table, read.index))
   233	    }
   234	
   235	    /// A negative program existence test establishes raw absence only when
   236	    /// every row selected by its complete point key is structurally readable.
   237	    fn program_absence_is_raw(&self, read: &ReadExpr) -> bool {
   238	        if self.caller_kind == ProgramKind::Job && self.viewer_depth.is_none() {
   239	            return true;
   240	        }
   241	        program_absence_is_raw(self.schema, read)
   242	    }
   243	
   244	    fn condition_known(&self, condition: &A, truth: bool) -> bool {
   245	        match condition {
   246	            A::Literal {
   247	                value: Literal::Bool(value),
   248	                ..
   249	            } => *value == truth,
   250	            A::Unary { op, value, .. } if op == "not" => self.condition_known(value, !truth),
   251	            A::Binary {
   252	                op, left, right, ..
   253	            } if op == "and" && truth => {
   254	                self.condition_known(left, true) && self.condition_known(right, true)
   255	            }
   256	            A::Binary {
   257	                op, left, right, ..
   258	            } if op == "or" && truth => {
   259	                self.condition_known(left, true) || self.condition_known(right, true)
   260	            }
   261	            A::Binary {
   262	                op, left, right, ..
   263	            } if matches!(op.as_str(), "=" | "!=") => {
   264	                let mut roots = BTreeSet::new();
   265	                let same = self
   266	                    .value_identity(left, AuthorizationDomain::Rule, &mut roots)
   267	                    .zip(self.value_identity(right, AuthorizationDomain::Rule, &mut roots))
   268	                    .is_some_and(|(left, right)| left == right);
   269	                same && ((op == "=") == truth)
   270	            }
   271	            _ => false,
   272	        }
   273	    }
   274	}
   275	
   276	/// Whether an authorized program miss is also a raw miss for the complete
   277	/// point key. Shared with the matching authoring note so type and lint facts
   278	/// use one witness-domain boundary.
   279	pub(in crate::bind) fn program_absence_is_raw(
   280	    schema: &snapback4_core::schema::Schema,
   281	    read: &ReadExpr,
   282	) -> bool {
   283	    let Some(table) = schema.table(&read.table) else { return false };
   284	    // A through child inherits its parent's visibility outside the child's
   285	    // compiled rule. A raw rule witness can still see the live child.
   286	    if table.through.is_some() { return false; }
   287	    if table.rules.read.is_uniformly_readable() { return true; }
   288	    if read.range.is_some() || read.matching.is_some() || read.filter.is_some() || !read.seeks.is_empty() {
   289	        return false;
   290	    }
   291	    let term = |value: &A| match value {
   292	        A::Name { name, .. } if name == "viewer" => Some(RuleTerm::Viewer),
   293	        A::Literal { value: Literal::Str(value), .. } => Some(RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::String(value.clone()))),
   294	        A::Literal { value: Literal::Number(value), site } => super::super::integer_literal(value, site).ok()
   295	            .map(|value| RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::Int(value))),
   296	        A::Literal { value: Literal::Bool(value), .. } => Some(RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::Bool(*value))),
   297	        A::Literal { value: Literal::Null, .. } => Some(RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::Null)),
   298	        _ => None,
   299	    };
   300	    let Some(supplied) = read.keys.iter().map(|key| term(&key.value).map(|term| (key.column.as_deref(), term)))
   301	        .collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>() else { return false };
   302	    let fits = |term: &RuleTerm, column: &snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType| {
   303	        if matches!(term, RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::Null)) { return column.is_optional(); }
   304	        match (term, column.required()) {
   305	            (RuleTerm::Viewer, snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Principal) => true,
   306	            (RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::Bool(_)), snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Bool) => true,
   307	            (RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::Int(_)), column) if column.is_int() => true,
   308	            (RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::String(_)), snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Text { .. } | snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Enum { .. }) => true,
   309	            (RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::String(_) | snapback4_core::Value::Id(_)), snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Id | snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Principal | snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Ref { .. }) => true,
   310	            _ => false,
   311	        }
   312	    };
   313	    let candidates: Vec<_> = table.indexes.iter().filter(|(name, index)| {
   314	        if read.index.as_ref().is_some_and(|selected| selected != *name) { return false; }
   315	        if !index.is_plain() { return false; }
   316	        if supplied.iter().all(|(column, _)| column.is_some()) {
   317	            index.components.len() == supplied.len()
   318	                && index.columns().iter().all(|column| supplied.iter().any(|(supplied, _)| supplied == &Some(column.as_str())))
   319	        } else {
   320	            supplied.iter().all(|(column, _)| column.is_none())
   321	                && index.unique && index.components.len() == supplied.len()
   322	                && index.columns().iter().zip(&supplied).all(|(column, (_, term))| table.columns.get(column).is_some_and(|ty| fits(term, ty)))
   323	        }
   324	    }).collect();
   325	    let [(index_name, index)] = candidates.as_slice() else { return false };
   326	    let key = if supplied.iter().all(|(column, _)| column.is_some()) {
   327	        let Some(key) = index.columns().iter().map(|column| supplied.iter()
   328	            .find_map(|(supplied, term)| (*supplied == Some(column)).then_some(term.clone())))
   329	            .collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>() else { return false };
   330	        key
   331	    } else {
   332	        supplied.into_iter().map(|(_, term)| term).collect()
   333	    };
   334	    schema.rule_input_is_readable(&read.table, index_name, &key)
   335	}
   336	
   337	fn substituted_rule(maintain: &ast::MaintainDecl, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<A> {
   338	    let rule = maintain.read.as_ref()?;
   339	    let keys = group_keys(maintain, read);
   340	    if keys.len() != maintain.by.len() { return None; }
   341	    let mut expected = rule.value.clone();
   342	    normalize_rule_constants(&mut expected);
   343	    // Rule focals name the maintained target row. Seal that namespace before
   344	    // substituting declared group fields so a remaining target-only field can
   345	    // never borrow the caller projection's focal row.
   346	    super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut expected, &mut |expr| {
   347	        if let A::Member { base, .. } = expr && let A::Focal { site } = base.as_ref() {
   348	            **base = A::Name { name: TARGET_ROW.into(), site: site.clone() };
   349	        }
   350	    });
   351	    super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut expected, &mut |expr| {
   352	        if let A::Member { base, name, .. } = expr
   353	            && matches!(base.as_ref(), A::Name { name, .. } if name == TARGET_ROW)
   354	            && let Some(value) = keys.get(name)
   355	        {
   356	            *expr = value.clone();
   357	        }
   358	    });
   359	    Some(expected)
   360	}
   361	
   362	fn group_keys(maintain: &ast::MaintainDecl, read: &ReadExpr) -> BTreeMap<String, A> {
   363	    if maintain.by.len() != read.keys.len() { return BTreeMap::new(); }
   364	    let mut keys: BTreeMap<_, _> = maintain.by.iter().map(|field| field.trim_start_matches('.').to_owned())
   365	        .zip(read.keys.iter().map(|key| key.value.clone())).collect();
   366	    for key in &read.keys {
   367	        if let Some(column) = &key.column { keys.insert(column.clone(), key.value.clone()); }
   368	    }
   369	    keys
   370	}
   371	
   372	/// Rule constants occupy their own namespace. Freeze that identity before a
   373	/// group field is replaced by a program expression whose lexical name may be
   374	/// `allow`, `public`, or `deny`.
   375	pub(in crate::bind) fn normalize_rule_constants(expression: &mut A) {
   376	    super::super::intent_walk::expression(expression, &mut |expr| {
   377	        let A::Name { name, site } = expr else { return };
   378	        let value = match name.as_str() {
   379	            "allow" | "public" => true,
   380	            "deny" => false,
   381	            _ => return,
   382	        };
   383	        let site = site.clone();
   384	        *expr = A::Literal {
   385	            value: Literal::Bool(value),
   386	            site,
   387	        };
   388	    });
   389	}
     1	//! Null facts for repeated, effect-free helper calls over lexical snapshots.
     2	use super::*;
     3	use std::collections::BTreeSet;
     4	
     5	pub(super) const CONSTANTS: &str = "$pureCallConstants";
     6	
     7	#[derive(Debug)]
     8	pub(super) enum Stable {
     9	    Literal(String),
    10	    Snapshot(String, Vec<String>),
    11	    Viewer,
    12	    Call(String, Vec<Stable>),
    13	}
    14	
    15	impl Binder<'_> {
    16	    // @ref LLP 3000.007#5.2 — a repeated pure call narrows like a snapshot field.
    17	    // Facts live on *every* argument root so the existing branch intersections,
    18	    // reassignment invalidation and loop fixed point apply to them as to fields.
    19	    fn stable_call(&self, value: &A) -> Option<(Vec<String>, BTreeSet<String>)> {
    20	        if !matches!(value, A::Call { .. }) { return None; }
    21	        let mut roots = BTreeSet::new();
    22	        let stable = self.stable_value(value, &mut roots)?;
    23	        if roots.is_empty() { roots.insert(CONSTANTS.into()); }
    24	        Some((vec!["$pureCall".into(), stable.key()], roots))
    25	    }
    26	
    27	    pub(super) fn stable_value(&self, value: &A, roots: &mut BTreeSet<String>) -> Option<Stable> {
    28	        match value {
    29	            A::Literal { .. } => Some(Stable::Literal(print::expression(value))),
    30	            A::Name { name, .. } if name == "viewer" => Some(Stable::Viewer),
    31	            A::Name { name, .. } if matches!(name.as_str(), "now" | "commit" | "new") => None,
    32	            A::Name { .. } | A::Member { .. } | A::Focal { .. } => {
    33	                let (root, path) = control::field_path(value, self.focal.as_ref().map(|(name, _)| name.as_str()))?;
    34	                let runtime = if let Some(binding) = self.binding(&root) {
    35	                    roots.insert(root.clone());
    36	                    binding.runtime.clone()
    37	                } else {
    38	                    // A shape's implicit row is an immutable lexical snapshot.
    39	                    // Its unique runtime identity separates projection scopes;
    40	                    // with no mutable argument roots, use the constant anchor.
    41	                    self.focal.as_ref().filter(|(name, _)| name == &root)?.0.clone()
    42	                };
    43	                Some(Stable::Snapshot(runtime, path))
    44	            }
    45	            A::Call { name, args, .. } => {
    46	                let function = self.catalog.functions.get(name)?;
    47	                if !self.pure_functions.get(&function.name).copied().unwrap_or(false) { return None; }
    48	                Some(Stable::Call(function.name.clone(), args.iter().map(|arg| self.stable_value(arg, roots)).collect::<Option<_>>()?))
    49	            }
    50	            _ => None,
    51	        }
    52	    }
    53	
    54	    pub(super) fn narrow_call(&mut self, value: &A, present: bool) {
    55	        let Some((key, roots)) = self.stable_call(value) else { return; };
    56	        let Some(ty) = self.call_types.get(&key) else { return; };
    57	        let ty = if present { ty.required() } else { Ty::Null };
    58	        for root in roots {
    59	            let mut binding = self.binding(&root).expect("a stable root").clone();
    60	            binding.fields.insert(key.clone(), ty.clone());
    61	            self.scopes.last_mut().expect("a scope").insert(root, binding);
    62	        }
    63	    }
    64	
    65	    pub(super) fn record_call(&mut self, value: &A, ty: &Ty) {
    66	        if let Some((key, _)) = self.stable_call(value) { self.call_types.insert(key, ty.clone()); }
    67	    }
    68	
    69	    pub(super) fn call_refinement(&self, value: &A) -> Option<Ty> {
    70	        let (key, roots) = self.stable_call(value)?;
    71	        let mut result = None;
    72	        for root in roots {
    73	            let ty = self.binding(&root)?.fields.get(&key)?;
    74	            if result.as_ref().is_some_and(|other| other != ty) { return None; }
    75	            result = Some(ty.clone());
    76	        }
    77	        result
    78	    }
    79	}
    80	
    81	impl Stable {
    82	    // Escaped serialization of the structured identity, never source
    83	    // sites or helper temporaries. Argument order and snapshot identity matter.
    84	    pub(super) fn key(&self) -> String {
    85	        match self {
    86	            Self::Literal(value) => format!("literal:{value:?}"),
    87	            Self::Snapshot(root, path) => format!("snapshot:{root:?}:{path:?}"),
    88	            Self::Viewer => "viewer".into(),
    89	            Self::Call(name, args) => format!("call:{name:?}:{:?}", args.iter().map(Self::key).collect::<Vec<_>>()),
    90	        }
    91	    }
    92	}
    93	
    94	// Inspect expanded IR, after argument setup: this covers transitive helpers,
    95	// named shapes, aggregates and reads hidden in blocks without a second AST
    96	// effect model. It only classifies effects; calls still execute and are metered.
    97	pub(super) fn expression(value: &Expr) -> bool {
    98	    match value {
    99	        Expr::Literal(_) | Expr::Variable(_) | Expr::Input(Input::Viewer) => true,
   100	        Expr::Input(Input::Now | Input::ReadTime | Input::NewId) => false,
   101	        Expr::Get(_) | Expr::Exists(_) | Expr::Scan(_) | Expr::Merge(_) => false,
   102	        Expr::Field { base, .. } | Expr::Unary { value: base, .. } => expression(base),
   103	        Expr::Array(values) => values.iter().all(expression),
   104	        Expr::Object(fields) => fields.values().all(expression),
   105	        Expr::Shape { base, fields } => expression(base) && fields.values().all(expression),
   106	        Expr::Binary { left, right, .. } => expression(left) && expression(right),
   107	        Expr::Builtin { builtin, args } => builtin_pure(builtin) && args.iter().all(expression),
   108	        Expr::Block { body, value } => statements(body) && expression(value),
   109	    }
   110	}
   111	
   112	fn builtin_pure(builtin: &Builtin) -> bool {
   113	    match builtin {
   114	        Builtin::RequireUncapped => false,
   115	        Builtin::JobAs | Builtin::JobQuery { .. } => false,
   116	        Builtin::Connected | Builtin::ReceiptSettled { .. } => false,
   117	        Builtin::ProjectFields { .. } | Builtin::OptionalField { .. } | Builtin::Calendar { .. } | Builtin::Civil(_)
   118	        | Builtin::Len | Builtin::Slice | Builtin::Concat | Builtin::Lower | Builtin::Trim
   119	        | Builtin::Sha256 | Builtin::Join | Builtin::Group { .. } | Builtin::First | Builtin::Last
   120	        | Builtin::At | Builtin::Min | Builtin::Max | Builtin::Extremum { .. }
   121	        | Builtin::ExtremumOf { .. } | Builtin::Divmod { .. } | Builtin::Divide { .. } | Builtin::Sort { .. }
   122	        | Builtin::MentionTokens | Builtin::SearchPresent | Builtin::DeterministicShare
   123	        | Builtin::Count | Builtin::Sum | Builtin::CountCapped => true,
   124	    }
   125	}
   126	
   127	pub(super) fn statements(body: &[Stmt]) -> bool {
   128	    body.iter().all(|statement| match statement {
   129	        Stmt::Let { value, .. } | Stmt::Const { value, .. } | Stmt::Assign { value, .. }
   130	        | Stmt::Push { value, .. } | Stmt::Return(value) => expression(value),
   131	        Stmt::Require { condition, .. } => expression(condition),
   132	        Stmt::If { condition, then_body, else_body } => expression(condition) && statements(then_body) && statements(else_body),
   133	        Stmt::For { domain, body, .. } => {
   134	            let pure = match domain {
   135	                LoopDomain::LiteralRange { .. } | LoopDomain::ArgList { .. } => true,
   136	                LoopDomain::ExpressionRange { start, end_exclusive } => expression(start) && expression(end_exclusive),
   137	                LoopDomain::DerivedArray { value, .. } => expression(value),
   138	                LoopDomain::Page(_) | LoopDomain::Recurse(_) => false,
   139	            };
   140	            pure && statements(body)
   141	        }
   142	        Stmt::Break | Stmt::Continue => true,
   143	        Stmt::Check { .. } | Stmt::InsertIfAbsent { .. } | Stmt::Insert { .. }
   144	        | Stmt::Update { .. } | Stmt::Upsert { .. } | Stmt::WriteTarget { .. }
   145	        | Stmt::Delete { .. } | Stmt::DeleteTarget { .. } | Stmt::Emit { .. } => false,
   146	    })
   147	}

     3	use super::*;

     5	impl Binder<'_> {
     6	    // ----- statements -----
     7	
     8	    pub(super) fn block(&mut self, statements: &[S], out: &mut setup::Body, completes: bool) -> Fallible<()> {
     9	        let before = self.visible_bindings();
    10	        self.push_scope();
    11	        let count = statements.len();
    12	        let mut result = Ok(());
    13	        for (index, statement) in statements.iter().enumerate() {
    14	            if let Err(error) = self.statement(statement, out, completes && index + 1 == count) {
    15	                if result.is_ok() { result = Err(error); }
    16	            }
    17	        }
    18	        let after = self.visible_bindings();
    19	        self.pop_scope();
    20	        for (name, mut old) in before {
    21	            let new = &after[&name];
    22	            if old.runtime == new.runtime && old.revision != new.revision {
    23	                self.scopes.last_mut().expect("a scope").insert(name, new.clone());
    24	            } else if old.runtime == new.runtime {
    25	                // Facts added inside a lexical block remain local, while a
    26	                // write's invalidation must reach the enclosing loop header.
    27	                let surviving_fields: BTreeMap<_, _> = old.fields.iter()
    28	                    .filter(|(path, ty)| new.fields.get(*path) == Some(*ty))
    29	                    .map(|(path, ty)| (path.clone(), ty.clone()))
    30	                    .collect();
    31	                if surviving_fields.len() != old.fields.len() {
    32	                    old.fields = surviving_fields;
    33	                    self.scopes.last_mut().expect("a scope").insert(name, old);
    34	                }
    35	            }
    36	        }
    37	        result
    38	    }

    40	    pub(super) fn append_errors(&mut self, diagnostics: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>) {
    41	        for error in self.errors.drain(..) {
    42	            if !diagnostics.contains(&error) { diagnostics.push(error); }
    43	        }
    44	    }
    45	
    46	    pub(super) fn record_error(&mut self, error: Diagnostic) {
    47	        if !self.errors.contains(&error) { self.errors.push(error); }
    48	    }
    49	
    50	    pub(super) fn statement(&mut self, statement: &S, out: &mut setup::Body, completes: bool) -> Fallible<()> {
    51	        let result = self.statement_inner(statement, out, completes);
    52	        if let Err(error) = &result {
    53	            self.record_error(error.clone());
    54	            if let S::Bind { names, .. } = statement {
    55	                for name in names {
    56	                    if self.binding(name).is_some_and(|binding| binding.parameter) { continue; }
    57	                    let runtime = self.declare(name, Ty::Unknown);
    58	                    let binding = self.scopes.last_mut().unwrap().get_mut(name).unwrap();
    59	                    binding.error = Some(error.clone());
    60	                    out.push(Stmt::Let { name: runtime, value: Expr::Literal(Value::Null) });
    61	                }
    62	            }
    63	        }
    64	        result
    65	    }

    67	    // @ref LLP 3000.007#5.1 — recursive growth is an assignment failure;

    75	    fn statement_inner(&mut self, statement: &S, out: &mut setup::Body, completes: bool) -> Fallible<()> {

     1	//! Resolve row extrema without changing the binary min/max overload.
     2	use super::*;
     3	
     4	impl Binder<'_> {
     5	    // @ref LLP 3000.019#3.2 — literal columns retain their encoding and seek;
     6	    // other scalar values bind to the aggregate row and fold the whole scan.
     7	    pub(super) fn extremum(&mut self, name: &str, args: &[A], out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<Option<(Expr, Ty)>> {
     8	        if matches!(&args[1], A::Member { base, .. } if matches!(base.as_ref(), A::Focal { .. })) {
     9	            return self.column_extremum(name, args, out);
    10	        }
    11	        let candidate = self.aggregate_domain(&args[0]).or_else(|| {
    12	            self.catalog.extremum_domains.get(&args[0].site().to_string()).filter(|read|
    13	                !self.catalog.tables.get(&read.table).and_then(|table| table.maintained.as_ref())
    14	                    .is_some_and(|maintain| maintain.by.is_empty())).cloned()
    15	        });
    16	        let domain = if let Some(read) = candidate {
    17	            self.is_page(&read, ReadMode::FilteredPoint)?.then_some(read)
    18	        } else { None };
    19	        let nonempty = domain.as_ref().is_some_and(|read| self.aggregate_nonempty(read));
    20	        let mut probe = self.clone();
    21	        let source_ty = match probe.expr(&args[0], &mut setup::Body::default()) {
    22	            Ok((_, ty)) => {
    23	                if ty.sequence().is_none() { return Ok(None); }
    24	                Some(ty)
    25	            }
    26	            Err(error) if domain.is_none() => return Err(error),
    27	            Err(_) => None,
    28	        };
    29	        let outer = probe.expr(&args[1], &mut setup::Body::default());
    30	        if let Ok((_, other)) = &outer {
    31	            if !other.is_optional() && source_ty.as_ref().is_some_and(|ty| ty.comparable(other)) {
    32	                return Ok(None); // the existing binary collection overload
    33	            }
    34	            // An expression which needs the enclosing focal row cannot silently
    35	            // switch to the aggregate row. Removing just that focal binding also
    36	            // distinguishes nested shapes/aggregates that own their own `.`.
    37	            probe.focal = None;
    38	            if self.focal.is_some() && probe.expr(&args[1], &mut setup::Body::default()).is_err() {
    39	                return Err(ambiguous(args[1].site(), format!("{name}'s value expression can bind the enclosing row"),
    40	                    format!("move `{name}({}, {})` into a helper outside the enclosing shape; pass enclosing values as named arguments", print::expression(&args[0]), print::expression(&args[1]))).guide("reads"));
    41	            }
    42	        }
    43	        let (source, item, domain) = if let Some(read) = domain {
    44	            let (scan, _) = self.scan_at(&read, out, prefix::Position::Aggregate)?;
    45	            self.shape_meter_note(&scan, &read);
    46	            (Expr::Scan(scan), Ty::Row(read.table), true)
    47	        } else {
    48	            let (source, ty) = self.expr(&args[0], out)?;
    49	            (source, ty.sequence().expect("probed sequence").0, false)
    50	        };
    51	        let saved = self.focal.replace(("$filter".into(), item.clone()));
    52	        let predicate = std::mem::replace(&mut self.in_predicate, false);
    53	        let kind = std::mem::replace(&mut self.kind, ProgramKind::Query);
    54	        self.push_scope();
    55	        self.declare("$filter", item);
    56	        let mut body = setup::Body::default();
    57	        let projected = self.expr(&args[1], &mut body);
    58	        self.pop_scope();
    59	        self.kind = kind;
    60	        self.in_predicate = predicate;
    61	        self.focal = saved;
    62	        let (value, ty) = projected?;
    63	        if matches!(ty.required(), Ty::List(..) | Ty::Collection(..) | Ty::Page(..) | Ty::Object(..) | Ty::RowShape(..) | Ty::RowObject(..) | Ty::Row(..) | Ty::WriteRow(..)) {

    66	        }

    69	        Ok(Some((Expr::Builtin { builtin: Builtin::ExtremumOf { max: name == "max", domain }, args: vec![source, value] }, result)))
    70	    }
    71	
    72	    // @ref LLP 3000.013#5.1 — domains retain aggregate admission and coverage.
    73	    fn column_extremum(&mut self, name: &str, args: &[A], out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<Option<(Expr, Ty)>> {
    74	        let A::Member { base, name: field, .. } = &args[1] else { return Ok(None); };
    75	        if !matches!(base.as_ref(), A::Focal { .. }) { return Ok(None); }
    76	        // Speculation must not emit setup or consume fresh names for a scalar
    77	        // binary call such as min(value, .column) inside an existing shape.
    78	        let mut probe = self.clone();
    79	        let domain = if let Some(read) = self.aggregate_domain(&args[0]) {
    80	            self.is_page(&read, ReadMode::FilteredPoint)?.then_some(read)
    81	        } else { None };
    82	        let probed = match probe.expr(&args[0], &mut setup::Body::default()) {
    83	            Ok(value) => Some(value),
    84	            Err(error) if domain.is_none() => return Err(error),
    85	            // A streaming domain need not be a valid materialized page.
    86	            Err(_) => None,
    87	        };
    88	        if let Some((_, ty)) = probed {
    89	            let Some((item, _)) = ty.sequence() else { return Ok(None); };
    90	            if let Ok((_, other)) = probe.expr(&args[1], &mut setup::Body::default())
    91	                && !other.is_optional() && ty.comparable(&other) {
    92	                if self.member(Expr::Literal(Value::Null), item, field, args[1].site()).is_ok() {
    93	                    return Err(ambiguous(args[1].site(), format!("{name} can compare two collections or aggregate .{field} from the first"),
    94	                        format!("bind `other = .{field}` before this call and write `{name}({}, other)` for the binary comparison; for an aggregate, call `{name}(rows, .{field})` outside the enclosing shape", print::expression(&args[0]))).guide("reads"));
    95	                }
    96	                return Ok(None);
    97	            }
    98	        }
    99	        let nonempty = domain.as_ref().is_some_and(|read| self.aggregate_nonempty(read));
   100	        let (source, item, domain) = if let Some(mut read) = domain {
   101	            // Bare table names reach the binder without intent planning.
   102	            if matches!(&args[0], A::Name { .. }) {
   103	                let info = self.table(&read.table, &read.site)?;
   104	                if let Some(index) = info.indexes.iter().filter(|index| index.is_plain() && index.columns.first() == Some(field)).min_by_key(|index| &index.name) {
   105	                    read.index = Some(index.name.clone());
   106	                }
   107	            }
   108	            let (mut scan, _) = self.scan_at(&read, out, prefix::Position::Aggregate)?;
   109	            if scan.orders_field(self.schema, field) {
   110	                scan.order = if name == "max" { Order::Desc } else { Order::Asc };
   111	                // Nulls contribute no extremum. Seek past their entire index
   112	                // segment, including when there are no present values.
   113	                if self.schema.tables[&read.table].columns.get(field).is_some_and(|column| matches!(column, snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Optional(_))) {
   114	                    let range = scan.range.get_or_insert(ir::Range { gt: None, gte: None, lt: None, lte: None });
   115	                    if range.gt.is_none() && range.gte.is_none() { range.gt = Some(Box::new(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))); }
   116	                }
   117	            }
   118	            // The runtime seeks the first qualifying ordered value. Price that
   119	            // candidate limit for advice only; the aggregate IR keeps its domain.
   120	            // Expired rows are charged and skipped before a value qualifies.
   121	            let mut advice = scan.clone();
   122	            if scan.orders_field(self.schema, field) && !self.schema.tables[&read.table].is_expiring() { advice.take = Bound::Literal(1); }
   123	            self.shape_meter_note(&advice, &read);
   124	            (Expr::Scan(scan), Ty::Row(read.table), true)
   125	        } else {
   126	            let (source, ty) = self.expr(&args[0], out)?;
   127	            (source, ty.sequence().expect("probed sequence").0, false)
   128	        };
   129	        let (_, ty) = self.member(Expr::Literal(Value::Null), item, field, args[1].site())?;
   130	        // Column ordering uses the same total order as binary min/max. Nulls
   131	        // contribute no value, including when every row's column is null.
   132	        let result = if nonempty && !ty.is_optional() { ty } else { ty.optional() };
   133	        Ok(Some((Expr::Builtin { builtin: Builtin::Extremum { field: field.clone(), max: name == "max", domain }, args: vec![source] }, result)))
   134	    }
   135	}
     1	//! Expression lowering with branch-owned setup statements.
     2	
     3	use super::*;
     4	use snapback4_core::calendar::Civil;
     5	
     6	impl Binder<'_> {
     7	    /// Bare tables are domains only at an aggregate/loop consumer; ordinary
     8	    /// names keep their lexical meaning and ordinary pages keep their bounds.
     9	    pub(super) fn aggregate_domain(&self, value: &A) -> Option<ReadExpr> {
    10	        match value {
    11	            A::Read(read) if read.range.is_none() && read.matching.is_none()
    12	                && !self.catalog.tables.get(&read.table).and_then(|table| table.maintained.as_ref()).is_some_and(|maintain| maintain.by.is_empty()) => Some(read.clone()),
    13	            A::Name { name, site } if self.binding(name).is_none() && self.catalog.tables.contains_key(name)
    14	                && !self.catalog.tables.get(name).and_then(|table| table.maintained.as_ref()).is_some_and(|maintain| maintain.by.is_empty()) =>
    15	                Some(ReadExpr { cursor_prefix: None, order_keys: Vec::new(), computed: Default::default(), table: name.clone(), keys: Vec::new(), index: Some("byId".into()), range: None, matching: None, filter: None, seeks: Vec::new(), site: site.clone() }),
    16	            _ => None,
    17	        }
    18	    }
    19	
    20	    // ----- expressions -----
    21	
    22	    /// An expression whose value is used (bound, returned, or a final
    23	    /// write): writes yield their row.
    24	    pub(super) fn value_of(&mut self, value: &A, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {
    25	        if let A::Write { .. } = value {
    26	            return self.write(value, out, true).map(|opt| opt.expect("a valued write"));
    27	        }
    28	        self.expr(value, out)
    29	    }
    30	
    31	    /// Strict compound children run in source order, before setup emitted by
    32	    /// a later child. Lexical values and their fields cannot change here;
    33	    /// leaving those fields in their object also preserves pending provenance.
    34	    pub(super) fn snapshot(&mut self, value: Expr, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Expr {
    35	        if matches!(value, Expr::Literal(_) | Expr::Input(Input::Viewer | Input::Now)) || lexical_value(&value) {
    36	            return value;
    37	        }
    38	        let name = self.temp("value");
    39	        out.push(Stmt::Let { name: name.clone(), value });
    40	        ir::var(&name)
    41	    }
    42	
    43	    pub(super) fn sequence_setup<'e>(&mut self, prior: impl Iterator<Item = &'e mut Expr>, setup: setup::Body, out: &mut setup::Body) {
    44	        if !setup.is_empty() {
    45	            for value in prior {
    46	                *value = self.snapshot(std::mem::replace(value, Expr::Literal(Value::Null)), out);
    47	            }
    48	            out.extend(setup);
    49	        }
    50	    }
    51	
    52	    pub(super) fn strict_args(&mut self, args: &[A], out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<Vec<(Expr, Ty)>> {
    53	        let mut values = Vec::<(Expr, Ty)>::new();
    54	        let mut failed = None;
    55	        for arg in args {
    56	            let mut setup = setup::Body::default();
    57	            match self.expr(arg, &mut setup) {
    58	                Ok(value) => {
    59	                    self.sequence_setup(values.iter_mut().map(|(e, _)| e), setup, out);
    60	                    values.push(value);
    61	                }
    62	                Err(error) => { self.record_error(error.clone()); failed.get_or_insert(error); }
    63	            }
    64	        }
    65	        match failed { Some(error) => Err(error), None => Ok(values) }
    66	    }
    67	
    68	    pub(super) fn expr(&mut self, expr: &A, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {
    69	        // Only shape/object/list construction forwards output position. Every
    70	        // operator, call, key, predicate and assertion consumes its operands.
    71	        let prior = self.returned_data;
    72	        self.returned_data &= matches!(expr, A::Array { .. } | A::Object { .. } | A::Shape { .. } | A::ShapeUse { .. } | A::Member { .. } | A::Name { .. } | A::Focal { .. });
    73	        let start = out.len();
    74	        let result = self.expr_inner(expr, out);
    75	        if !matches!(expr, A::Write { .. }) && point_facts::writes(&out[start..]) { self.invalidate_points(); }
    76	        self.returned_data = prior;
    77	        // Even a builtin's ordinary type refusal should explain why a whole
    78	        // maintained value cannot be consumed. Successful calls are checked by
    79	        // the shared IR allowlist; this only improves already-failing calls.
    80	        if result.as_ref().is_err_and(|d| d.code == "E_QUARRY_TYPE") {
    81	            let args: Vec<&A> = match expr { A::Call { args, .. } => args.iter().collect(), A::Binary { left, right, .. } => vec![left, right], _ => vec![] };
    82	            let mut probe = self.clone();
    83	            for arg in args {
    84	                if let Ok((_, ty)) = probe.expr(arg, &mut setup::Body::default()) {

    98	    fn maintenance_operand(&self, ty: &Ty, site: &Site) -> Fallible<()> {

   465	    /// After `require x != null` or inside `if x != null:`, `x` is present.
   466	    pub(super) fn narrow(&mut self, condition: &A, then_branch: bool) {
   467	        // Aggregate facts may describe filtered domains, so they have a
   468	        // slightly wider write-free admission check than point facts. Record
   469	        // them once for the whole, already-evaluated condition: recursively
   470	        // recording a safe left operand would be stale when a later operand
   471	        // calls a writeful helper.
   472	        if self.aggregate_guard_safe(condition) {
   473	            self.record_aggregate_nonempty(condition, then_branch);
   474	        }
   475	        self.narrow_inner(condition, then_branch, self.point_guard_safe(condition));
   476	    }
   477	
   478	    fn narrow_inner(&mut self, condition: &A, then_branch: bool, points: bool) {
   479	        // The condition is evaluated before narrowing. If any operand may
   480	        // write, a fact observed by an earlier operand can already be stale.
   481	        if points {
   482	            self.record_maintain_authorization(condition, then_branch);
   483	        }
   484	        match condition {
   485	            A::Exists { read, .. } if then_branch && points => self.narrow_point(read),
   486	            A::Call { name, args, site } if name == super::super::intent_variants::PRESENT => self.narrow_inner(&A::Binary {
   487	                op: "!=".into(), left: Box::new(args[0].clone()), right: Box::new(A::Literal { value: Literal::Null, site: site.clone() }), site: site.clone(),
   488	            }, then_branch, points),
   489	            A::Unary { op, value, .. } if op == "not" => self.narrow_inner(value, !then_branch, points),
   490	            A::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if (op == "and" && then_branch) || (op == "or" && !then_branch) => {
   491	                self.narrow_inner(left, then_branch, points);
   492	                self.narrow_inner(right, then_branch, points);
   493	            }
   494	            A::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "!=" || op == "=" => {
   495	                let candidate = match (left.as_ref(), right.as_ref()) {
   496	                    (A::Literal { value: Literal::Null, .. }, other) | (other, A::Literal { value: Literal::Null, .. }) => other,
   497	                    _ => return,
   498	                };
   499	                if let A::Read(read) = candidate {
   500	                    if points && (op == "!=") == then_branch { self.narrow_point(read); }
   501	                } else if let A::Name { name, .. } = candidate
   502	                    && let Some(mut binding) = self.binding(name).cloned()
   503	                {
   504	                    binding.ty = if (op == "!=") == then_branch { binding.ty.required() } else { Ty::Null };
   505	                    self.scopes.last_mut().expect("a scope").insert(name.clone(), binding);
   506	                } else if let Some((name, path)) = field_path(candidate, self.focal.as_ref().map(|(name, _)| name.as_str()))
   507	                    && !path.is_empty()
   508	                    && let Some(mut binding) = self.binding(&name).cloned()

   513	                    if present { binding.ty = binding.ty.required(); }
   514	                    binding.fields.insert(path, if present { ty.required() } else { Ty::Null });
   515	                    self.scopes.last_mut().expect("a scope").insert(name.to_owned(), binding);
   516	                } else {
   517	                    self.narrow_call(candidate, (op == "!=") == then_branch);
   518	                }
   519	            }
   520	            _ => {}
   521	        }
   522	    }

     6	    pub(super) fn conditional(&mut self, then: &A, condition: &A, otherwise: &A, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {

   601	                    (_, Ty::Int, Ty::Int) => Ty::Int,

   603	                };
   604	                let ir_op = match op {
   605	                    "+" => BinaryOp::Add,
   606	                    "-" => BinaryOp::Sub,
   607	                    _ => BinaryOp::Mul,
   608	                };
   609	                return Ok((Expr::Binary { op: ir_op, left: Box::new(l), right: Box::new(r) }, result));
   610	            }
   611	            other => return Err(unknown(site, format!("unknown operator {other}"), "use =, !=, <, <=, >, >=, +, -, *, and, or, ??")),
   612	        };
   613	        let mut present = Vec::new();
   614	        if matches!(ir_op, BinaryOp::Lt | BinaryOp::Lte | BinaryOp::Gt | BinaryOp::Gte) {
   615	            if nullable_left { present.push(l.clone()); }
   616	            if nullable_right { present.push(r.clone()); }
   617	        }
   618	        let mut comparison = Expr::Binary { op: ir_op, left: Box::new(l), right: Box::new(r) };
   619	        // @ref LLP 3000.013#2.1 — predicate nulls do not enter ordering;
   620	        // the ordinary interpreter's total value ordering remains unchanged.
   621	        for value in present.into_iter().rev() {
   622	            comparison = Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::And,
   623	                left: Box::new(Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::Ne, left: Box::new(value), right: Box::new(ir::lit(Value::Null)) }),
   624	                right: Box::new(comparison) };
   625	        }
   626	        Ok((comparison, Ty::Bool))
   627	    }

   629	    /// An expression in an identity position: a text literal becomes an id.
   630	    pub(super) fn expr_as_identity(&mut self, expr: &A, out: &mut setup::Body, expected: &Ty) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {
   631	        if let A::Literal { value: Literal::Str(text), .. } = expr {
   632	            if text.is_empty() {
   633	                return Err(type_error(expr.site(), "an empty text literal cannot be an id", "use a non-empty id literal, or null"));
   634	            }
   635	            return Ok((Expr::Literal(Value::Id(text.clone())), expected.required()));
   636	        }
   637	        self.expr(expr, out)
   638	    }
   639	
   640	    pub(super) fn expr_as_reference(&mut self, expr: &A, out: &mut setup::Body, expected: &Ty) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {
   641	        let (value, ty) = self.expr_as_identity(expr, out, expected)?;
   642	        Ok(self.row_identity(value, ty, expected))
   643	    }
   644	
   645	    // @ref LLP 3000.036.002#8 — contextual projection is exactly `.id`,
   646	    // without a lookup; optional and cross-table rows still need an edit.
   647	    pub(super) fn row_identity(&self, value: Expr, ty: Ty, expected: &Ty) -> (Expr, Ty) {
   648	        if !ty.is_optional() && matches!(ty.required(), Ty::Row(_) | Ty::WriteRow(_)) {
   649	            let table = match ty.required() { Ty::Row(t) | Ty::WriteRow(t) => t, _ => unreachable!() };
   650	            if expected.required() == Ty::Ref(table.clone()) {
   651	                return (ir::field(value, "id"), Ty::Ref(table));
   652	            }
   653	        }
   654	        (value, ty)
   655	    }

   657	    pub(super) fn call(&mut self, name: &str, args: &[A], site: &Site, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {
   658	        if let Some(result) = self.job_query_call(name, args, site, out) { return result; }
   659	        // Compiler-only references bypass the function catalog entirely.
   660	        let primitive = name.strip_prefix("$builtin:");
   661	        if primitive.is_none() && let Some(function) = self.catalog.functions.get(name).cloned() {
   662	            return self.fn_call(&function, args, site, out);
   663	        }
   664	        let name = primitive.unwrap_or(name);
   665	        let arity = |n: usize, rewrite: &str| -> Fallible<()> {
   666	            if args.len() != n {
   667	                return Err(type_error(site, format!("{name} takes {n} argument{}; received {}", if n == 1 { "" } else { "s" }, args.len()), rewrite));
   668	            }
   669	            Ok(())
   670	        };
   671	        match name {
   672	            "receiptSettled" if primitive.is_some() => {
   673	                arity(1, "receipt settlement needs a row")?;
   674	                let (value, ty) = self.expr(&args[0], out)?;
   675	                let Ty::Row(table) = ty else { return Err(type_error(site, "receipt settlement needs a row", "use a receipt row")); };
   676	                Ok((Expr::Builtin { builtin: Builtin::ReceiptSettled { table }, args: vec![value] }, Ty::Bool))
   677	            }
   678	            super::super::intent_variants::PRESENT => {
   679	                let (value, _) = self.expr(&args[0], out)?;
   680	                Ok((Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::Ne, left: Box::new(value), right: Box::new(Expr::Literal(Value::Null)) }, Ty::Bool))
   681	            }
   682	            super::super::intent_variants::SEARCH => {
   683	                let (value, ty) = self.expr(&args[0], out)?;
   684	                if !matches!(ty, Ty::Text(_)) { return Err(type_error(site, "matching needs present text", "matching (q ?? '')")); }
   685	                let A::Name { name, .. } = &args[1] else { unreachable!("planned needle binding") };
   686	                let runtime = self.temp("intentNeedle");
   687	                self.declare(name, ty);
   688	                self.scopes.last_mut().unwrap().get_mut(name).unwrap().runtime = runtime.clone();
   689	                out.push(Stmt::Let { name: runtime.clone(), value });
   690	                Ok((Expr::Builtin { builtin: Builtin::SearchPresent, args: vec![ir::var(&runtime)] }, Ty::Bool))
   691	            }
   692	            "day" | "hour" | "bucket" => {
   693	                let (millis, offset) = super::super::maintains::calendar(name, args, site)?;
   694	                let (value, ty) = self.expr(&args[0], out)?;
   695	                if ty != Ty::Time { return Err(type_error(args[0].site(), format!("{name} needs a required time, got {}", ty.name()), "pass a required time column or now")); }
   696	                let mut args = vec![value];
   697	                if offset != 0 { args.push(Expr::Literal(Value::Int(offset))); }
   698	                Ok((Expr::Builtin { builtin: Builtin::Calendar { millis }, args }, Ty::Int))
   699	            }
   700	            // @ref LLP 3000.040#22-builtins — months since 1970-01 and calendar years, UTC, and back to a time.
   701	            "month" | "year" => {
   702	                arity(1, &format!("{name}(time); civil periods are UTC and take no offset"))?;
   703	                let (value, ty) = self.expr(&args[0], out)?;
   704	                if ty != Ty::Time { return Err(type_error(args[0].site(), format!("{name} needs a required time, got {}", ty.name()), format!("{name}(.at), {name}(now), or {name}(t ?? now)")).guide("time")); }
   705	                let function = if name == "month" { Civil::Month } else { Civil::Year };
   706	                Ok((Expr::Builtin { builtin: Builtin::Civil(function), args: vec![value] }, Ty::Int))
   707	            }
   708	            "monthStart" | "yearStart" => {
   709	                let unit = if name == "monthStart" { "month" } else { "year" };
   710	                arity(1, &format!("{name}({unit}); {unit}(t) gives one"))?;
   711	                let (value, ty) = self.expr(&args[0], out)?;
   712	                if ty != Ty::Int { return Err(type_error(args[0].site(), format!("{name} takes a {unit} number (an int), got {}", ty.name()), format!("{name}({unit}(t)), or {name}({unit}(now) + 1) for the next")).guide("time")); }
   713	                let function = if name == "monthStart" { Civil::MonthStart } else { Civil::YearStart };
   714	                Ok((Expr::Builtin { builtin: Builtin::Civil(function), args: vec![value] }, Ty::Time))
   715	            }
   716	            "count" => {
   717	                arity(1, "count(list)")?;
   718	                if let Some(read) = self.aggregate_domain(&args[0]) && self.is_page(&read, ReadMode::FilteredPoint)? {
   719	                    let saved = self.total.replace(("count", None));
   720	                    let scanned = self.scan_at(&read, out, prefix::Position::Aggregate);
   721	                    self.total = saved;
   722	                    let (scan, _) = scanned?;
   723	                    self.shape_meter_note(&scan, &read);
   724	                    return Ok((Expr::Builtin { builtin: Builtin::Count, args: vec![Expr::Scan(scan)] }, Ty::Int));
   725	                }
   726	                let (e, t) = self.expr(&args[0], out)?;
   727	                if matches!(t, Ty::Text(_)) {
   728	                    return Ok((Expr::Builtin { builtin: Builtin::Len, args: vec![e] }, Ty::Int));
   729	                }
   730	                if t.loop_max().is_none() {
   731	                    return Err(type_error(
   732	                        args[0].site(),
   733	                        format!("count takes present text, a list or a page, got {}", t.name()),
   734	                        "count(text), count(list), or count(page); narrow optional values first",
   735	                    ));
   736	                }
   737	                Ok((Expr::Builtin { builtin: Builtin::Count, args: vec![e] }, Ty::Int))
   738	            }
   739	            "lower" | "trim" => {
   740	                arity(1, &format!("{name}(text)"))?;
   741	                let (value, ty) = self.expr(&args[0], out)?;
   742	                let Ty::Text(max) = ty else {
   743	                    return Err(type_error(site, format!("{name} takes present text, got {}", ty.name()), &format!("{name}(text ?? '')")));
   744	                };
   745	                let (builtin, max) = if name == "lower" { (Builtin::Lower, max.saturating_mul(3)) } else { (Builtin::Trim, max) };
   746	                Ok((Expr::Builtin { builtin, args: vec![value] }, Ty::Text(max)))
   747	            }
   748	            "sha256" => {
   749	                arity(1, "sha256(text)")?;
   750	                let (value, ty) = self.expr(&args[0], out)?;
   751	                if !matches!(ty, Ty::Text(_)) {
   752	                    return Err(type_error(site, format!("sha256 takes present text, got {}", ty.name()), "sha256(text ?? '')"));
   753	                }
   754	                Ok((Expr::Builtin { builtin: Builtin::Sha256, args: vec![value] }, Ty::Text(64)))
   755	            }
   756	            "group" => {
   757	                arity(2, "group(list, 'column')")?;
   758	                let (value, ty) = self.expr(&args[0], out)?;
   759	                let Some((item, max)) = ty.sequence() else { return Err(type_error(site, "group takes a list or page", "group(rows, 'column')")); };
   760	                let A::Literal { value: Literal::Str(field), .. } = &args[1] else { return Err(type_error(args[1].site(), "group names one field with a text literal", "group(rows, 'column')")); };
   761	                let (selection, key) = self.member(Expr::Literal(Value::Null), item.clone(), field, args[1].site())?;
   762	                let optional = matches!(selection, Expr::Builtin { builtin: Builtin::OptionalField { .. }, .. });
   763	                let group = Ty::Object(BTreeMap::from([("key".into(), key), ("items".into(), Ty::List(Box::new(item), max))]));
   764	                Ok((Expr::Builtin { builtin: Builtin::Group { field: field.clone(), optional }, args: vec![value] }, Ty::List(Box::new(group), max)))
   765	            }
   766	            "first" | "last" | "at" => {
   767	                arity(if name == "at" { 2 } else { 1 }, &format!("{name}(list{})", if name == "at" { ", index" } else { "" }))?;
   768	                let values = self.strict_args(args, out)?;
   769	                let Some((item, _)) = values[0].1.sequence() else { return Err(type_error(args[0].site(), format!("{name} takes a list or page"), &format!("{name}(rows)"))); };
   770	                if name == "at" && values[1].1 != Ty::Int { return Err(type_error(args[1].site(), "at's index is an int", "at(list, 0)")); }
   771	                Ok((Expr::Builtin { builtin: match name { "first" => Builtin::First, "last" => Builtin::Last, _ => Builtin::At }, args: values.into_iter().map(|(value, _)| value).collect() }, item.optional()))
   772	            }
   773	            "join" => {
   774	                arity(2, "join(list, separator)")?;
   775	                let [(list, lt), (sep, st)]: [(Expr, Ty); 2] = self.strict_args(args, out)?.try_into().expect("checked arity");
   776	                let max = match (lt.sequence(), &st) {
   777	                    (Some((item, count)), Ty::Text(sep)) => match item {
   778	                        Ty::Text(max) => max.saturating_mul(count).saturating_add(sep.saturating_mul(count.saturating_sub(1))),
   779	                        Ty::Unknown if count == 0 => 0,
   780	                        _ => return Err(type_error(site, "join takes a list of present text values", "join(['a', 'b'], ', ')")),
   781	                    },
   782	                    _ => return Err(type_error(site, "join takes a list of present text values and a text separator", "join(['a', 'b'], ', ')")),
   783	                };
   784	                Ok((Expr::Builtin { builtin: Builtin::Join, args: vec![list, sep] }, Ty::Text(max)))
   785	            }
   786	            "sum" if args.len() == 2 => {
   787	                let source = if let Some(read) = self.aggregate_domain(&args[0]) {
   788	                    let saved = self.total.replace(("sum", Some(print::expression(&args[1]))));
   789	                    let scanned = self.scan_at(&read, out, prefix::Position::Aggregate);
   790	                    self.total = saved;
   791	                    let (scan, _) = scanned?;
   792	                    self.shape_meter_note(&scan, &read);
   793	                    if scan.take == Bound::Meter {
   794	                        let saved = self.focal.replace(("$filter".into(), Ty::Row(read.table.clone())));
   795	                        let mut setup = setup::Body::default();
   796	                        let predicate_context = std::mem::replace(&mut self.in_predicate, false);
   797	                        let projected = self.expr(&args[1], &mut setup);
   798	                        self.in_predicate = predicate_context;
   799	                        self.focal = saved;
   800	                        let (projection, ty) = projected?;
   550	    format!("give each reader a row in a membership table synced to its members, and read through it: `table {table}Readers:` with `item: {table}`, `reader: principal`, `unique item, reader`, `immutable item, reader`, `read <- .reader = viewer`, `insert <- created {table}[.item] or exists {table}Readers[.item, viewer]`, `sync`; then here `read <- exists {table}Readers[.id, viewer]` and `sync to {table}Readers[.id] by .reader` (guide sharing){online}")
   551	}

   553	/// A rule stops binding at its first refused hop, so `check` printed one
   554	/// site per rule and builders fixed hops one run at a time (wave 7 lane 062).
   555	/// Each `and`/`or` operand is bound alone to find the rule's other hops,
   556	/// which the one diagnostic names (its rewrite already repairs every hop it
   557	/// can), and a table that reads through this one is named: its copy of the
   558	/// rule is checked only once this rule binds.
   559	// @ref LLP 3000.036#17.6 — every E_QUARRY_RULE_HOP site in one run.
   560	fn every_hop(catalog: &Catalog, info: &TableInfo, rule: &RuleDecl, diagnostic: &mut Diagnostic) {
   561	    fn leaves<'a>(value: &'a Expr, out: &mut Vec<&'a Expr>) {
   562	        match value {
   563	            Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "and" || op == "or" => { leaves(left, out); leaves(right, out); }
   564	            other => out.push(other),
   565	        }
   566	    }
   567	    let mut parts = Vec::new();
   568	    leaves(&rule.value, &mut parts);
   569	    let mut more: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
   570	    for part in parts {
   571	        let single = RuleDecl { value: part.clone(), ..rule.clone() };
   572	        if let Err(other) = bind_rule(catalog, info, &single) && other.code == "E_QUARRY_RULE_HOP" && other.site != diagnostic.site {
   573	            let text = crate::print::expression(part);
   574	            let text = text.strip_prefix('(').and_then(|t| t.strip_suffix(')')).map(str::to_owned).unwrap_or(text);
   575	            let entry = format!("{} (`{text}`)", other.site);
   576	            if !more.contains(&entry) { more.push(entry); }
   577	        }
   578	    }
   579	    if !more.is_empty() {
   580	        diagnostic.message = format!("{}; this {} rule hops at {} more site{} too: {} — the rewrite repairs every hop it names; repair the rest the same way", diagnostic.message, rule.effect.word(), more.len(), if more.len() == 1 { "" } else { "s" }, more.join(", "));
   581	    }
   582	    if rule.effect == Effect::Read {
   583	        let children: Vec<&str> = catalog.tables.iter().filter(|(_, child)| child.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|t| t.parent == info.decl.name)).map(|(name, _)| name.as_str()).collect();
   584	        if !children.is_empty() {
   585	            diagnostic.message = format!("{}; {} read{} through {}, so {} copy of this rule is checked once it binds", diagnostic.message, children.join(", "), if children.len() == 1 { "s" } else { "" }, info.decl.name, if children.len() == 1 { "its" } else { "their" });
   586	        }
   587	    }
   588	}
   589	
   590	/// Bind a rule expression over a table's focal row.
   591	pub fn bind_rule(catalog: &Catalog, info: &TableInfo, rule: &RuleDecl) -> Fallible<RuleExpr> {
   592	    let bindings = RuleBindings { old: rule.bindings.first().cloned(), next: rule.bindings.get(1).cloned(), update: rule.effect == Effect::Update, insert: rule.effect == Effect::Insert, hops: rule.effect != Effect::Read, present: Vec::new() };
   593	    rule_expr(catalog, info, &rule.value, &bindings).map_err(|error| {
   594	        let mut error = super::first_paste::inherited_rule(catalog, info, rule).unwrap_or(error);
   595	        if error.code == "E_QUARRY_RULE_OPERATOR" && rule.effect == Effect::Update && rule.bindings.is_empty()
   596	            && error.rewrite == "write this as a named transition" {
   597	            let source = super::rule_operator::named_update_source(&rule.value);
   598	            error.rewrite = format!("name both row images: `{source}`");
   599	            error.source_rewrite = Some(source);
   600	            error.source_rewrite_kind = Some(crate::diag::SourceRewriteKind::NamedUpdate);
   601	        }
   602	        // @ref LLP 3000.036#13.6 — the named form of a whole unnamed update rule, so the rewrite keeps every other clause.
   603	        if error.code == "E_QUARRY_RULE_OPERATOR" && rule.effect == Effect::Update && rule.bindings.is_empty() && error.rewrite.starts_with("name both images") {
   604	            let comparison = error.message.split('`').nth(1).unwrap_or_default().to_owned();
   605	            error.rewrite = super::rule_operator::named_update(&info.decl.name, &rule.value, &comparison);
   606	        }
   607	        if error.code == "E_QUARRY_RULE_HOP" {
   608	            error.rewrite = match rule.effect {
   609	                Effect::Read => reference::read_hop_rewrite(catalog, info, &rule.value).unwrap_or_else(|| "a read rule probes one hop: copy the deciding value onto this row (immutable, checked against the parent by the insert rule), or use read through .parent".into()),
   610	                _ => reference::reference_rewrite(catalog, info, &rule.value),
   611	            };
   612	        }
   613	        // @ref LLP 3000.036#14.3 — a read-through child binds its parent's rule over hidden
   614	        // captures (`.$enrollment.dancer`): its refusal is the parent's (`internal_names` drops the copy).
   615	        // @ref LLP 3000.036#15.6 — a `read through` that did not expand (E_READ_THROUGH says why)
   616	        // leaves its internal call; its refusal is that problem's cascade, spelled as written.
   617	        if let Expr::Call { name, args, .. } = &rule.value && name == "$readThrough" {
   618	            let reference = match args.as_slice() { [Expr::Name { name, .. }] => name.clone(), _ => "parent".into() };
   619	            error.message = format!("`read through .{reference}` did not expand, so {} has no read rule yet", info.decl.name);
   620	            // The cascade convention: `check` hides it beside its cause unless --verbose.
   621	            error.rewrite = "fix this Quarry line and rerun snapback4 check".into();
   622	            error.cascade_of = Some(rule.site.clone());
   623	            error.mechanical = false;
   624	            return error;
   625	        }
   626	        if rule.effect == Effect::Read && let Some(through) = &info.through {
   627	            error.rewrite = format!("repair {}'s read rule (its own diagnostic prints the repair); {} reads through it", through.parent, info.decl.name);
   628	            error.cascade_of = Some(error.site.clone());
   629	        }
   630	        error
   631	    })
   632	}
   633	
   634	#[derive(Clone)]
   635	struct RuleBindings {
   636	    old: Option<String>,
   637	    next: Option<String>,
   638	    update: bool,
   639	    insert: bool,
   640	    hops: bool,
   641	    present: Vec<RuleTerm>,
   642	}
   643	
   644	// @ref LLP 3000.013#2.2 — presence facts retain a reference term's old/next image.
   645	// Facts hold on the logical edge where the next operand can affect truth.
   646	// Expiry accounting may evaluate other edges too: the shared runtime returns
   647	// no witness for an ordinary unique null key, without accessing tied rows.
   648	// Thus admission does not require physical short-circuiting. Facts still
   649	// cannot leak from one OR alternative into an unrelated alternative.
   650	fn present_terms(rule: &RuleExpr, truth: bool) -> Vec<RuleTerm> {
   651	    match rule {
   652	        RuleExpr::Ne(left, right) if truth => non_null_term(left, right),
   653	        RuleExpr::Eq(left, right) if !truth => non_null_term(left, right),
   654	        RuleExpr::Not(inner) => present_terms(inner, !truth),
   655	        RuleExpr::And(parts) | RuleExpr::Or(parts) => {
   656	            let union = matches!(rule, RuleExpr::And(_)) == truth;
   657	            let mut facts = parts.iter().map(|part| present_terms(part, truth));
   658	            let mut result = facts.next().unwrap_or_default();
   659	            for facts in facts {
   660	                if union { for term in facts { if !result.contains(&term) { result.push(term); } } }
   661	                else { result.retain(|term| facts.contains(term)); }
   662	            }
   663	            result
   664	        }
   665	        _ => Vec::new(),
   666	    }
   667	}
   668	
   669	fn non_null_term(left: &RuleTerm, right: &RuleTerm) -> Vec<RuleTerm> {
   670	    match (left, right) {
   671	        (RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Null), term) | (term, RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Null)) => vec![term.clone()],
   672	        _ => Vec::new(),
   673	    }
   674	}
   675	
   676	fn rule_expr(catalog: &Catalog, info: &TableInfo, expr: &Expr, bindings: &RuleBindings) -> Fallible<RuleExpr> {
   677	    let site = expr.site();
   678	    match expr {
   679	        Expr::Name { name, .. } => match name.as_str() {
   680	            "allow" => Ok(RuleExpr::Allow),
   681	            "deny" => Ok(RuleExpr::Deny),
   682	            "public" => Ok(RuleExpr::Public { reason: format!("{} is public", info.decl.name) }),
   683	            "true" => Ok(RuleExpr::Allow),
   684	            "false" => Ok(RuleExpr::Deny),
   685	            other => Err(unknown(site, format!("`{other}` is not a rule; rules are allow, deny, public, exists, created, and comparisons over ., viewer and literals"), "read <- .ownerId = viewer")),
   686	        },
   687	        Expr::Call { name, args, .. } if name == "public" => {
   688	            let reason = match args.first() {
   689	                Some(Expr::Literal { value: crate::ast::Literal::Str(reason), .. }) => reason.clone(),
   690	                _ => format!("{} is public", info.decl.name),
   691	            };
   692	            Ok(RuleExpr::Public { reason })
   693	        }
   694	        Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } => match op.as_str() {
   695	            "and" | "or" => {
   696	                let left = rule_expr(catalog, info, left, bindings)?;
   697	                let mut guarded = bindings.clone();
   698	                guarded.present.extend(present_terms(&left, op == "and"));

   107	            // lane is decided (a grant lane by a grant covering the rule) and that the lanes share one
   108	            // position. Otherwise a page with a grant branch reads as written, the grant branch alone.

   135	    fn push_value_lane(&mut self, lanes: &mut Vec<MergeLane>, ast: &ast::MergeLane, mut lane: MergeLane, position: prefix::Position) -> Fallible<()> {

   148	                Stmt::Let { name: nulls.clone(), value: Expr::Array(Vec::new()) },

   158	            scan.after = lane.scan.after.clone();

   815	fn rule_term(catalog: &Catalog, info: &TableInfo, expr: &Expr, bindings: &RuleBindings) -> Fallible<(RuleTerm, Ty)> {
   816	    let site = expr.site();
   817	    match expr {
   818	        Expr::Name { name, .. } if name == "viewer" => Ok((RuleTerm::Viewer, Ty::Principal)),
   819	        Expr::Member { base, name, .. } => match base.as_ref() {
   820	            Expr::Read(read) if read.keys.len() == 1 && read.keys[0].column.as_deref().is_none_or(|c| c == "id")
   821	                && read.range.is_none() && read.filter.is_none() && read.matching.is_none()

   870	
   871	fn column_ty(info: &TableInfo, name: &str) -> Option<Ty> {
   872	    if name == "id" {
   873	        return Some(Ty::Ref(info.decl.name.clone()));
   874	    }

   877	
   878	/// Final-insert obligations retain their exact-index write-path contract.
   879	/// Ordinary effect rules use `probe` with residual equalities enabled.
   880	pub fn bind_probe(catalog: &Catalog, info: &TableInfo, read: &ReadExpr, site: &Site, update: bool) -> Fallible<(String, String, Vec<RuleTerm>)> {
   881	    let bindings = RuleBindings { old: None, next: None, update, insert: false, hops: true, present: Vec::new() };
   882	    let (table, index, key, _) = probe(catalog, info, read, site, &bindings, false).map_err(|mut error| {
   883	        if error.code == "E_QUARRY_RULE_HOP" { error.rewrite = reference::reference_rewrite(catalog, info, &Expr::Exists { read: read.clone(), site: site.clone() }); }
   884	        error
   885	    })?;
   886	    Ok((table, index, key))
   887	}
   888	
   889	fn probe(catalog: &Catalog, info: &TableInfo, read: &ReadExpr, site: &Site, bindings: &RuleBindings, extras: bool) -> Fallible<(String, String, Vec<RuleTerm>, Vec<(String, RuleTerm)>)> {
   890	    if catalog.tables.get(&read.table).is_some_and(|t| t.decl.ephemeral.is_some()) {
   891	        return Err(Diagnostic::new(site, "E_QUARRY_EPHEMERAL", format!("a rule or after insert cannot probe ephemeral table {}", read.table), "probe a durable table instead").guide("ephemeral"));
   892	    }
   893	    // @ref LLP 3000.013.001#3-global-maintained-view-representation — rules
   894	    // and postconditions must not recover the compiler-owned physical row.
   895	    if catalog.tables.get(&read.table).and_then(|table| table.maintained.as_ref())
   896	        .is_some_and(|maintain| maintain.by.is_empty())
   897	    {
   898	        return Err(type_error(site, format!("{} is one global maintained scalar, not a rule-probe table", read.table),
   899	            format!("read `{}` as a scalar in a query; keep rules over application tables", read.table)).guide("maintains"));
   900	    }

   111	    pub(super) fn maintain(&mut self, source: &str, old: Option<&Value>, new: Option<&Value>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   112	        self.receipt_pending_groups.clear();
   113	        // Inserts ordinarily charge their actual aggregate work. A deferred
   114	        // predicate cannot select that work on a device, so use the same public
   115	        // worst-case reservation as update/delete before skipping it. Already
   116	        // prepaid writes must not pay twice.
   117	        if self.device && !self.maintain_prepaid && self.schema.maintains.iter().any(|m|
   118	            m.source == source && m.private.is_none() && m.row_key.is_none() && (self.schema.receipt_dependencies.contains_key(&m.target) || self.schema.tables[&m.target].sync.is_none())) {
   119	            self.reserve_maintains(source, &self.schema.tables[source])?;
   120	        }
   121	        self.maintain_row_views(source, old, new)?;
   122	        let maintains = self.schema.maintains.iter().filter(|maintain| maintain.source == source && maintain.row_key.is_none());
   123	        for maintain in maintains {
   124	            if self.defer_receipt_maintain(maintain) || (self.device && self.schema.tables[&maintain.target].sync.is_none()) { continue; }
   125	            let source_bytes = super::write_target::row_copy_ceiling(&self.schema.tables[&maintain.source]);
   126	            let target = &self.schema.tables[&maintain.target];
   127	            let bytes = source_bytes.saturating_mul(2).saturating_add(super::write_target::row_copy_ceiling(target).saturating_mul(16 + 4 * target.indexes.len() as u64));
   128	            let prepaid = self.maintain_prepaid;
   129	            if maintain.private.is_some() && self.column_prepaid { self.maintain_prepaid = true; }
   130	            let result = self.maintain_scratch(bytes, |runner| runner.maintain_one(maintain, old, new));
   131	            self.maintain_prepaid = prepaid;
   132	            result?;
   133	        }
   134	        Ok(())
   135	    }
   136	
   137	    // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.9 — reserve interpreted images, both keys, targets and index buffers before allocation; release transient ownership on every exit.
   138	    pub(super) fn maintain_scratch<T>(&mut self, bytes: u64, work: impl FnOnce(&mut Self) -> Result<T, RuntimeError>) -> Result<T, RuntimeError> {
   139	        if self.maintain_prepaid { return work(self); }
   140	        self.meter.heap(bytes)?;
   141	        let result = work(self);
   142	        self.meter.used.heap_bytes = self.meter.used.heap_bytes.saturating_sub(bytes);
