   145	
   146	    fn maintain_one(&mut self, maintain: &Maintain, old: Option<&Value>, new: Option<&Value>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   147	        let table = self.schema.table(&maintain.source).expect("validated");
   148	        let mut changes: Vec<(Vec<Value>, i128)> = Vec::new();
   149	        for (image, direction) in [(old, -1), (new, 1)] {
   150	            let Some(image) = image else { continue };
   151	            self.charge_maintain(MeterKind::Steps, maintain.evaluation_steps())?;
   152	            let image = crate::schema::row_image(image, table);
   153	            if !maintain.selected(&image) { continue; }
   154	            let key = maintain.source_key(&image);
   155	            let change = delta(maintain, &image, direction)?;
   156	            if let Some(existing) = changes.iter_mut().find(|(k, _)| *k == key) { existing.1 += change; }
   157	            else { changes.push((key, change)); }
   158	        }
   159	        for (key, change) in changes {
   160	            #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
   161	            if let Some(retiring) = self.retiring.as_mut() {
   162	                let entry = retiring.aggregates.entry((maintain.target.clone(), canonical_json(&key))).or_insert_with(|| (key, 0));
   163	                entry.1 = entry.1.checked_add(change).ok_or_else(|| aggregate_overflow(maintain))?;
   164	                continue;
   165	            }
   166	            if change != 0 || self.live_sum(maintain) {
   167	                self.apply_aggregate(maintain, key, change, None)?;
   168	            }
   169	        }
   170	        Ok(())
   171	    }

   173	    /// Apply a retirement's net aggregate changes, one per target row, in
   174	    /// key order. Only a total the commit really ends with can overflow.
   175	    #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
   176	    pub(super) fn apply_retired_aggregates(&mut self, changes: BTreeMap<(String, String), (Vec<Value>, i128)>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   177	        let schema = self.schema;
   178	        for ((target, _), (key, change)) in changes {
   179	            let maintain = schema.maintain_for(&target).ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Schema(format!("missing maintain for {target}")))?;
   180	            if change != 0 || self.live_sum(maintain) {

   187	    #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
   188	    pub(crate) fn rebuild_maintain_reported(&mut self, maintain: &Maintain, report: &mut crate::store::adoption_report::AdoptionReport) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   189	        let started = std::time::Instant::now();
   190	        self.rebuilt_source_rows = 0;
   191	        self.rebuild_maintain(maintain)?;
   192	        let rows = self.rebuilt_source_rows;

   194	        Ok(())

   197	    /// Derive every legacy-to-digest aggregate id map to a fixed point, then
   198	    /// rewrite typed references before rebuilding the target rows. The fixed
   199	    /// point matters when one aggregate groups by a reference to another.
   200	    /// Every pass is inside the adoption writer transaction, so no reader can
   201	    /// observe a dangling intermediate reference.
   202	    #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
   203	    pub(crate) fn rewrite_legacy_maintained_references(&mut self, maintains: &[Maintain]) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
   204	        let mut legacy = Vec::new();
   205	        for maintain in maintains.iter().filter(|maintain| maintain.private.is_none() && maintain.row_key.is_none()) {
   206	            let target = self.schema.tables[&maintain.target].clone();
   207	            let fields: Vec<_> = maintain.group_fields().into_iter().map(|field| {
   208	                let kind = target.columns.get(&field).expect("validated maintain group").clone();
   209	                (field, kind)
   210	            }).collect();
   211	            let mut rows = Vec::new();
   212	            let mut bounds = Bounds::default();
   213	            loop {
   214	                let page = self.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| runner.tx.scan(allowance, &maintain.target, "byId", &[], &bounds, Dir::Asc, 256))?;
   215	                if page.is_empty() { break; }
   216	                for (row, encoded) in page {
   217	                    bounds.after = crate::key::decode(&encoded);
   218	                    let old = row.field("id")?.as_str()?.to_owned();
   219	                    let key = fields.iter().map(|(field, kind)| Ok((kind.clone(), row.field(field)?.clone())))
   220	                        .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, RuntimeError>>()?;
   221	                    rows.push((old, key));
   222	                }
   223	            }
   224	            legacy.push((maintain.target.clone(), rows));
   225	        }
   226	
   227	        let mut ids: BTreeMap<String, BTreeMap<String, String>> = BTreeMap::new();
   228	        let mut settled = false;
   229	        for _ in 0..=legacy.len() {
   230	            let mut next_ids: BTreeMap<String, BTreeMap<String, String>> = BTreeMap::new();
   231	            for (target, rows) in &legacy {
   232	                let target_ids = next_ids.entry(target.clone()).or_default();
   233	                for (old, key) in rows {
   234	                    let key: Vec<_> = key.iter().map(|(kind, value)| {
   235	                        let mut value = value.clone();
   236	                        rewrite_legacy_maintained_reference(kind, &mut value, &ids);
   237	                        value
   238	                    }).collect();
   239	                    let next = maintained_id(&key);
   240	                    if old != &next { target_ids.insert(old.clone(), next); }
   241	                }
   242	            }
   243	            if next_ids == ids {
   244	                ids = next_ids;
   245	                settled = true;
   246	                break;
   247	            }
   248	            ids = next_ids;
   249	        }
   250	        if !settled {
   251	            return Err(RuntimeError::Schema("legacy aggregate identity references form a cycle".into()));
   252	        }
   253	
   254	        let references: Vec<_> = self.schema.tables.iter().filter_map(|(name, table)| {
   255	            let columns: Vec<_> = table.columns.iter().filter_map(|(column, kind)|
   256	                has_legacy_maintained_reference(kind, &ids).then(|| (column.clone(), kind.clone())))
   257	                .collect();
   258	            (!columns.is_empty()).then(|| (name.clone(), table.clone(), columns))
   259	        }).collect();
   260	        let mut rewritten = 0_u64;
   261	        for (name, table, columns) in references {
   262	            let mut bounds = Bounds::default();
   263	            loop {
   264	                let page = self.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| runner.tx.scan(allowance, &name, "byId", &[], &bounds, Dir::Asc, 256))?;
   265	                if page.is_empty() { break; }
   266	                for (old, encoded) in page {
   267	                    bounds.after = crate::key::decode(&encoded);
   268	                    let mut next = old.clone();
   269	                    let mut changed = false;
   270	                    if let Value::Row(fields) | Value::Map(fields) = &mut next {
   271	                        for (column, kind) in &columns {
   272	                            let Some(value) = fields.get_mut(column) else { continue };
   273	                            changed |= rewrite_legacy_maintained_reference(kind, value, &ids);
   274	                        }
   275	                    }
   276	                    if changed {
   277	                        self.tx.put(&name, &table, &next, Some(&old))?;
   278	                        rewritten = rewritten.saturating_add(1);
   279	                    }
   280	                }
   281	            }
   282	        }
   283	        Ok(rewritten)
   284	    }
   285	
   286	    /// Build one aggregate from the rows in the store: the target's rows go,
   287	    /// then every source row contributes once. A schema change that adds or
   288	    /// redefines a `maintain` calls this, so a count over existing rows is
   289	    /// right from its first read (day two, LLP 2000.000 §11.2).
   290	    #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
   291	    pub(crate) fn rebuild_maintain(&mut self, maintain: &Maintain) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   292	        let heap = self.meter.used.heap_bytes;
   293	        let saved = self.column_rebuild;
   294	        self.column_rebuild |= maintain.private.is_some();
   295	        let result = self.rebuild_maintain_inner(maintain);
   296	        self.column_rebuild = saved;
   297	        self.meter.used.heap_bytes = heap;
   298	        result
   299	    }
   300	
   301	    #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
   302	    fn rebuild_maintain_inner(&mut self, maintain: &Maintain) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   303	        if maintain.row_key.is_some() { return self.rebuild_row_view(maintain); }
   304	        let target = self.schema.table(&maintain.target).expect("validated");
   305	        let mut bounds = Bounds::default();
   306	        let target_bytes = super::write_target::row_copy_ceiling(target).saturating_mul(16 + 4 * target.indexes.len() as u64);
   307	        self.meter.heap(target_bytes.saturating_mul(256))?;
   308	        loop {
   309	            let page = self.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| runner.tx.scan(allowance, &maintain.target, "byId", &[], &bounds, Dir::Asc, 256))?;
   310	            if page.is_empty() { break; }
   311	            for (existing, key) in page {
   312	                bounds.after = crate::key::decode(&key);
   313	                self.refuse_timeline_write(&maintain.target, crate::schema::Effect::Delete)?;
   314	                let entries = self.tx.delete(&maintain.target, target, &existing)?;
   315	                self.charge_maintain(MeterKind::IndexEntries, entries)?;
   316	                self.row_changed(&maintain.target, Some(&existing), None, Origin::Maintained)?;
   317	            }
   318	        }
   319	        let source = self.schema.table(&maintain.source).expect("validated");
   320	        self.meter.heap(super::write_target::row_copy_ceiling(source).saturating_mul(256))?;
   321	        let mut totals: BTreeMap<String, (Vec<Value>, i128)> = BTreeMap::new();
   322	        bounds = Bounds::default();
   323	        loop {
   324	            let page = self.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| runner.tx.scan(allowance, &maintain.source, "byId", &[], &bounds, Dir::Asc, 256))?;
   325	            if page.is_empty() { break; }
   326	            for (mut image, key) in page {
   327	                self.rebuilt_source_rows += 1;
   328	                bounds.after = crate::key::decode(&key);
   329	                materialize_optional_fields(&mut image, source);
   330	                self.charge_maintain(MeterKind::Examined, 1)?;
   331	                self.charge_maintain(MeterKind::Steps, maintain.evaluation_steps())?;
   332	                if !maintain.selected(&image) { continue; }
   333	                // @ref LLP 3000.028#6 — a total unique owner key has one contribution; backfill streams bounded pages instead of retaining all lifetime keys in a totals map.
   334	                if maintain.own_keys.is_some() {
   335	                    let heap = self.meter.used.heap_bytes;
   336	                    self.meter.heap(target_bytes)?;
   337	                    self.apply_aggregate(maintain, maintain.source_key(&image), 1, Some(true))?;
   338	                    self.meter.used.heap_bytes = heap;
   339	                    continue;
   340	                }
   341	                self.meter.heap(target_bytes)?;
   342	                let key = maintain.source_key(&image);
   343	                let change = delta(maintain, &image, 1)?;
   344	                let entry = match totals.entry(canonical_json(&key)) {
   345	                    std::collections::btree_map::Entry::Occupied(entry) => {
   346	                        self.meter.used.heap_bytes -= target_bytes;
   347	                        entry.into_mut()
   348	                    }
   349	                    std::collections::btree_map::Entry::Vacant(entry) => entry.insert((key, 0)),
   350	                };
   351	                entry.1 = entry.1.checked_add(change).ok_or_else(|| aggregate_overflow(maintain))?;
   352	            }
   353	        }
   354	        for (_, (key, change)) in totals {
   355	            if change != 0 || self.live_sum(maintain) {
   356	                self.apply_aggregate(maintain, key, change, Some(true))?;
   357	            }
   358	        }
   359	        Ok(())
   360	    }

    50	    for expression in ["total first 1 by .id", "total[true]"] {
    51	        let bound = compile(&[("bad.q".into(), format!("{source}\nquery bad(c: cursor ?):\n  return {expression}\n"))]);
    52	        assert!(bound.diagnostics.iter().any(|diagnostic| diagnostic.code == "E_QUARRY_TYPE"
    53	            && diagnostic.message.contains("global maintained scalar")), "{expression}: {:#?}", bound.diagnostics);
    54	    }
    55	    for domain in ["total", "total first 1 by .id"] {
    56	        let bound = compile(&[("bad.q".into(), format!("{source}\nquery badLoop():\n  for row in {domain}:\n    return row\n  return null\n"))]);
    57	        assert!(bound.diagnostics.iter().any(|diagnostic| diagnostic.code == "E_QUARRY_TYPE"
    58	            && diagnostic.message.contains("global maintained scalar")), "{domain}: {:#?}", bound.diagnostics);
    59	    }
    60	    for expression in ["count(total)", "exists total"] {
    61	        let bound = compile(&[("bad.q".into(), format!("{source}\nquery badAggregate():\n  return {expression}\n"))]);
    62	        assert!(bound.diagnostics.iter().any(|diagnostic| diagnostic.code == "E_QUARRY_TYPE"),
    63	            "{expression}: {:#?}", bound.diagnostics);
    64	    }
    65	    for expression in ["min((total where true by byId), .value + 0)", "max((total where true by byId), .value + 0)"] {
    66	        let bound = compile(&[("bad.q".into(), format!("{source}\nquery badExtremum():\n  return {expression}\n"))]);
    67	        assert!(bound.diagnostics.iter().any(|diagnostic| diagnostic.code == "E_QUARRY_TYPE"
    68	            && diagnostic.message.contains("global maintained scalar")), "{expression}: {:#?}", bound.diagnostics);
    69	    }
    70	    let mut engine = engine(source);
    71	    let query = |engine: &mut Engine| engine.query("totalEntries", Context::new("alice", BTreeMap::new())).unwrap().data;
    72	    let private = |engine: &mut Engine, viewer| engine.query("privateEntries", Context::new(viewer, BTreeMap::new())).unwrap().data;
    73	    assert_eq!(query(&mut engine), Value::Int(0));
    74	    assert_eq!(private(&mut engine, "alice"), Value::Null, "an unauthorized empty global stays hidden");
    75	    engine.seed([("moderators".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([
    76	        ("id", Value::Id("mod".into())), ("principal", Value::Id("alice".into())),
    77	    ]))]).unwrap();
    78	    assert_eq!(private(&mut engine, "alice"), Value::Int(0), "authorized absence is the count identity");
    79	    assert_eq!(private(&mut engine, "bob"), Value::Null, "a hidden absence is not zero");
    80	    for id in ["a", "b"] {
    81	        let outcome = engine.mutate("add", None, Context::new("alice", BTreeMap::from([("id".into(), Value::Id(id.into()))]))).unwrap();
    82	        assert!(matches!(outcome.state, WriteState::Sent { .. }), "{outcome:?}");
    83	    }
    84	    assert_eq!(query(&mut engine), Value::Int(2));
    85	    assert_eq!(private(&mut engine, "alice"), Value::Int(2));
    86	    assert_eq!(private(&mut engine, "bob"), Value::Null, "a hidden stored scalar remains null");
    87	    engine.mutate("remove", None, Context::new("alice", BTreeMap::from([("id".into(), Value::Id("a".into()))]))).unwrap();
    88	    assert_eq!(query(&mut engine), Value::Int(1));
    89	    engine.mutate("remove", None, Context::new("alice", BTreeMap::from([("id".into(), Value::Id("b".into()))]))).unwrap();
    90	    assert_eq!(query(&mut engine), Value::Int(0));
    91	    assert!(engine.store().rows("total").unwrap().is_empty(), "zero deletes the physical aggregate row");
    92	    assert!(engine.store().rows("privateTotal").unwrap().is_empty(), "private zero also has no physical row");
    93	}

   107	#[test]
   108	fn matching_count_guard_narrows_only_the_same_required_projection_until_a_write() {
   109	    let schema = r#"
   110	table entries:
   111	  owner: principal
   112	  score: int
   113	  optionalScore: int ?
   114	  by byOwner: owner, id
   115	  public
   116	  insert <- allow
   117	  delete <- allow
   118	  online only
   119	"#;
   120	    let sound = checked(&format!(r#"{schema}
   121	query stats(owner: principal):
   122	  n = count(entries[owner] by byOwner)
   123	  require n > 0 else EMPTY
   124	  return {{ low: min(entries[owner] by byOwner, .score), high: max(entries[owner] by byOwner, .score), total: sum(entries[owner] by byOwner, .score) }}
   125	query filtered(owner: principal):
   126	  require count(entries where .owner = owner) > 0 else EMPTY
   127	  return min(entries where .owner = owner, .score) + 1
   128	mutation freshAfterWrite(owner: principal, victim: entries):
   129	  delete entries[victim]
   130	  n = count(entries[owner] by byOwner)
   131	  require n > 0 else EMPTY
   132	  return min(entries[owner] by byOwner, .score) + 1
   133	"#));
   134	    let result = &sound.programs.iter().find(|program| program.program.name == "stats").unwrap().result;
   135	    assert!(matches!(result, Ty::Object(fields) if fields.values().all(|ty| ty == &Ty::Int)), "{result:?}");
   136	    assert_eq!(sound.programs.iter().find(|program| program.program.name == "filtered").unwrap().result, Ty::Int);
   137	
   138	    for (name, body) in [
   139	        ("different", "n = count(entries[owner] by byOwner)\n  require n > 0 else EMPTY\n  return min(entries['other'] by byOwner, .score) + 1"),
   140	        ("optional", "n = count(entries[owner] by byOwner)\n  require n > 0 else EMPTY\n  return min(entries[owner] by byOwner, .optionalScore) + 1"),
   141	        ("written", "n = count(entries[owner] by byOwner)\n  require n > 0 else EMPTY\n  delete entries[victim]\n  return min(entries[owner] by byOwner, .score) + 1"),
   142	        ("stale_binding", "n = count(entries[owner] by byOwner)\n  delete entries[victim]\n  require n > 0 else EMPTY\n  return min(entries[owner] by byOwner, .score) + 1"),
   143	        ("rebound", "n = count(entries[owner] by byOwner)\n  n = 1\n  require n > 0 else EMPTY\n  return min(entries[owner] by byOwner, .score) + 1"),
   144	        ("joined", "n = count(entries[owner] by byOwner)\n  if flag:\n    n = 1\n  require n > 0 else EMPTY\n  return min(entries[owner] by byOwner, .score) + 1"),
   145	        ("root_rebound", "who = owner\n  n = count(entries[who] by byOwner)\n  who = next\n  require n > 0 else EMPTY\n  return min(entries[who] by byOwner, .score) + 1"),
   146	        ("root_recaptured", "who = owner\n  n = count(entries[who] by byOwner)\n  who = next\n  m = count(entries[who] by byOwner)\n  require n > 0 else EMPTY\n  return min(entries[who] by byOwner, .score) + 1"),
   147	    ] {
   148	        let args = match name {
   149	            "written" | "stale_binding" => "owner: principal, victim: entries",
   150	            "joined" => "owner: principal, flag: bool",
   151	            "root_rebound" | "root_recaptured" => "owner: principal, next: principal",
   152	            _ => "owner: principal",
   153	        };
   154	        let kind = if matches!(name, "written" | "stale_binding") { "mutation" } else { "query" };
   155	        let bound = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), format!("{schema}{kind} {name}({args}):\n  {body}\n"))]);
   156	        assert!(bound.diagnostics.iter().any(|d| d.code == "E_QUARRY_TYPE" && d.message.contains("int ?")), "{name}: {:#?}", bound.diagnostics);
   157	    }
   158	
   159	    let helper = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), format!(r#"{schema}
   160	fn remove(victim: entries):
   161	  delete entries[victim]
   162	  return true
   163	mutation staleHelper(owner: principal, victim: entries):
   164	  n = count(entries[owner] by byOwner)
   165	  removed = remove(victim)
   166	  require n > 0 else EMPTY
   167	  return min(entries[owner] by byOwner, .score) + 1
   168	"#))]);
   169	    assert!(helper.diagnostics.iter().any(|d| d.code == "E_QUARRY_TYPE" && d.message.contains("int ?")), "{:#?}", helper.diagnostics);
   170	
   171	    let authority = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), format!(r#"{schema}
   172	job staleAuthority():
   173	  for row in entries:
   174	    n = count(entries[row.owner] by byOwner)
   175	    as row.owner:
   176	      require n > 0 else EMPTY
   177	      narrowed = min(entries[row.owner] by byOwner, .score) + 1
   178	"#))]);
   179	    assert!(authority.diagnostics.iter().any(|d| d.code == "E_QUARRY_TYPE" && d.message.contains("int ?")), "{:#?}", authority.diagnostics);
   180	
   181	    let self_dependent = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), r#"
   182	table buckets:
   183	  bucket: int
   184	  score: int
   185	  by byBucket: bucket, id
   186	  public
   187	  online only
   188	query staleSelfDependent():
   189	  bucket = 1
   190	  bucket = count(buckets[bucket] by byBucket)
   191	  fresh = count(buckets[bucket] by byBucket)
   192	  require bucket > 0 else EMPTY
   193	  return min(buckets[bucket] by byBucket, .score) + 1
   194	"#.into())]);
   195	    assert!(self_dependent.diagnostics.iter().any(|d| d.code == "E_QUARRY_TYPE" && d.message.contains("int ?")),
   196	        "a stale result must not inherit another count's fresh domain marker: {:#?}", self_dependent.diagnostics);
   197	
   198	    let pure_domain = checked(&format!(r#"{schema}
   199	fn sameOwner(owner: principal):
   200	  return owner
   201	query pureDomain(owner: principal):
   202	  n = count(entries[sameOwner(owner)] by byOwner)
   203	  require n > 0 else EMPTY
   204	  return min(entries[sameOwner(owner)] by byOwner, .score) + 1
   205	"#));
   206	    assert_eq!(pure_domain.programs.iter().find(|program| program.program.name == "pureDomain").unwrap().result, Ty::Int);
   207	
   208	    let builtin_domains = checked(r#"
   209	table labels:
   210	  label: text <= 30
   211	  score: int
   212	  by byLabel: label, id
   213	  public
   214	  online only
   215	table dates:
   216	  day: int
   217	  score: int
   218	  by byDay: day, id
   219	  public
   220	  online only
   221	query folded(label: text <= 10):
   222	  require count(labels[lower(label)] by byLabel) > 0 else EMPTY
   223	  return min(labels[lower(label)] by byLabel, .score) + 1
   224	query currentDay():
   225	  require count(dates[day(now)] by byDay) > 0 else EMPTY
   226	  return min(dates[day(now)] by byDay, .score) + 1
   227	"#);
   228	    for name in ["folded", "currentDay"] {
   229	        assert_eq!(builtin_domains.programs.iter().find(|program| program.program.name == name).unwrap().result, Ty::Int);
   230	    }

   256	#[test]
   257	fn matching_role_guard_narrows_only_the_authorized_keyed_total() {
   258	    let source = r#"
   259	table entries:
   260	  owner: principal
   261	  public
   262	  online only
   263	table staff:
   264	  principal: principal
   265	  role: enum('finance')
   266	  unique principal, role
   267	  read <- .principal = viewer
   268	  delete <- .principal = viewer
   269	  online only
   270	view totals: count entries by .owner
   271	  read <- exists staff[principal = viewer, role = 'finance']
   272	  online only
   273	query raw(owner: principal):
   274	  return totals[owner]
   275	query guarded(owner: principal):
   276	  require exists staff[principal = viewer, role = 'finance'] else NOT_FINANCE
   277	  return totals[owner] + 1
   278	"#;
   279	    let bound = checked(source);
   280	    assert_eq!(bound.programs.iter().find(|program| program.program.name == "raw").unwrap().result, Ty::Int.optional());
   281	    assert_eq!(bound.programs.iter().find(|program| program.program.name == "guarded").unwrap().result, Ty::Int);
   282	    let mut engine = Engine::memory(bound.schema, bound.programs.into_iter().map(|program| program.program)).unwrap();
   283	    engine.seed([("entries".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([
   284	        ("id", Value::Id("one".into())), ("owner", Value::Id("bob".into())),
   285	    ]))]).unwrap();
   286	    let raw = |engine: &mut Engine, owner: &str| engine.query("raw", Context::new("alice", BTreeMap::from([
   287	        ("owner".into(), Value::Id(owner.into())),
   288	    ]))).unwrap().data;
   289	    assert_eq!(raw(&mut engine, "bob"), Value::Null, "a hidden stored total is not disclosed");
   290	    assert_eq!(raw(&mut engine, "nobody"), Value::Null, "a hidden miss is indistinguishable from a hidden hit");
   291	    engine.seed([("staff".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([
   292	        ("id", Value::Id("finance".into())), ("principal", Value::Id("alice".into())),
   293	        ("role", Value::String("finance".into())),
   294	    ]))]).unwrap();
   295	    assert_eq!(raw(&mut engine, "bob"), Value::Int(1));
   296	    assert_eq!(raw(&mut engine, "nobody"), Value::Int(0), "an authorized keyed miss has the count identity");
   297	    assert_eq!(engine.query("guarded", Context::new("alice", BTreeMap::from([
   298	        ("owner".into(), Value::Id("nobody".into())),
   299	    ]))).unwrap().data, Value::Int(1));
   300	
   301	    let stale = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), format!(r#"{source}
   302	mutation stale(owner: principal, grant: staff):
   303	  require exists staff[principal = viewer, role = 'finance'] else NOT_FINANCE
   304	  delete staff[grant]
   305	  return totals[owner] + 1
   306	"#))]);
   307	    assert!(stale.diagnostics.iter().any(|diagnostic| diagnostic.code == "E_QUARRY_TYPE"
   308	        && diagnostic.message.contains("int ?")), "a write must invalidate the role proof: {:#?}", stale.diagnostics);
   309	}

   312	fn email_format_is_one_shared_ascii_contract() {
   313	    let source = r#"
   314	table contacts:
   315	  email: text 3..254 format email
   316	  public
   317	  insert <- allow
   318	  online only
   319	mutation addUnchecked(id: id, email: text 3..254):
   320	  return insert contacts { id, email }
   321	query echo(email: text 3..254 format email):
   322	  return email
   323	"#;
   324	    let mut engine = engine(source);
   325	    for email in ["a@example.com", "first.last+tag@sub.example", "x@localhost"] {
   326	        let result = engine.query("echo", Context::new("alice", BTreeMap::from([("email".into(), Value::String(email.into()))]))).unwrap();
   327	        assert_eq!(result.data, Value::String(email.into()));
   328	    }
   329	    for email in ["a..b@example.com", ".a@example.com", "a@-example.com", "a@example..com", "a@пример.рф", "a@@example.com"] {
   330	        let error = engine.query("echo", Context::new("alice", BTreeMap::from([("email".into(), Value::String(email.into()))]))).unwrap_err();
   331	        assert_eq!(error.code, "E_INPUT", "{email}: {error:?}");
   332	    }
   333	    let rejected = engine.mutate("addUnchecked", None, Context::new("alice", BTreeMap::from([
   334	        ("id".into(), Value::Id("bad".into())), ("email".into(), Value::String("a..b@example.com".into())),
   335	    ]))).unwrap();
   336	    assert!(matches!(rejected.state, WriteState::Failed { ref why, .. } if why.code == "E_CONSTRAINT"), "{rejected:?}");
   337	}

   363	#[test]
   364	fn rounded_division_is_exact_signed_and_keeps_numeric_types() {
   365	    let source = r#"
   366	query rounded():
   367	  return [
   368	    divide(5, 2, 'toward-zero'), divide(5, 2, 'away-from-zero'),
   369	    divide(5, 2, 'floor'), divide(5, 2, 'ceiling'),
   370	    divide(5, 2, 'half-away-from-zero'), divide(5, 2, 'half-even'),
   371	    divide(7, 2, 'half-even'), divide(-5, 2, 'half-away-from-zero'),
   372	    divide(-5, 2, 'floor'), divide(-5, 2, 'ceiling'),
   373	    divide(5, -2, 'floor'), divide(5, -2, 'ceiling'),
   374	    divide(7, 3, 'half-even'), divide(8, 3, 'half-even')
   375	  ]
   376	query decimalValue(value: decimal 2):
   377	  return divide(value, 2, 'half-even')
   378	query moneyValue(value: money USD 2):
   379	  return divide(value, 2, 'half-even')
   380	query scaled(numerator: int, denominator: int):
   381	  return [
   382	    divide(numerator, denominator, 2, 'half-even'),
   383	    divide(numerator, denominator, 2, 'half-away-from-zero')
   384	  ]
   385	query wide(numerator: int, denominator: int):
   386	  return divide(numerator, denominator, 12, 'half-even')
   387	query scaleZero(numerator: int, denominator: int):
   388	  return divide(numerator, denominator, 0, 'half-even')
   389	query scaledOverflow():
   390	  return divide(9007199254740991, 1, 1, 'half-even')
   391	query zero():
   392	  return divide(1, 0, 'half-even')
   393	"#;
   394	    let bound = checked(source);
   395	    assert_eq!(bound.programs.iter().find(|p| p.program.name == "decimalValue").unwrap().result, Ty::Decimal(2));
   396	    assert_eq!(bound.programs.iter().find(|p| p.program.name == "moneyValue").unwrap().result, Ty::Money("USD".into(), 2));
   397	    assert_eq!(bound.programs.iter().find(|p| p.program.name == "scaled").unwrap().result,
   398	        Ty::List(Box::new(Ty::Decimal(2)), 2));
   399	    assert_eq!(bound.programs.iter().find(|p| p.program.name == "wide").unwrap().result, Ty::Decimal(12));
   400	    assert_eq!(bound.programs.iter().find(|p| p.program.name == "scaleZero").unwrap().result, Ty::Decimal(0));
   401	    let mut engine = Engine::memory(bound.schema, bound.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program)).unwrap();
   402	    let values = engine.query("rounded", Context::new("alice", BTreeMap::new())).unwrap().data;
   403	    assert_eq!(values, Value::Array([2, 3, 2, 3, 3, 2, 4, -3, -3, -2, -3, -2, 2, 3].into_iter().map(Value::Int).collect()));
   404	    for name in ["decimalValue", "moneyValue"] {
   405	        let value = engine.query(name, Context::new("alice", BTreeMap::from([("value".into(), Value::Int(5))]))).unwrap().data;
   406	        assert_eq!(value, Value::Int(2), "{name}");
   407	    }
   408	    let scaled = |engine: &mut Engine, numerator, denominator| engine.query("scaled", Context::new("alice", BTreeMap::from([
   409	        ("numerator".into(), Value::Int(numerator)), ("denominator".into(), Value::Int(denominator)),
   410	    ]))).unwrap().data;
   411	    assert_eq!(scaled(&mut engine, 101, 2), Value::Array(vec![Value::Int(5050), Value::Int(5050)]));
   412	    assert_eq!(scaled(&mut engine, -101, 2), Value::Array(vec![Value::Int(-5050), Value::Int(-5050)]));
   413	    assert_eq!(scaled(&mut engine, 1, 8), Value::Array(vec![Value::Int(12), Value::Int(13)]));
   414	    assert_eq!(scaled(&mut engine, -1, 8), Value::Array(vec![Value::Int(-12), Value::Int(-13)]));
   415	    assert_eq!(engine.query("wide", Context::new("alice", BTreeMap::from([
   416	        ("numerator".into(), Value::Int(snapback4_core::value::I53_MAX)),
   417	        ("denominator".into(), Value::Int(snapback4_core::value::I53_MAX)),
   418	    ]))).unwrap().data, Value::Int(1_000_000_000_000), "the wide scaled intermediate is not restricted to i53");
   419	    assert_eq!(engine.query("scaleZero", Context::new("alice", BTreeMap::from([
   420	        ("numerator".into(), Value::Int(5)), ("denominator".into(), Value::Int(2)),
   421	    ]))).unwrap().data, Value::Int(2));
   422	    assert_eq!(engine.query("scaledOverflow", Context::new("alice", BTreeMap::new())).unwrap_err().code, "E_OVERFLOW");
   423	    assert_eq!(engine.query("zero", Context::new("alice", BTreeMap::new())).unwrap_err().code, "E_DIV_ZERO");
   424	
   425	    use snapback4_core::ir::{Builtin, Expr, Program, ProgramKind, Rounding, Stmt};
   426	    let overflow = Program {
   427	        name: "overflow".into(), kind: ProgramKind::Query, args: BTreeMap::new(),
   428	        read_time_granularity_ms: None, native: false, returned_shapes: BTreeMap::new(),
   429	        body: vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Builtin { builtin: Builtin::Divide { rounding: Rounding::TowardZero, scale: None },
   430	            args: vec![Expr::Literal(Value::Int(i64::MIN)), Expr::Literal(Value::Int(-1))] })],
   431	    };
   432	    let mut raw = Engine::memory(Default::default(), [overflow]).unwrap();
   433	    assert_eq!(raw.query("overflow", Context::new("alice", BTreeMap::new())).unwrap_err().code, "E_OVERFLOW");
   434	
   435	    for bad in ["divide(1, 2, 'nearest')", "divide(1, 2, mode)", "divide(1, divisor, 'half-even')",
   436	        "divide(1, 2, 13, 'half-even')", "divide(1, 2, scale, 'half-even')"] {
   437	        let source = if bad.contains("mode") { format!("query bad(mode: enum('half-even')):\n  return {bad}\n") }
   438	            else if bad.contains("scale") { format!("query bad(scale: int):\n  return {bad}\n") }
   439	            else if bad.contains("divisor") { format!("query bad(divisor: decimal 2):\n  return {bad}\n") }
   440	            else { format!("query bad():\n  return {bad}\n") };
   441	        let bound = compile(&[("bad.q".into(), source)]);
   442	        assert!(bound.diagnostics.iter().any(|d| d.code == "E_QUARRY_TYPE"), "{bad}: {:#?}", bound.diagnostics);
   443	    }
   444	}

     9	pub(super) fn explicit_fields<'a>(fields: impl Iterator<Item = (&'a str, &'a Site)>) -> Fallible<()> {

    16	        }

    22	    pub(super) fn object(&mut self, fields: &[ObjectField], out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {

    43	    fn plain_object(&mut self, fields: &[ObjectField], out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {

   136	    /// Write payloads share object syntax. Expand before ordinary column,
   137	    /// maintained-column, identity, default and write-rule validation.
   138	    pub(super) fn expand_object_fields(&mut self, fields: &[ObjectField], table: Option<&TableInfo>, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<Vec<ObjectField>> {
   139	        let mut expanded = BTreeMap::new();
   140	        for (name, value, ty, site) in self.object_fields(fields, table, out)?.0 {
   141	            let temporary = self.temp("field");
   142	            let runtime = self.declare(&temporary, ty);

   145	                value: Some(A::Name { name: temporary, site: site.clone() }), site });
   146	        }
   147	        Ok(expanded.into_values().collect())
   148	    }

   151	/// Shape accepts a metered array of row bases for the union of output metadata.
   152	/// Keep their provenance: recreating an object from field booleans would either
   153	/// lose retained history or mistake an application field for the engine's mark.
   154	fn pending_base(mut bases: Vec<Expr>, retained: bool) -> Option<Expr> {
   155	    let first = bases.pop()?;
   156	    if bases.is_empty() { return Some(first); }
   157	    if retained {
   158	        bases.push(first);
   159	        return Some(Expr::Array(bases));
   160	    }
   161	    // Preserve the existing lowering and its metering when no table can
   162	    // contribute the new retained-row metadata.
   163	    let marked = |base| ir::field(Expr::Shape {
   164	        base: Box::new(base),
   165	        fields: BTreeMap::from([("pending".into(), Expr::Literal(Value::Bool(false)))]),
   166	    }, "pending");
   167	    let pending = bases.into_iter().fold(marked(first), |right, base| Expr::Binary {
   168	        op: BinaryOp::Or, left: Box::new(marked(base)), right: Box::new(right),
   169	    });
   170	    Some(Expr::Object(BTreeMap::from([("pending".into(), pending)])))
   171	}

   144	    pub(super) fn scan_filter(&mut self, read: &ReadExpr, index: &ast::IndexDecl) -> Fallible<(ReadExpr, Option<Box<Expr>>)> {
   145	        let mut read = read.clone();
   146	        let Some(mut predicate) = read.filter.take() else { return Ok((read, None)); };

   154	            let info = self.table(&read.table, &read.site)?;
   155	            let mut parts = Vec::new(); super::super::intent::split(*predicate, &mut parts);
   156	            let mut residual = Vec::new();
   157	            for part in parts {
   158	                let candidate = super::super::intent::comparison(&part).map(|(column, op, value)| (column, vec![op], value))
   159	                    .or_else(|| super::super::intent::equality(&part, info)
   160	                        .filter(|(column, _)| !self.key_ty(info, index, column).is_optional())
   161	                        .map(|(column, value)| (column, vec![">=".into(), "<=".into()], value)));
   162	                if let Some((column, ops, value)) = candidate
   163	                    && index.columns.get(read.keys.len()) == Some(&column)
   164	                    && super::super::intent_aggregates::safe_endpoint(&value, &required)
   165	                    && ops.iter().all(|op| !read.seeks.iter().any(|bound| bound.op.starts_with('<') == op.starts_with('<'))) {
   166	                    for op in ops { read.seeks.push(ast::SeekBound { column: column.clone(), op, value: value.clone(), site: part.site().clone() }); }
   167	                } else { residual.push(part); }
   168	            }
   169	            let Some(remaining) = super::super::intent::conjunction(residual) else { return Ok((read, None)); };
   170	            predicate = remaining;
   171	        }
   172	        // A single next-column comparison is the same seek as its key spelling.
   173	        if read.seeks.is_empty() && read.matching.is_none()
   174	            && let A::Binary { op, left, right, site } = predicate.as_ref()
   175	            && matches!(op.as_str(), ">" | ">=" | "<" | "<=")
   176	            && let A::Member { base, name, .. } = left.as_ref()
   177	            && matches!(base.as_ref(), A::Focal { .. })
   178	            && index.columns.get(read.keys.len()) == Some(name)
   179	            && !has_focal(right)
   180	            && super::super::intent_aggregates::safe_endpoint(right, &required)
   181	        {
   182	            read.seeks.push(ast::SeekBound { column: name.clone(), op: op.clone(), value: *right.clone(), site: site.clone() });
   183	            self.notes.push(Diagnostic::new(site, "N_SEEK", "a `where` on the next index column is a seek; write it in the key", print::read_expr(&read)).guide("reads"));

   189	            && has_focal(&predicate) && !pins_leading_column(&predicate, index.columns.first().map(String::as_str))
   190	        {
   191	            let info = self.table(&read.table, &read.site)?;
   192	            let follows = follow_edge_filter(&predicate, info);
   193	            let consequence = if follows {
   194	                "pages come back short and incomplete when followed authors are sparse (`complete: false`)"
   195	            } else { "sparse matches can exhaust the row meter and return short `complete: false` pages" };
   196	            // @ref LLP 3000.036#12 — wave 2 (024): a `where` every row passes is the trivial repair.
   197	            let rewrite = match always_true(&predicate, info) {
   198	                Some(column) => format!("drop this `where`: .{column} is required, so every row passes it"),
   199	                None if follows => "pin a column with `where .col = value`, choose `by .col`, or read a `view` (see `snapback4 guide feeds`)".to_owned(),
   200	                None => "pin a column with `where .col = value`, choose `by .col`, read a `view` for a count or total, or drop a `where` every row passes".to_owned(),
   201	            };
   202	            self.notes.push(Diagnostic::new(predicate.site(), "N_SPARSE_FILTER",
   203	                format!("{} by {}: this unscoped `where` pins no index prefix; {consequence}", read.table, index.name),
   204	                rewrite).guide(if follows { "feeds" } else { "reads" }));
   205	        }
   206	        let value = self.row_predicate(&read.table, &predicate)?;
   207	        Ok((read, Some(Box::new(value))))
   208	    }
   209	
   210	    pub(super) fn row_predicate(&mut self, table: &str, predicate: &A) -> Fallible<Expr> {
   211	        let row_ty = Ty::Row(table.into());
   212	        let prior = self.focal.replace(("$filter".into(), row_ty.clone()));
   213	        let kind = std::mem::replace(&mut self.kind, ProgramKind::Query);
   214	        let predicate_context = std::mem::replace(&mut self.in_predicate, true);
   215	        let mut body = setup::Body::default();
   216	        self.push_scope();
   217	        // A fresh lexical row snapshot lets short-circuit guards narrow its
   218	        // fields without leaking facts to another scan or an enclosing row.
   219	        self.scopes.last_mut().unwrap().insert("$filter".into(), Binding {
   220	            runtime: "$filter".into(), declared: row_ty.clone(), ty: row_ty,
   221	            parameter: false, revision: 0, fields: BTreeMap::new(), error: None, origins: Vec::new(),
   222	        });
   223	        let bound = self.expr(predicate, &mut body);
   224	        self.pop_scope();
   225	        self.kind = kind;
   226	        self.in_predicate = predicate_context;
   227	        self.focal = prior;
   228	        let (value, ty) = bound?;
   229	        if !ty.is_bool() { return Err(type_error(predicate.site(), format!("where needs a bool predicate, got {}", ty.name()), "where .col = value")); }
   230	        // @ref LLP 3000.007#5.6 — predicate setup, including lazy reads, runs afresh for each rule-visible row.
   231	        Ok(if body.is_empty() { value } else { Expr::Block { body: body.code, value: Box::new(value) } })
   232	    }
   233	}
   234	
   235	fn pins_leading_column(expr: &A, column: Option<&str>) -> bool {
   236	    let focal_column = |value: &A| matches!(value, A::Member { base, name, .. } if matches!(base.as_ref(), A::Focal { .. }) && Some(name.as_str()) == column);
   237	    match expr {
   238	        A::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "and" => pins_leading_column(left, column) || pins_leading_column(right, column),
   239	        A::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "or" => pins_leading_column(left, column) && pins_leading_column(right, column),
   240	        A::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "=" => (focal_column(left) && !has_focal(right)) || (focal_column(right) && !has_focal(left)),
   241	        _ => false,
   242	    }
   243	}
   244	
   245	fn has_focal(expr: &A) -> bool {
   246	    match expr {
   247	        A::Focal { .. } => true,
   248	        A::Name { .. } | A::Literal { .. } => false,
   249	        A::Member { base, .. } | A::Unary { value: base, .. } => has_focal(base),
   250	        A::Binary { left, right, .. } => has_focal(left) || has_focal(right),
   251	        A::Call { args, .. } => args.iter().any(has_focal),
   252	        // Other forms stay predicates; a read on a row-dependent key must not escape the row.
   253	        _ => true,
   254	    }
   255	}
   256	
   257	/// Only the rightmost postfix can be the builder's attempted page shape.
   258	fn trailing_shape(expr: &A) -> Option<(&A, &str, &Site)> {
   259	    match expr {
   260	        A::ShapeUse { base, name, site } => Some((base, name, site)),
   261	        A::Unary { value, .. } => trailing_shape(value),
   262	        A::Binary { right, .. } => trailing_shape(right),
   263	        A::If { otherwise, .. } => trailing_shape(otherwise),
   264	        _ => None,
   265	    }
   266	}

   270	        A::ShapeUse { base, .. } => *expr = *base.clone(),

   277	    /// `connected[p]`: how many connections the principal holds open on the
   278	    /// server now, an int; `exists connected[p]` is the bool. Not a table:
   279	    /// the server's registry of long polls answers it, a device answers 1
   280	    /// and marks its read, and the server decides the write.
   281	    fn connected(&mut self, read: &ReadExpr, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {
   282	        let site = &read.site;
   283	        if read.keys.len() != 1 || read.range.is_some() || read.matching.is_some() || read.index.is_some() {
   284	            return Err(unknown(site, "connected takes one principal: connected[p] counts its connections, exists connected[p] tests", "exists connected[m.principal]"));
   285	        }
   286	        let key = &read.keys[0];
   287	        let (e, t) = self.expr_as_identity(&key.value, out, &Ty::Principal)?;
   288	        if !matches!(t.required(), Ty::Principal | Ty::Id) {
   289	            return Err(type_error(key.value.site(), format!("connected takes a principal, got {}", t.name()), "connected[m.principal]"));
   290	        }
   291	        // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.1 — retain the author's deciding read alongside the IR, never in its serialized form.
   292	        self.presence_sources.push((e.clone(), site.clone(), print::read_expr(read)));
   293	        self.sources.insert(format!("presence/{site}"), (site.clone(), print::read_expr(read)));
   294	        Ok((Expr::Builtin { builtin: Builtin::Connected, args: vec![e] }, Ty::Int))
   295	    }

   297	    pub(super) fn name(&mut self, name: &str, site: &Site) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {
   298	        match name {
   299	            "viewer" | "$input:viewer" => Ok((Expr::Input(Input::Viewer), Ty::Principal)),
   300	            "now" | "commit" => {
   301	                if self.kind == ProgramKind::Query && name == "commit" {
   302	                    return Err(unknown(site, "commit is the mutation commit clock; queries read now", "now"));
   303	                }
   304	                Ok((Expr::Input(Input::Now), Ty::Time))
   305	            }
   306	            "new" => {
   307	                if self.viewer_depth.is_some() { return Err(job_reads::read_only(site)); }
   308	                if self.kind == ProgramKind::Query {
   309	                    return Err(unknown(site, "queries cannot mint ids", "mint ids in a mutation"));
   310	                }
   311	                Ok((Expr::Input(Input::NewId), Ty::Id))
   312	            }
   313	            "true" => Ok((Expr::Literal(Value::Bool(true)), Ty::Bool)),
   314	            "false" => Ok((Expr::Literal(Value::Bool(false)), Ty::Bool)),
   315	            "null" => Ok((Expr::Literal(Value::Null), Ty::Null)),
   316	            _ => {
   317	                if let Some(binding) = self.binding(name) {
   318	                    if let Some(error) = &binding.error { return Err(error.clone()); }
   319	                    return Ok((ir::var(&binding.runtime), binding.ty.clone()));
   320	                }
   321	                if self.catalog.tables.contains_key(name) {
   322	                    return Err(unknown(site, format!("{name} is a table; a read needs a key or a range"), format!("{name}[key]  -- or: {name}[prefix] first 50 by index")));
   323	                }
   324	                let declared: Vec<&str> = self.scopes.iter().flat_map(|s| s.keys().map(String::as_str)).chain(["viewer", "now", "new"]).collect();
   325	                Err(unknown(site, format!("unknown name {name}"), name_rewrite(name, declared, None)))
   326	            }
   327	        }
   328	    }

   336	    /// A local name for the row a reference column names, unused in scope.
   337	    fn row_local(&self, column: &str) -> String {
   338	        let taken = |name: &str| self.scopes.iter().any(|scope| scope.contains_key(name)) || self.catalog.tables.contains_key(name);
   339	        let mut name = column.to_owned();

   345	    /// `x.a.b.c` where `x.a` is a required reference: read the `a` row, then one hop.
   346	    fn second_hop_rewrite(&self, base: &A, target: &str, field: &str) -> Option<String> {
   347	        let A::Member { base: inner, name: hop, .. } = base else { return None };
   348	        let Some(Ty::Ref(first)) = self.speculative_type(inner).filter(|ty| !ty.is_optional()) else { return None };
   349	        let reference = print::expression(inner);
   350	        let row = self.row_local(inner_name(inner).unwrap_or(&first));
   351	        let mut second = self.row_local(hop);
   352	        if second == row { second.push_str("Row"); }
   353	        Some(format!("read the first row, then one hop: `{row} = {first}[{reference}] ! else NOT_FOUND`, then `{row}.{hop}.{field}` (null when the {target} row is missing or hidden); for a present value read that row too: `{second} = {target}[{row}.{hop}] ! else NOT_FOUND`, then `{second}.{field}`"))
   354	    }

   358	        let A::Member { base, name: field, .. } = operand else { return None };

   364	    }

     1	//! One recognition of point keys versus prefix populations, shared by reads,
     2	//! loop domains, merge discovery and program existence tests.
     3	use super::*;
     4	
     5	#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
     6	pub(super) enum Position { Page, Domain, Aggregate, Exists }
     7	
     8	impl Binder<'_> {
     9	    pub(super) fn is_page(&self, read: &ReadExpr, point: ReadMode) -> Fallible<bool> {
    10	        let global = read.keys.is_empty() && self.catalog.tables.get(&read.table)
    11	            .and_then(|table| table.maintained.as_ref()).is_some_and(|maintain| maintain.by.is_empty());
    12	        if !read.order_keys.is_empty() || read.range.is_some() || read.matching.is_some() || read.filter.is_some() || !read.seeks.is_empty() || read.keys.is_empty() && !global { return Ok(true); }
    13	        if global { return Ok(false); }
    14	        // Speculation cannot emit setup or consume compiler names. The selected
    15	        // form binds its key expressions exactly once in the caller's body.
    16	        let mut probe = self.clone();
    17	        let info = probe.table(&read.table, &read.site)?;
    18	        let mut lookup = read.clone();
    19	        if point == ReadMode::FilteredPoint && info.maintained.is_some() && lookup.index.is_none() { lookup.index = Some("byGroup".into()); }
    20	        match probe.select_index(info, &lookup, point, &mut setup::Body::default()) {
    21	            Ok(_) => Ok(false),
    22	            Err(error) if error.code == "E_QUARRY_AMBIGUOUS" => Err(error),
    23	            Err(point_error) => {
    24	                // Only an actual index prefix changes the form. A mistyped
    25	                // full key or an uncovered named equality keeps its point
    26	                // diagnostic, including the established repair.
    27	                let mut probe = self.clone();
    28	                match probe.select_index(info, read, ReadMode::Range, &mut setup::Body::default()) {
    29	                    Ok(_) => Ok(true),
    30	                    Err(error) if error.code == "E_QUARRY_AMBIGUOUS" => Err(error),
    31	                    Err(error) if error.code == "E_QUARRY_POSITIONAL_SCAN" => Err(error),
    32	                    Err(_) => Err(point_error),
    33	                }
    34	            },
    35	        }
    36	    }
    37	
    38	    pub(super) fn prefix_bound(&self, read: &ReadExpr, index: &ast::IndexDecl, position: Position) -> Fallible<(ast::RangeSpec, Bound)> {
    39	        if let Some(range) = &read.range { return Ok((range.clone(), Bound::Literal(range.take))); }
    40	        let ceiling = if self.caller_kind == ProgramKind::Job { snapback4_core::Ceilings::job() } else { snapback4_core::Ceilings::default() }.examined;
    41	        let pinned = &index.columns[..read.keys.len()];
    42	        // One group per distinct source key: a parent-scoped source cap bounds
    43	        // its aggregate rows too, without copying a constraint onto the view.
    44	        // A pinned unique key bounds the group as a cap does (wave 3 #7); `.id = x`
    45	        // plans as the closed seek `.id >= x and .id <= x` on the next column.
    46	        let mut equal = pinned.to_vec();
    47	        if let Some(next) = index.columns.get(read.keys.len()) {
    48	            let printed = |op: &str| read.seeks.iter().filter(|seek| &seek.column == next && seek.op == op).map(|seek| crate::print::expression(&seek.value)).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    49	            let (low, high) = (printed(">="), printed("<="));

     6	// @ref LLP 3000.013#5.1 — choose an access path, leaving aggregate scan

     8	#[derive(Clone, Default)]
     9	pub(super) struct Contexts {

    14	impl Contexts {
    15	    pub fn safe(&self, read: &ReadExpr, value: &Expr) -> bool {
    16	        safe_endpoint(value, self.required.get(&read.site.to_string()).unwrap_or(&BTreeSet::new()))
    17	    }
    18	    fn visit(&mut self, value: &mut Expr, tables: &BTreeMap<String, TableInfo>, functions: &BTreeMap<String, FunctionDecl>, required: &BTreeSet<String>) {
    19	        if let Expr::Read(read) | Expr::Exists { read, .. } = value {
    20	            self.required.insert(read.site.to_string(), required.clone());
    21	        }
    22	        if let Expr::Call { name, args, .. } = value
    23	            && is_consumer(name, args, tables) && !functions.contains_key(name)
    24	            && let Some(Expr::Read(read)) = args.first() && read.range.is_none() {
    25	            self.domains.insert(read.site.to_string());
    26	            if matches!(name.as_str(), "min" | "max") && let Some(field) = args.get(1).and_then(intent::column) {
    27	                self.extrema.insert(read.site.to_string(), field.into());
    28	            }
    29	        }
    30	        consumer(value, tables, functions, required);
    31	    }
    32	}
    33	pub(super) fn order(catalog: &mut Catalog) -> Contexts {
    34	    let mut contexts = Contexts::default();
    35	    for doc in &mut catalog.documents {
    36	        for program in &mut doc.programs {
    37	            let required = required_args(&program.args);
    38	            let mut visit = |value: &mut Expr| contexts.visit(value, &catalog.tables, &catalog.functions, &required);
    39	            intent_walk::statements(&mut program.prelude, &mut visit);
    40	            intent_walk::statements(&mut program.body, &mut visit);
    41	        }
    42	        for function in &mut doc.functions {
    43	            let required = required_args(&function.args);
    44	            let mut visit = |value: &mut Expr| contexts.visit(value, &catalog.tables, &catalog.functions, &required);
    45	            match &mut function.body {
    46	                FunctionBody::Expression(value) => intent_walk::expression(value, &mut visit),
    47	                FunctionBody::Block(body) => intent_walk::statements(body, &mut visit),
    48	            }
    49	        }
    50	        for shape in &mut doc.shapes {
    51	            intent_walk::shape(&mut shape.fields, &mut |value| contexts.visit(value, &catalog.tables, &catalog.functions, &BTreeSet::new()));
    52	        }
    53	    }
    54	    contexts
    55	}

    57	// @ref LLP 3000.019#3.2 — defer overload selection to the typed binder.
    58	// Optional filters and inferred lanes may materialize an ordinary read before
    59	// then. Keep the unpaged domain as an alternative for a scalar projection;
    60	// a binary collection comparison still receives its original page plan.
    61	pub(super) fn remember_domains(catalog: &mut Catalog) {
    62	    for doc in &mut catalog.documents {
    63	        intent_walk::document(doc, &mut |expr| {
    64	            if let Expr::Call { name, args, .. } = expr

    66	                && !catalog.functions.contains_key(name) && intent::column(&args[1]).is_none()
    67	                && let Expr::Read(read) = &args[0]
    68	                && read.range.is_none() && read.matching.is_none()

    70	                    !table.maintained.as_ref().is_some_and(|maintain| maintain.by.is_empty())) {
    71	                catalog.extremum_domains.insert(read.site.to_string(), read.clone());
    72	            }
    73	        });
    74	    }
    75	}

    77	fn required_args(args: &[ArgDecl]) -> BTreeSet<String> {

    81	pub(super) fn is_consumer(name: &str, args: &[Expr], tables: &BTreeMap<String, TableInfo>) -> bool {
    82	    if matches!(name, "count" | "sum") { return true; }
    83	    if !matches!(name, "min" | "max") { return false; }
    84	    let field = args.get(1).and_then(intent::column);
    85	    // @ref LLP 3000.019#3.2 — expression extrema use ordinary aggregate scan planning.
    86	    // Collection-valued second arguments keep binary page comparison planning.
    87	    if field.is_none() && args.get(1).is_none_or(may_be_collection) { return false; }
    88	    // A binary comparison of two pages keeps their observable row order.
    89	    // A focal member absent from this table can belong to an outer shape.
    90	    match args.first() {
    91	        Some(Expr::Read(read)) => tables.get(&read.table).is_some_and(|table|
    92	            extremum_domain(read, table) && field.is_none_or(|field| field == "id" || table.columns.contains_key(field))),
    93	        _ => true,
    94	    }
    95	}

    97	// Before type binding, only select an aggregate order for a definitely scalar
    98	// result. Otherwise leave the source order intact for the binary overload;
    99	// the binder can still admit a scalar aggregate over that ordinary scan.
   100	fn may_be_collection(value: &Expr) -> bool {
   101	    match value {
   102	        Expr::Literal { .. } | Expr::Unary { .. } | Expr::Exists { .. } | Expr::Created { .. } => false,
   103	        Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "??" || op == "+" => may_be_collection(left) || may_be_collection(right),
   104	        Expr::Binary { .. } => false,
   105	        Expr::If { then, otherwise, .. } => may_be_collection(then) || may_be_collection(otherwise),
   106	        Expr::Assert { value, .. } => may_be_collection(value),
   107	        // Calls can be user helpers, including helpers which return pages.
   108	        _ => true,
   109	    }
   110	}
   111	
   112	fn extremum_domain(read: &ReadExpr, table: &TableInfo) -> bool {
   113	    if !read.order_keys.is_empty() || read.range.is_some() || read.matching.is_some()
   114	        || read.filter.is_some() || !read.seeks.is_empty() || read.keys.is_empty() { return true; }
   115	    // Decide the argument's kind before adding an aggregate order. In
   116	    // particular, a complete maintained group lookup is a scalar total.
   117	    // Positional keys without an index retain their point/refusal path;
   118	    // only the binder can resolve their types or diagnose an ambiguity.
   119	    let named = read.keys.iter().all(|key| key.column.is_some());
   120	    if !named && read.index.is_none() { return false; }
   121	    let index = read.index.as_deref().or_else(|| table.maintained.as_ref().map(|_| "byGroup"));
   122	    !table.indexes.iter().any(|candidate| candidate.unique && candidate.is_plain()
   123	        && index.is_none_or(|name| candidate.name == name)
   124	        && (candidate.columns.len() == read.keys.len()
   125	            || named && table.total_unique(candidate) && candidate.columns.len() < read.keys.len())
   126	        && (!named || candidate.columns.iter().all(|column| read.keys.iter().any(|key| key.column.as_ref() == Some(column)))))
   127	}

   129	fn consumer(value: &mut Expr, tables: &BTreeMap<String, TableInfo>, functions: &BTreeMap<String, FunctionDecl>, required: &BTreeSet<String>) {

   133	        }
   134	        Expr::Exists { read, .. } => choose(read, tables, required),
   135	        _ => {},
   136	    }
   137	}

   177	    let source = SOURCE.replace("  at: time", "  until: time\n  by byUntil: until\n  expire at .until\n  at: time");
   178	    let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("expiry.q".into(), format!("{source}\nmaintain totals: count posts by author\n  read <- allow\n  by byCount: value, id\n"))]);
   179	    let diagnostic = bound.diagnostics.iter().find(|d| d.code == "E_QUARRY_MAINTAIN_EXPIRE").unwrap();
   180	    println!("{diagnostic}");
   181	    assert_eq!(diagnostic.message, "maintain totals cannot order by value because source posts expires: stored order is stale before the physical sweep, so taking stored top-N can miss a live winner; a count upper-bound threshold scan is not built");
   182	    assert_eq!(diagnostic.guide, "feeds");
   183	    assert_eq!(diagnostic.rewrite, "for an entity-time window over a non-expiring source, use the Trending predicate in guide feeds; for expiring contributions, stream each candidate's live count and rank only a complete candidate domain; a meter cut makes the ranking incomplete");
   184	}

   186	#[test]
   187	fn fresh_trending_has_no_writable_score_and_an_explicit_public_device_variant() {
   188	    let source = snapback4_lang::guide::quarry(include_str!(concat!(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"), "/../snapback4-lang/tests/fixtures/legacy-feed-recipes.md"))).into_iter()
   189	        .find(|s| s.contains("query rollingLikes():")).unwrap();
   190	    let private = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source.clone())]);
   191	    assert!(private.diagnostics.is_empty() && private.notes.is_empty());
   192	    assert!(!private.schema.tables.contains_key("ranked"));
   193	    assert!(private.schema.schedules.is_empty());
   194	    for name in ["rollingLikes"] { assert_eq!(private.schema.operations[name].runs.as_deref(), Some("server")); }
   195	    let forged = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), format!("{source}\nmutation forge(postId: posts):\n  update likeCounts[postId] {{ value: 999 }}\n"))]);
   196	    assert!(forged.diagnostics.iter().any(|d| d.message == "likeCounts is a maintained aggregate; write its source rows instead"), "{:?}", forged.diagnostics);
   197	    let horizon = private.schema.tables["posts"].sync.as_ref().unwrap().horizon.as_ref().unwrap();
   198	    assert_eq!((horizon.last, horizon.by.as_str()), (2000, "byTime"));
   199	    let public = source.replace("  read <- .principal = viewer", "  read <- allow")
   200	        .replace("  sync to .principal", "  sync public").replace("  online only\n", "")
   201	        .replace("  sync public last 2000 by byTime", "  sync public");
   202	    let public = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), public)]);
   203	    assert!(public.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", public.diagnostics);
   204	    assert_eq!(public.notes.iter().map(|d| d.code.as_str()).collect::<Vec<_>>(), ["N_MAINTAIN_SYNCED"]);
   205	    assert!(public.schema.tables["posts"].sync.as_ref().unwrap().horizon.is_none());
   206	    assert!(public.schema.tables["likes"].sync.as_ref().unwrap().horizon.is_none());
   207	    assert!(public.schema.tables["likeCounts"].sync.is_some());
   208	}

   210	#[test]
   211	fn w14a_hour_lanes_are_the_first_trending_answer() {
   212	    let feeds = include_str!(concat!(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"), "/../snapback4-lang/tests/fixtures/legacy-feed-recipes.md"));
   213	    let first = feeds.split("## Trending:").nth(1).unwrap();
   214	    assert!(first.starts_with(" posts younger than a day, ranked in the like's own transaction"));
   215	    let source = snapback4_lang::guide::quarry(first).into_iter().next().unwrap();
   216	    assert!(source.contains("likeCount: count likes by postId"));
   217	    assert!(source.contains("insert <- .principal = viewer"));
   218	    assert!(source.contains("immutable postId, principal"));
   219	    assert!(source.contains("unique byPair: postId, principal"));
   220	    assert!(source.contains("delete <- .principal = viewer"));
   221	    assert_eq!(source.lines().filter(|line| line.starts_with("mutation ")).collect::<Vec<_>>(),
   222	        ["mutation create(body: text <=500):", "mutation setLike(postId: posts, liked: bool):", "mutation remove(postId: posts):"]);
   223	    assert!(source.contains("and not .removed"));
   224	    assert_eq!(source.matches("[h] for h in range(hour(now) - 24, hour(now) + 1)").count(), 1);
   225	    let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source.clone())]);
   226	    assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
   227	    assert!(bound.notes.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.notes);
   228	    assert!(bound.schema.schedules.is_empty());
   229	    assert_eq!(bound.schema.operations["trending"].runs.as_deref(), Some("server"));
   230	    let forged = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), format!("{source}\nmutation forge():\n  update posts['p'] {{ likeCount: 999 }}\n"))]);
   231	    assert!(forged.diagnostics.iter().any(|d| d.code == "E_MAINTAINED_COLUMN"), "{:?}", forged.diagnostics);
   232	    let public = source.replace("  insert <- .authorId = viewer", "  insert <- .authorId = viewer\n  sync public")
   233	        .replace("  delete <- .principal = viewer", "  delete <- .principal = viewer\n  sync public");
   234	    let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), public)]);
   235	    assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
   236	    assert_eq!(bound.notes.iter().map(|d| d.code.as_str()).collect::<Vec<_>>(), ["N_MAINTAINED_COLUMN_STREAM"]);
   237	    assert_eq!(bound.schema.operations["trending"].runs.as_deref(), Some("device"));
   238	    assert_eq!(bound.schema.operations["setLike"].predicts.as_deref(), Some("eligible"));
   239	    for name in ["posts", "likes"] { assert!(bound.schema.tables[name].sync.as_ref().unwrap().horizon.is_none()); }
   240	}

   269	#[test]
   270	fn cards_pin_retirement_and_newest_first_column_recipe() {
   271	    let maintains = snapback4_lang::guide::card("maintains").unwrap().split_whitespace().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" ");
   272	    assert!(maintains.contains("A maintained view has rows `{ id, <group columns>, value }`."));
   273	    let feeds = include_str!(concat!(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"), "/../snapback4-lang/tests/fixtures/legacy-feed-recipes.md"));
   274	    let source = snapback4_lang::guide::quarry(feeds).into_iter().find(|s| s.contains("likeCount: count likes by postId")).unwrap();
   275	    assert!(source.contains("by byHourLikes: hour(at), likeCount, at, id\n"));
   276	    assert!(feeds.split_whitespace().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" ").contains("Equal scores put the newest post first"));
   277	    let readme = include_str!("../../../packages/snapback4/README.md").split_whitespace().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" ");
   278	    assert!(readme.contains("`guide maintains`") && readme.contains("`guide feeds`"), "the orientation reaches both complete references");
   279	    // The complete ordering law above and the recipe's key now live on these linked cards.
   280	    let reference = format!("{maintains} {}", feeds.split_whitespace().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" "));
   281	    assert!(reference.contains("by byHourLikes: hour(at), likeCount, at, id") && reference.contains("Equal scores put the newest post first"));
   282	    assert!(!reference.contains("ties use maintained id") && !readme.contains("ties use maintained id"));
   283	    let b = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source)]);
   284	    assert!(b.diagnostics.is_empty() && b.notes.is_empty(), "{:?}", b.diagnostics);
   285	}
