   287	#[test]
   288	fn recorded_attribution_admission_and_nullable_keys() {
   289	    let source = include_str!("fixtures/recorded-attribution.q");
   290	    let compile = |source: &str| snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source.into())]);
   291	    let good = compile(source);
   292	    assert!(good.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", good.diagnostics);
   293	    let likes = &good.schema.tables["likes"];
   294	    assert_eq!(likes.recorded.len(), 3);
   295	    assert!(likes.columns["postId.at"].is_optional());
   296	    assert!(good.schema.tables["trend"].columns["hour"].is_optional());
   297	    assert!(good.schema.operations["like"].sites.iter().any(|s| s.table == "posts" && s.kind == "recorded attribution"));
   298	    let types = snapback4_lang::emit::typescript(&good);
   299	    let likes_type = types.split("  likes: {").nth(1).unwrap().split("  };").next().unwrap();
   300	    assert!(!likes_type.contains("postId."), "{likes_type}");
   301	    for bad in [
   302	        source.replacen("read <- allow", "read <- .authorId = viewer", 1),
   303	        source.replace("  immutable postId\n", ""),
   304	        source.replace("immutable authorId, parentId, at", "immutable parentId, at"),
   305	        source.replace("by postId.authorId", "by postId.parentId.authorId"),
   306	        source.replace("by postId.authorId", "by postId.unknown"),
   307	        source.replace("where true by", "where postId.at > 0 by"),
   308	    ] {
   309	        let bound = compile(&bad);
   310	        assert!(bound.diagnostics.iter().any(|d| d.code == "E_QUARRY_MAINTAIN" && d.guide == "maintains" && !d.rewrite.is_empty()), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
   311	    }
   312	}
   889	fn probe(catalog: &Catalog, info: &TableInfo, read: &ReadExpr, site: &Site, bindings: &RuleBindings, extras: bool) -> Fallible<(String, String, Vec<RuleTerm>, Vec<(String, RuleTerm)>)> {
   890	    if catalog.tables.get(&read.table).is_some_and(|t| t.decl.ephemeral.is_some()) {
   891	        return Err(Diagnostic::new(site, "E_QUARRY_EPHEMERAL", format!("a rule or after insert cannot probe ephemeral table {}", read.table), "probe a durable table instead").guide("ephemeral"));
   892	    }
   893	    // @ref LLP 3000.013.001#3-global-maintained-view-representation — rules
   894	    // and postconditions must not recover the compiler-owned physical row.
   895	    if catalog.tables.get(&read.table).and_then(|table| table.maintained.as_ref())
   896	        .is_some_and(|maintain| maintain.by.is_empty())
   897	    {
   898	        return Err(type_error(site, format!("{} is one global maintained scalar, not a rule-probe table", read.table),
   899	            format!("read `{}` as a scalar in a query; keep rules over application tables", read.table)).guide("maintains"));
   900	    }
   901	    // `exists pieces[.id = .piece, …]`: a comparison where a key belongs. Name
   902	    // the column (`id = .piece`) or give the full unique key by position first.
   903	    // @ref LLP 3000.036#11 — app-farm pattern 27: the positional unique-key form.
   904	    for key in &read.keys {
   905	        if let Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } = &key.value && op == "="
   906	            && let Expr::Member { base, name, .. } = left.as_ref() && matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Focal { .. }) {
   907	            let others: Vec<String> = read.keys.iter().filter(|other| !std::ptr::eq(*other, key)).map(|other| match &other.column {
   908	                Some(column) => format!("{column} = {}", crate::print::expression(&other.value)),
   909	                None => crate::print::expression(&other.value),
   910	            }).collect();
   911	            let value = crate::print::expression(right);
   912	            let positional = if name == "id" { std::iter::once(value.clone()).chain(others.iter().cloned()).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ") } else { std::iter::once(format!("{name} = {value}")).chain(others.iter().cloned()).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ") };
   913	            return Err(unknown(&key.site, format!("`.{name} = {value}` is a comparison inside a probe key; in {}[..] a key names a column without a dot (`{name} = {value}`), or gives a full unique key by position first", read.table),
   914	                format!("exists {}[{positional}]", read.table)).guide("rules"));
   915	        }
   916	    }
   917	    let mixed = read.keys.iter().any(|key| key.column.is_some()) && read.keys.iter().any(|key| key.column.is_none());
   918	    let types = if mixed { Some(read.keys.iter().take_while(|key| key.column.is_none()).map(|key| rule_term(catalog, info, &key.value, bindings)
   919	        .map(|(term, ty)| (ty, matches!(term, RuleTerm::Literal(Value::String(_)))))).collect::<Fallible<Vec<_>>>()?) } else { None };
   920	    let normalized = catalog.tables.get(&read.table).map(|target| super::mixed_keys::read_typed(target, read, catalog, types.as_deref())).transpose()?;
   921	    let read = normalized.as_deref().unwrap_or(read);
   922	    super::validate_key_names(read)?;
   923	    if read.range.is_some() || !read.seeks.is_empty() || read.matching.is_some() || read.filter.is_some() { return Err(unknown(site, "a rule probe takes equalities, not a range", "exists t[key = .key]")); }
   924	    let Some(target) = catalog.tables.get(&read.table) else {
   925	        return Err(unknown(site, format!("exists probes unknown table {}", read.table), name_rewrite(&read.table, catalog.tables.keys().map(String::as_str), None)));
   926	    };
   927	    for key in &read.keys {
   928	        if let Some(field) = &key.column { super::row_views::predicate_field(target, field, site)?; }
   929	    }
   930	    let mut terms = Vec::new();
   931	    let mut types = Vec::new();
   932	    for key in &read.keys {
   933	        let (term, ty) = rule_term(catalog, info, &key.value, bindings)?;
   934	        terms.push((key.column.clone(), term));
   935	        types.push(ty);
   936	    }
   937	    let named: Option<Vec<String>> = if terms.iter().all(|(c, _)| c.is_some()) && !terms.is_empty() { Some(terms.iter().map(|(c, _)| c.clone().unwrap()).collect()) } else { None };
   938	    // @ref LLP 3000.007#17 — an expiring maintain's physical value is not its live fact; only existence is a one-unit rule probe.
   939	    if named.as_ref().is_some_and(|names| names.iter().any(|name| name == "value"))
   940	        && catalog.maintains.get(&read.table).filter(|m| m.row_key.is_none()).and_then(|maintain| catalog.tables.get(&maintain.source)).is_some_and(|source| source.decl.expire.is_some() || source.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|t| t.expiring)) {
   941	        return Err(Diagnostic::new(site, "E_QUARRY_LIVE_MAINTAIN_PROBE", format!("a rule cannot probe {}.value because its source expires", read.table),
   942	            "read the view in the program; its live value cannot be probed at one unit").guide("expire"));
   943	    }
   944	    if let Some(names) = &named {
   945	        for ((_, term), (column, ty)) in terms.iter_mut().zip(names.iter().zip(&mut types)) {
   946	            let expected = column_ty(target, column).ok_or_else(|| unknown(site, format!("{} has no column {column}", read.table), name_rewrite(column, target.columns.keys().map(String::as_str).chain(["id"]), None)))?;
   947	            if !ty.comparable(&expected) && !(expected.is_identity() && matches!(term, RuleTerm::Literal(Value::String(_)))) { return Err(type_error(site, format!("{column} is {}; {}.{column} is {}", ty.name(), read.table, expected.name()), format!("use {} for {column}", expected.name()))); }
   948	            if expected.required().is_identity() && let RuleTerm::Literal(Value::String(text)) = term { *term = RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Id(text.clone())); *ty = Ty::Id; }
   949	            if let (Ty::Enum(variants), RuleTerm::Literal(Value::String(text))) = (expected.required(), &*term) {
   950	                if !variants.contains(text) { return Err(type_error(site, format!("'{text}' is not one of {}.{column}'s variants {}", read.table, variants.join(", ")), format!("'{}'", variants[0]))); }
   951	            }
   952	        }
   953	    }
   954	    if named.is_none() {
   955	        let typed = types.iter().cloned().zip(terms.iter().map(|(_, term)| matches!(term, RuleTerm::Literal(Value::String(_))))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   956	        let declared = super::positional_keys::candidates(target, read, &typed, &catalog.synthesized);
   957	        if declared.len() != 1 {
   958	            return Err(super::positional_keys::refusal(target, read, &typed, &declared, catalog));
   959	        }
   960	        if read.index.is_none() {
   961	            let mut selected = read.clone();
   962	            selected.index = Some(declared[0].name.clone());
   963	            return probe(catalog, info, &selected, site, bindings, extras);
   964	        }
   965	    }
   966	    let mut candidates: Vec<&IndexDecl> = target
   967	        .indexes
   968	        .iter()
   969	        .filter(|index| index.is_plain())
   970	        .filter(|index| named.is_some() || !catalog.synthesized.contains(&(read.table.clone(), index.name.clone())))
   971	        .filter(|index| match &read.index {
   972	            Some(explicit) => &index.name == explicit,
   973	            None => true,
   974	        })
   975	        .filter(|index| match &named {
   976	            Some(names) => (index.columns.len() == names.len() && names.iter().all(|n| index.columns.contains(n))) || (extras && target.total_unique(index) && index.columns.iter().all(|column| names.contains(column))),
   977	            None => (index.unique || read.index.is_some()) && index.columns.len() == terms.len(),
   978	        })
   979	        .filter(|index| {
   980	            index.columns.iter().enumerate().all(|(position, column)| {
   981	                let ty = match &named {
   982	                    Some(names) => names.iter().position(|n| n == column).map(|p| &types[p]),
   983	                    None => types.get(position),
   984	                };
   985	                let expected = if column == "id" { Ty::Ref(read.table.clone()) } else { target.columns.get(column).map(Ty::from_scalar).unwrap_or(Ty::Unknown) };
   986	                ty.is_some_and(|ty| ty.comparable(&expected) || (expected.is_identity() && matches!(terms[position].1, RuleTerm::Literal(Value::String(_)))))
   987	            })
   988	        })
   989	        .collect();
   990	    if extras && named.is_some() {
   991	        if let Some(width) = candidates.iter().filter(|i| target.total_unique(i)).map(|i| i.columns.len()).min() {
   992	            candidates.retain(|i| target.total_unique(i) && i.columns.len() == width);
   993	            // @ref LLP 3000.036#11 — named equalities covering two total unique keys
   994	            // test one row either way (the other key's columns are extra
   995	            // equalities on it), so the first declared key decides; no ambiguity.
   996	            candidates.truncate(1);
   997	        }
   998	        else if candidates.iter().any(|i| i.unique) { candidates.retain(|i| i.unique); }
   999	    }
  1000	    if named.is_none() {
  1001	        let unique: Vec<_> = candidates.iter().copied().filter(|index| index.unique && index.columns.len() == terms.len()).collect();
  1002	        if unique.len() == 1 { candidates = unique; }
  1003	    }
  1004	    let index = match candidates.as_slice() {
  1005	        [one] => (*one).clone(),
   100	    }
   101	
   102	    pub(super) fn check_meter_prefix(&mut self, read: &ReadExpr, scan: &Scan, aggregate: bool) -> Fallible<()> {
   103	        if scan.take != Bound::Meter || snapback4_core::engine::rules::scan_visibility(self.schema, scan).uniform { return Ok(()); }
   104	        let table = &self.schema.tables[&scan.table];
   105	        let index = &table.indexes[&scan.index];
   106	        let pinned = index.columns().into_iter().take(scan.prefix.len()).collect::<std::collections::BTreeSet<_>>();
   107	        let used = snapback4_core::engine::rules::deciding_fields(&scan.table, table, &table.rules.read);
   108	        let missing = used.difference(&pinned).cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>();
   109	        let mut leading = missing.clone();
   110	        for column in index.columns().into_iter().skip(scan.prefix.len()) { if !leading.contains(&column) { leading.push(column); } }
   111	        let mut page = read.clone();
   112	        page.range = Some(ast::RangeSpec { order: ast::Order::Asc, take: 50, cursor: Some(Box::new(A::Name { name: "c".into(), site: read.site.clone() })), site: read.site.clone() });
   113	        // @ref LLP 3000.028#4-put-the-rules-where-the-builder-meets-them — preserve the loop, callers and truncation cost in the reason.
   114	        let jobs = self.call_chain.last().filter(|_| self.call_chain.len() > 1)
   115	            .map(|(helper, _)| self.helper_job_callers(helper)).unwrap_or_default();
   116	        let query_site = &self.call_chain[0].1;
   117	        let declaration = self.catalog.source.declarations.get(&query_site.to_string());
   118	        if aggregate && (self.caller_kind != ProgramKind::Query || jobs.is_empty() || declaration.is_none()) {
   119	            // @ref LLP 3000.036#11 — a total has no first-N repair (app-farm lane 087).
   120	            let deciding = if missing.is_empty() { leading.join(", ") } else { missing.iter().map(|c| format!(".{c}")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ") };
   121	            let grant = snapback4_core::engine::rules::grant_decision(self.schema, scan);
   122	            // @ref LLP 3000.036#17.8 — a readable grant the program requires before the total (`require exists staff[viewer]`)
   123	            // makes every row readable for every caller who reaches it, which the server decides once (read.rs `granted`).
   124	            let guard = grant.as_ref().map_or_else(String::new, |grant| format!("; or guard it with the grant, so every caller who reaches it reads every row: `require {} else …` before it (or `if {}:` around it)", grant.quarry(), grant.quarry()));
   125	            let diagnostic = Diagnostic::new(&read.site, "E_QUARRY_BOUND", format!("{} by {}: a total over rows the viewer may only partly read is not decided by its pinned columns, so no page bound makes it exact", scan.table, scan.index),
   126	                format!("pin {deciding} with where equalities so one verdict covers every row (the total then covers only that scope){guard}; {}. codex
The final schema-probe check covers both effect rules and postconditions. I found a separate concern to trace: aggregate identity records focal fields as `focal.owner`, which may let a guard for an outer projection apply to a different row in a nested projection.

     1	//! Wave 12 language parity on the web-memory and SQLite device stores.
     2	mod common;
     3	use common::{Host, Memory, Sqlite, compile, id, snapshot_pages, stream_page};
     4	use serde_json::json;
     5	use snapback4_core::{Context, Value, WriteState};
     6	
     7	const SOURCE: &str = r#"
     8	table entries:
     9	  category: enum('coffee-shop', 'book_store', 'museum')
    10	  contact: text 3..254 format email
    11	  amount: decimal 2
    12	  search category
    13	  public 'wave 12 device fixture'
    14	  insert <- allow
    15	  sync public
    16	view total: count entries
    17	mutation add(id: id, category: enum('coffee-shop', 'book_store', 'museum'), contact: text 3..254, amount: decimal 2):
    18	  return insert entries { id, category, contact, amount }
    19	query totalEntries():
    20	  return total
    21	query search(q: text <=40, c: cursor ?):
    22	  return entries matching q first 20 by .id after c
    23	query extrema():
    24	  n = count(entries)
    25	  require n > 0 else EMPTY
    26	  return { low: min(entries, .amount), high: max(entries, .amount), sum: sum(entries, .amount) }
    27	query rounded(value: decimal 2):
    28	  return [divide(value, 2, 'half-even'), divide(value, 2, 'half-away-from-zero')]
    29	query average(total: int, count: int):
    30	  return divide(total, count, 2, 'half-even')
    31	query email(value: text 3..254 format email):
    32	  return value
    33	"#;
    34	
    35	fn parity<H: Host>(host: &mut H) {
    36	    let (mut server, backend) = compile(SOURCE);
    37	    server.seed([
    38	        ("entries".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([
    39	            ("id", id("a")), ("category", Value::String("coffee-shop".into())),
    40	            ("contact", Value::String("a@example.com".into())), ("amount", Value::Int(5)),
    41	        ])),
    42	        ("entries".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([
    43	            ("id", id("b")), ("category", Value::String("book_store".into())),
    44	            ("contact", Value::String("b@example.com".into())), ("amount", Value::Int(-5)),
    45	        ])),
    46	    ]).unwrap();
    47	    let mut device = host.open("alice", Some(backend)).unwrap();
    48	    let pages = snapshot_pages(&mut server, "alice", 100);
    49	    for (index, page) in pages.iter().enumerate() { device.apply(page, index == 0).unwrap(); }
    50	
    51	    assert_eq!(device.query("totalEntries", "alice", &json!({}), 10).unwrap().data, Value::Int(2));
    52	    assert_eq!(device.query("extrema", "alice", &json!({}), 10).unwrap().data,
    53	        snapback4_core::value::object([("low", Value::Int(-5)), ("high", Value::Int(5)), ("sum", Value::Int(0))]));
    54	    assert_eq!(device.query("rounded", "alice", &json!({"value":5}), 10).unwrap().data,
    55	        Value::Array(vec![Value::Int(2), Value::Int(3)]));
    56	    assert_eq!(device.query("average", "alice", &json!({"total":101,"count":2}), 10).unwrap().data, Value::Int(5050));
    57	    assert_eq!(device.query("average", "alice", &json!({"total":-1,"count":8}), 10).unwrap().data, Value::Int(-12));
    58	    assert_eq!(device.query("email", "alice", &json!({"value":"a@localhost"}), 10).unwrap().data,
    59	        Value::String("a@localhost".into()));
    60	    assert_eq!(device.query("email", "alice", &json!({"value":"a..b@example.com"}), 10).unwrap_err().code, "E_INPUT");
    61	    assert_eq!(device.predict("add", "alice", &json!({
    62	        "id":"bad", "category":"museum", "contact":"a..b@example.com", "amount":0
    63	    }), 10, vec![], 1).unwrap_err().code(), "E_CONSTRAINT", "{}", host.label());
    64	    let hits = device.query("search", "alice", &json!({"q":"cof","c":null}), 10).unwrap();
    65	    assert_eq!(hits.data.as_array().unwrap().len(), 1);
    66	    assert_eq!(hits.data.as_array().unwrap()[0].field("id").unwrap(), &id("a"));
    67	
    68	    let watermark = pages.last().unwrap().watermark;
    69	    let result = server.mutate("add", None, Context::new("alice", [
    70	        ("id".into(), id("c")), ("category".into(), Value::String("museum".into())),
    71	        ("contact".into(), Value::String("c@example.com".into())), ("amount".into(), Value::Int(9)),
    72	    ].into_iter().collect())).unwrap();
    73	    assert!(matches!(result.state, WriteState::Sent { .. }), "{result:?}");
    74	    device.apply(&stream_page(&mut server, "alice", watermark), false).unwrap();
    75	    assert_eq!(device.query("totalEntries", "alice", &json!({}), 10).unwrap().data, Value::Int(3));
    76	    host.close("alice", device);
    77	    let mut reopened = host.open("alice", None).unwrap();
    78	    assert_eq!(reopened.query("totalEntries", "alice", &json!({}), 10).unwrap().data, Value::Int(3),
    79	        "the compiler-owned global discriminator survives store reopen");
    80	    host.close("alice", reopened);
    81	}
    82	
    83	#[test]
    84	fn wave12_semantics_match_on_both_device_stores() {
    85	    parity(&mut Memory::new());
    86	    parity(&mut Sqlite::new("wave12-language"));
    87	}
    88	
    89	#[test]
    90	fn pre_wave_closed_enums_reject_email_and_rounded_division() {
    91	    #[allow(dead_code)]
    92	    #[derive(Debug, serde::Deserialize)]
    93	    enum PreWaveTextFormat { Handle, Url }
    94	    #[allow(dead_code)]
    95	    #[derive(Debug, serde::Deserialize)]
    96	    enum PreWaveBuiltin { Count, Sum }
    97	
    98	    let email = serde_json::to_value(snapback4_core::schema::TextFormat::Email).unwrap();
    99	    let divide = serde_json::to_value(snapback4_core::ir::Builtin::Divide {
   100	        rounding: snapback4_core::ir::Rounding::HalfEven,
   101	        scale: Some(2),
   102	    }).unwrap();
   103	    assert!(serde_json::from_value::<PreWaveTextFormat>(email).unwrap_err().to_string().contains("unknown variant"));
   104	    assert!(serde_json::from_value::<PreWaveBuiltin>(divide).unwrap_err().to_string().contains("unknown variant"));
   105	}
     1	/-!
     2	# Wave 12 semantic witnesses
     3	
     4	These executable definitions cover language contracts outside the row IR:
     5	global count visibility, capture-time count provenance, email validation, and
     6	enum values entering the existing word-search path. Rounded division lives in
     7	`Quarry.Eval` because it is an IR builtin.
     8	-/
     9	
    10	namespace Quarry.Wave12
    11	
    12	def globalCount (rows : List α) : Nat := rows.length
    13	
    14	@[simp] theorem globalCount_empty : globalCount ([] : List α) = 0 := rfl
    15	
    16	theorem positive_count_has_member {rows : List α} (h : 0 < globalCount rows) :
    17	    ∃ row, row ∈ rows := by
    18	  cases rows with
    19	  | nil => simp [globalCount] at h
    20	  | cons row rows => exact ⟨row, by simp⟩
    21	
    22	/-- A readable absent global count is zero; hidden hit and miss are both
    23	`none`, so absence never becomes a disclosure channel. -/
    24	def visibleGlobalCount (authorized : Bool) (stored : Option Nat) : Option Nat :=
    25	  if authorized then some (stored.getD 0) else none
    26	
    27	example : visibleGlobalCount true none = some 0 := rfl
    28	example : visibleGlobalCount false none = none := rfl
    29	example : visibleGlobalCount false (some 7) = none := rfl
    30	
    31	/-- Binder provenance is a capture-time domain, transaction and authority
    32	triple. A retained diagnostic origin is intentionally not part of it. -/
    33	structure CountCapture where
    34	  domain : String
    35	  transaction : Nat
    36	  authority : String
    37	  definition : Nat
    38	  deriving DecidableEq
    39	
    40	def countCaptureLive (capture : CountCapture) (domain : String)
    41	    (transaction : Nat) (authority : String) (definition : Nat) : Bool :=
    42	  capture.domain == domain && capture.transaction == transaction &&
    43	    capture.authority == authority && capture.definition == definition
    44	
    45	example : countCaptureLive ⟨"entries/owner=alice", 4, "alice", 7⟩
    46	    "entries/owner=alice" 4 "alice" 7 = true := rfl
    47	example : countCaptureLive ⟨"entries/owner=alice", 4, "alice", 7⟩
    48	    "entries/owner=alice" 5 "alice" 7 = false := rfl
    49	example : countCaptureLive ⟨"entries/owner=alice", 4, "alice", 7⟩
    50	    "entries/owner=bob" 4 "alice" 7 = false := rfl
    51	example : countCaptureLive ⟨"entries/owner=alice", 4, "alice", 7⟩
    52	    "entries/owner=alice" 4 "bob" 7 = false := rfl
    53	example : countCaptureLive ⟨"entries/owner=alice", 4, "alice", 7⟩
    54	    "entries/owner=alice" 4 "alice" 8 = false := rfl
    55	
    56	/-- A keyed maintained count becomes required only for the exact key whose
    57	role predicate was proved in the same transaction. A readable miss is zero;
    58	hidden hits and misses share `none`. -/
    59	def keyedTotalAfterRoleGuard (guardKey readKey : String)
    60	    (sameTransaction authorized : Bool) (stored : Option Nat) : Option Nat :=
    61	  if sameTransaction && authorized && guardKey == readKey
    62	    then some (stored.getD 0)
    63	    else none
    64	
    65	example : keyedTotalAfterRoleGuard "dept-a" "dept-a" true true none = some 0 := rfl
    66	example : keyedTotalAfterRoleGuard "dept-a" "dept-b" true true (some 7) = none := rfl
    67	example : keyedTotalAfterRoleGuard "dept-a" "dept-a" false true (some 7) = none := rfl
    68	example : keyedTotalAfterRoleGuard "dept-a" "dept-a" true false (some 7) = none := rfl
    69	
    70	def asciiAlphaNum (c : Char) : Bool :=
    71	  let n := c.toNat
    72	  (65 ≤ n && n ≤ 90) || (97 ≤ n && n ≤ 122) || (48 ≤ n && n ≤ 57)
    73	
    74	def asciiLower (c : Char) : Char :=
    75	  if 65 ≤ c.toNat && c.toNat ≤ 90 then Char.ofNat (c.toNat + 32) else c
    76	
    77	def splitOnce (needle : Char) : List Char → Option (List Char × List Char)
    78	  | [] => none
    79	  | c :: cs =>
    80	      if c == needle then some ([], cs) else
    81	        (splitOnce needle cs).map fun pair => (c :: pair.1, pair.2)
    82	
    83	def splitDots : List Char → List (List Char)
    84	  | [] => [[]]
    85	  | c :: cs =>
    86	      let rest := splitDots cs
    87	      if c == '.' then [] :: rest else
    88	        match rest with
    89	        | [] => [[c]]
    90	        | label :: labels => (c :: label) :: labels
    91	
    92	def noAdjacentDots : List Char → Bool
    93	  | '.' :: '.' :: _ => false
    94	  | _ :: cs => noAdjacentDots cs
    95	  | [] => true
    96	
    97	def localPunctuation : List Char :=
    98	  ['.', '!', '#', '$', '%', '&', '\'', '*', '+', '/', '=', '?', '^', '_', '`', '{', '|', '}', '~', '-']
    99	
   100	def localPartValid (part : List Char) : Bool :=
   101	  !part.isEmpty && part.length ≤ 64 && part.head? != some '.' &&
   102	    part.getLast? != some '.' && noAdjacentDots part &&
   103	    part.all fun c => asciiAlphaNum c || localPunctuation.contains c
   104	
   105	def domainLabelValid (label : List Char) : Bool :=
   106	  !label.isEmpty && label.length ≤ 63 &&
   107	    label.head?.any asciiAlphaNum && label.getLast?.any asciiAlphaNum &&
   108	    label.all fun c => asciiAlphaNum c || c == '-'
   109	
   110	def emailValid (email : String) : Bool :=
   111	  let chars := email.toList
   112	  chars.length ≤ 254 && chars.all (·.toNat ≤ 127) &&
   113	    chars.count '@' == 1 && match splitOnce '@' chars with
   114	      | some (localChars, domain) => localPartValid localChars &&
   115	          !domain.isEmpty && domain.length ≤ 253 && (splitDots domain).all domainLabelValid
   116	      | none => false
   117	
   118	example : emailValid "first.last+tag@sub.example" = true := by decide
   119	example : emailValid "x@localhost" = true := by decide
   120	example : emailValid "a..b@example.com" = false := by decide
   121	example : emailValid "a@-example.com" = false := by decide
   122	example : emailValid "a@пример.рф" = false := by decide
   123	
   124	/-- ASCII executable witness for enum spellings using the shared word-search
   125	shape: lowercase, alphanumeric token boundaries, and query-prefix matching.
   126	The Rust/device tests own NFC and the production Unicode tables. -/
   127	def asciiTokens (text : String) : List (List Char) :=
   128	  let rec go (remaining current : List Char) (tokens : List (List Char)) :=
   129	    match remaining with
   130	    | [] => (if current.isEmpty then tokens else current.reverse :: tokens).reverse
   131	    | c :: cs =>
   132	        if asciiAlphaNum c then go cs (asciiLower c :: current) tokens
   133	        else go cs [] (if current.isEmpty then tokens else current.reverse :: tokens)
   134	  go text.toList [] []
   135	
   136	def isPrefix : List Char → List Char → Bool
   137	  | [], _ => true
   138	  | _, [] => false
   139	  | p :: ps, w :: ws => p == w && isPrefix ps ws
   140	
   141	def enumMatchesAscii (value query : String) : Bool :=
   142	  let words := asciiTokens value
   143	  (asciiTokens query).all fun term => words.any fun word => isPrefix term word
   144	
   145	example : enumMatchesAscii "coffee-shop" "cof" = true := by decide
   146	example : enumMatchesAscii "book_store" "BOOK" = true := by decide
   147	example : enumMatchesAscii "museum" "music" = false := by decide
   148	
   149	end Quarry.Wave12

   971	impl TextFormat {
   972	    pub fn matches(self, text: &str) -> bool {
   973	        match self {
   974	            Self::Handle => text.bytes().all(|byte| byte.is_ascii_lowercase() || byte.is_ascii_digit() || byte == b'_'),
   975	            Self::Url => text
   976	                .strip_prefix("https://")
   977	                .or_else(|| text.strip_prefix("http://"))
   978	                .is_some_and(|rest| !rest.is_empty() && rest.chars().all(|c| !c.is_whitespace() && !c.is_control())),
   979	            // @ref LLP 3000.013.001#1-decision — one deterministic ASCII mailbox grammar is shared by arguments, writes and devices.
   980	            Self::Email => {
   981	                if text.len() > 254 { return false; }
   982	                let Some((local, domain)) = text.split_once('@') else { return false };
   983	                if domain.contains('@') || local.is_empty() || local.len() > 64
   984	                    || domain.is_empty() || domain.len() > 253
   985	                    || local.starts_with('.') || local.ends_with('.') || local.contains("..")
   986	                {
   987	                    return false;
   988	                }
   989	                let local_ok = local.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_alphanumeric()
   990	                    || matches!(b, b'.' | b'!' | b'#' | b'$' | b'%' | b'&' | b'\'' | b'*'
   991	                        | b'+' | b'/' | b'=' | b'?' | b'^' | b'_' | b'`' | b'{' | b'|'
   992	                        | b'}' | b'~' | b'-'));
   993	                local_ok && domain.split('.').all(|label| !label.is_empty() && label.len() <= 63
   994	                    && !label.starts_with('-') && !label.ends_with('-')
   995	                    && label.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || b == b'-'))
   996	            }
   997	        }
   998	    }
   999	}

    41	struct Generated {
    42	    seed: u64,
    43	    source: String,
    44	    role_source: String,
    45	    count_domain: String,
    46	    role: &'static str,
    47	    categories: [&'static str; 3],
    48	    search_prefix: &'static str,
    49	    viewer: &'static str,
    50	    granted: bool,
    51	    valid_email: &'static str,

    63	struct Counts {

    69	
    70	fn source(seed: u64) -> Generated {
    71	    let (categories, search_prefix) = if seed % 2 == 0 {
    72	        (["coffee-shop", "book_store", "museum"], "cof")
    73	    } else {
    74	        (["tea-house", "art_gallery", "planetarium"], "tea")
    75	    };
    76	    let role = if seed % 3 == 0 { "finance" } else { "auditor" };
    77	    let viewer = ["alice", "bob", "carol"][(seed as usize) % 3];
    78	    let granted = seed % 4 < 2;
    79	    let population = (seed as usize) % 5;
    80	    let count_domain = if seed % 2 == 0 {
    81	        "entries[owner] by byOwner".to_owned()
    82	    } else {
    83	        "entries where .owner = owner".to_owned()
    84	    };
    85	    let valid_email = [
    86	        "a@example.com",
    87	        "first.last+tag@sub.example",
    88	        "x@localhost",
    89	        "team_member@example-domain.com",
    90	    ][(seed as usize) % 4];
    91	    let invalid_email = [
    92	        "a..b@example.com",
    93	        ".a@example.com",
    94	        "a@-example.com",
    95	        "a@example..com",
    96	        "a@пример.рф",
    97	        "a@@example.com",
    98	    ][(seed as usize) % 6];
    99	    let owners = ["alice", "bob", "carol"];
   100	    let entries = (0..population)
   101	        .map(|i| Entry {
   102	            id: format!("e{seed}-{i}"),
   103	            owner: owners[(i + seed as usize) % owners.len()].to_owned(),
   104	            score: (i as i64 + 1) * if (seed + i as u64) % 2 == 0 { 3 } else { -2 },
   105	            category: categories[i % categories.len()].to_owned(),
   106	            contact: format!("entry{i}@example.com"),
   107	        })
   108	        .collect();
   109	    let source = format!(
   110	        r#"
   111	table entries:
   112	  owner: principal
   113	  score: int
   114	  category: enum('{category0}', '{category1}', '{category2}')
   115	  contact: text 3..254 format email
   116	  by byOwner: owner, id
   117	  search category
   118	  public 'generated Wave 12 semantics'
   119	  insert <- allow
   120	  delete <- allow
   121	  sync public
   122	view total: count entries
   123	mutation add(id: id, owner: principal, score: int, category: enum('{category0}', '{category1}', '{category2}'), contact: text 3..254 format email):
   124	  return insert entries {{ id, owner, score, category, contact }}
   125	mutation addUnchecked(id: id, owner: principal, score: int, category: enum('{category0}', '{category1}', '{category2}'), contact: text 3..254):
   126	  return insert entries {{ id, owner, score, category, contact }}
   127	mutation removeEntry(id: entries):
   128	  delete entries[id]
   129	  return null
   130	query totalEntries():
   131	  return total
   132	query stats(owner: principal):
   133	  n = count({count_domain})
   134	  require n > 0 else EMPTY
   135	  return {{ low: min({count_domain}, .score), high: max({count_domain}, .score), sum: sum({count_domain}, .score) }}
   136	query email(value: text 3..254 format email):
   137	  return value
   138	query search(q: text <=40, c: cursor ?):
   139	  return entries matching q first 20 by .id after c
   140	query rounded(value: decimal 2, numerator: int, denominator: int):
   141	  return {{ preserved: divide(value, 2, 'half-even'), scaled: divide(numerator, denominator, 2, 'half-even') }}
   142	query divideZero(numerator: int, denominator: int):
   143	  return divide(numerator, denominator, 2, 'half-even')
   144	mutation freshCount(owner: principal, victim: entries):
   145	  delete entries[victim]
   146	  n = count({count_domain})
   147	  require n > 0 else EMPTY
   148	  return min({count_domain}, .score) + 1
   149	"#,
   150	        category0 = categories[0],
   151	        category1 = categories[1],
   152	        category2 = categories[2]
   153	    );
   154	    // A role-gated maintained view is deliberately online-only: inheriting
   155	    // `sync public` would disclose every group and the compiler refuses that
   156	    // audience conflict. This family therefore runs on the real server while
   157	    // the public source above runs through both complete device stores.
   158	    let role_source = format!(
   159	        r#"
   160	table entries:
   161	  owner: principal
   162	  public
   163	  online only
   164	table staff:
   165	  principal: principal
   166	  role: enum('{role}')
   167	  unique principal, role
   168	  read <- .principal = viewer
   169	  delete <- allow
   170	  online only
   171	view privateTotal: count entries
   172	  read <- exists staff[principal = viewer, role = '{role}']
   173	  online only
   174	view totals: count entries by .owner
   175	  read <- exists staff[principal = viewer, role = '{role}']
   176	  online only
   177	query privateEntries():
   178	  return privateTotal
   179	query rawRole(owner: principal):
   180	  return totals[owner]
   181	query guardedRole(owner: principal):
   182	  require exists staff[principal = viewer, role = '{role}'] else NOT_FINANCE
   183	  return totals[owner] + 1
   184	mutation freshRole(owner: principal, grant: staff):
   185	  delete staff[grant]
   186	  require exists staff[principal = viewer, role = '{role}'] else NOT_FINANCE
   187	  return totals[owner] + 1
   188	"#
   189	    );
   190	    Generated {
   191	        seed,
   192	        source,
   193	        role_source,
   194	        count_domain,
   195	        role,
   196	        categories,
   197	        search_prefix,
   198	        viewer,
   199	        granted,
   200	        valid_email,
   201	        invalid_email,
   202	        entries,
   203	    }
   204	}

   211	            .collect::<Map<_, _>>(),

    38	pub struct Catalog {
    39	    pub ordinary_searches: Vec<String>,
    40	    pub source: crate::source::SourceMap,
    41	    pub feed_views: BTreeMap<String, super::feed_view::Plan>,
    42	    pub extremum_domains: BTreeMap<String, ReadExpr>,
    43	    pub read_origins: BTreeMap<String, String>,
    44	    pub canonical_read_origins: BTreeMap<String, String>,
    45	    pub view_reads: BTreeMap<String, crate::ast::MergeExpr>,

    47	    pub planner_notes: Vec<Diagnostic>,
    48	    /// Exact cap declarations that would admit an otherwise fully verified
    49	    /// owner-or-membership page, keyed by the read's source site.
    50	    pub membership_cap_repairs: BTreeMap<String, String>,
    51	    pub synthesized: std::collections::BTreeSet<(String, String)>,
    52	    pub documents: Vec<Document>,
    53	    pub tables: BTreeMap<String, TableInfo>,
    54	    pub maintains: BTreeMap<String, MaintainDecl>,

    57	    pub effects: BTreeMap<String, String>,

    59	
    60	impl Catalog {
    61	    pub fn build(documents: Vec<Document>, diagnostics: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>) -> Self {
    62	        let mut tables: BTreeMap<String, TableInfo> = BTreeMap::new();
    63	        let mut maintains = BTreeMap::new();
    64	        let mut shapes: BTreeMap<String, ShapeDecl> = BTreeMap::new();
    65	        let mut functions: BTreeMap<String, FunctionDecl> = BTreeMap::new();
    66	        let mut effects = BTreeMap::new();
    67	        let mut effect_sites: BTreeMap<String, Site> = BTreeMap::new();
    68	        for doc in &documents {
    69	            for table in &doc.tables {
    70	                if tables.contains_key(&table.name) {
    71	                    diagnostics.push(unknown(&table.site, format!("duplicate table {}", table.name), "rename one of the two tables"));
    72	                    continue;
    73	                }
    74	                let mut columns = BTreeMap::new();
    75	                let mut ok = true;
    76	                for column in &table.columns {
    77	                    if column.name == "id" {
    78	                        diagnostics.push(unknown(&column.site, format!("table {} gets id implicitly; remove the id column", table.name), "delete this line"));
    79	                        ok = false;
    80	                        continue;
    81	                    }
    82	                    if columns.insert(column.name.clone(), column.type_.clone()).is_some() {
    83	                        diagnostics.push(unknown(&column.site, format!("duplicate column {}.{}", table.name, column.name), "rename one of the two columns"));
    84	                        ok = false;
    85	                    }
    86	                }
    87	                for field in &table.search {
    88	                    if !matches!(columns.get(field), Some(ScalarType { kind: TypeKind::Text { .. } | TypeKind::Enum { .. }, .. })) {
    89	                        diagnostics.push(crate::diag::type_error(&table.site, format!("search {} requires a text or enum column on {}", field, table.name), format!("declare `{field}: text <=2000` or `{field}: enum('one', 'two')` before `search {field}`")).guide("search"));
    90	                    }
     1	//! Job viewer scopes and calls to the same declared queries used by clients.
     2	use super::*;
     3	
     4	pub(super) fn diagnostic(site: &Site, code: &str, message: &str, rewrite: &str) -> Diagnostic {
     5	    Diagnostic::new(site, code, message, rewrite).guide("jobs")
     6	}
     7	pub(super) fn read_only(site: &Site) -> Diagnostic {
     8	    diagnostic(site, "E_JOB_VIEWER_READ_ONLY", "as is read-only: writes, emits, new ids and once asset consumption have side effects", "compute inside as; act outside the block")
     9	}
    10	
    11	impl Binder<'_> {
    12	    // @ref LLP 3000.007#21.12 — viewer scopes are read-only, under the job's transaction and meter.
    13	    pub(super) fn as_viewer(&mut self, principal: &A, body: &[S], site: &Site, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<()> {
    14	        if self.caller_kind != ProgramKind::Job || self.in_prelude || self.loops + self.caller_loops == 0 || self.viewer_depth.is_some() {
    15	            return Err(diagnostic(site, "E_JOB_VIEWER_CONTEXT", "as belongs inside a job sweep and cannot nest", "put as recipient.principal: inside for recipient in recipients:"));
    16	        }
    17	        let (value, ty) = self.expr(principal, out)?;
    18	        if !matches!(ty, Ty::Principal) && !matches!(principal, A::Literal { value: Literal::Str(s), .. } if snapback4_core::value::identity_fits(s) && s != snapback4_core::SYSTEM_PRINCIPAL) {
    19	            return Err(diagnostic(principal.site(), "E_JOB_VIEWER_PRINCIPAL", "as requires a present non-system principal", "use a principal column or argument; keep intentional system reads outside as"));
    20	        }
    21	        let loops = self.loops;
    22	        self.loops = 0;
    23	        self.caller_loops += loops;
    24	        self.clear_authority_refinements();
    25	        self.viewer_depth = Some(self.depth);
    26	        let mut code = setup::Body::default();
    27	        let result = self.block(body, &mut code, false);
    28	        self.clear_authority_refinements();
    29	        self.viewer_depth = None;
    30	        self.loops = loops;
    31	        self.caller_loops -= loops;
    32	        result?;
    33	        out.push(Stmt::Let { name: self.temp("as"), value: Expr::Builtin { builtin: Builtin::JobAs, args: vec![value, Expr::Block { body: code.code, value: Box::new(ir::lit(Value::Null)) }] } });
    34	        Ok(())
    35	    }
    36	
    37	    // @ref LLP 3000.007#21.12 — calls are reevaluated under the new viewer,
    38	    // including implicit/transitive helper dependencies. Snapshot fields stay
    39	    // valid. Conservatively discard call, point and maintained-authorization
    40	    // facts at both authority edges.
    41	    fn clear_authority_refinements(&mut self) {
    42	        self.call_types.clear();
    43	        for scope in &mut self.scopes {
    44	            for binding in scope.values_mut() {
    45	                binding.fields.retain(|path, _| path.first().is_none_or(|part| {
    46	                    part != "$pureCall"
    47	                        && part != "$point"
    48	                        && part != maintain_types::AUTHORIZATION
    49	                        && part != aggregate_facts::NONEMPTY
    50	                        && part != aggregate_facts::COUNT_CAPTURE
    51	                }));
    52	            }
    53	        }
    54	    }
    55	
    56	    pub(super) fn job_query_call(&mut self, name: &str, args: &[A], site: &Site, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Option<Fallible<(Expr, Ty)>> {
    57	        if self.viewer_depth.is_none() || self.catalog.functions.contains_key(name) { return None; }
    58	        let all = name == "$jobAll" && self.viewer_depth.is_some();
    59	        let (name, args) = if all {
    60	            match args { [A::Call { name, args, .. }] => (name.as_str(), args.as_slice()), _ => return Some(Err(diagnostic(site, "E_JOB_VIEWER_QUERY", "all takes a declared paged query call", "for row in all(unreadPreview(periodStart, periodEnd, null)):"))) }
    61	        } else { (name, args) };
    62	        if name == "all" && self.viewer_depth.is_some() { return Some(Err(diagnostic(site, "E_JOB_VIEWER_QUERY", "all is a streaming for domain", "for row in all(queryName(..., null)):"))); }
    63	        if self.viewer_depth.is_some() && matches!(name, "consume" | "consumeOnce") { return Some(Err(read_only(site))); }
    64	        let caller_module = std::path::Path::new(&site.file).file_stem().and_then(|s| s.to_str()).unwrap_or("schema");
    65	        let (module, decl) = self.catalog.documents.iter().find_map(|doc| {
    66	            let module = std::path::Path::new(&doc.file).file_stem()?.to_str()?;
    67	            doc.programs.iter().find(|decl| super::super::operation_name(module, &decl.name) == name || module == caller_module && decl.name == name).map(|decl| (module.to_owned(), decl.clone()))
    68	        })?;
    69	        Some((|| {
    70	            if self.viewer_depth.is_none() || decl.kind != ProgramKind::Query || decl.native || decl.held {
    71	                return Err(diagnostic(site, "E_JOB_VIEWER_QUERY", "only declared Quarry queries may be called inside a job as block", "compute with a query inside as; call mutations, actions and native operations outside it"));
    72	            }
    73	            let mut diagnostics = Vec::new();
    74	            let query = Binder::bind(self.catalog, self.schema, &module, &decl, &mut diagnostics)
    75	                .ok_or_else(|| diagnostics.into_iter().next().unwrap_or_else(|| diagnostic(site, "E_JOB_VIEWER_QUERY", "query could not be bound", "fix the declared query first")))?;
    76	            if snapback4_core::ir::read_sites(&query.program).iter().any(|read| self.schema.table(&read.table).is_some_and(|table| table.ephemeral.is_some())) {
    77	                return Err(diagnostic(site, "E_JOB_VIEWER_QUERY", "job viewer queries read durable data", "keep ephemeral reads in a client query; persist the state the job needs"));
    78	            }
    79	            if args.len() != query.args.len() { return Err(type_error(site, format!("{name} takes {} arguments", query.args.len()), "pass the same positional arguments as the declared query, including its cursor")); }
    80	            let mut fields = BTreeMap::new();
    81	            for ((name, expected), given) in query.args.iter().zip(args) {
    82	                let (value, ty) = if expected.required().is_identity() { self.expr_as_reference(given, out, expected)? } else { self.expr(given, out)? };
    83	                if !ty.assignable_to(expected) { return Err(type_error(given.site(), format!("{name} requires {}, got {}", expected.name(), ty.name()), "pass the query's declared argument type")); }
    84	                fields.insert(name.clone(), value);
    85	            }
    86	            let cursors: Vec<_> = query.args.iter().filter(|(_, ty)| ty.required() == Ty::Cursor).map(|(name, _)| name.clone()).collect();
    87	            let cursor = (cursors.len() == 1).then(|| cursors[0].clone());
    88	            let ty = if all {
    89	                let Some((item, _)) = query.result.sequence() else { return Err(diagnostic(site, "E_JOB_VIEWER_QUERY", "all needs a query returning a row list", "return the paged list from the query")); };
    90	                if cursor.is_none() || snapback4_core::engine::drain_cursor(&query.program) != cursor { return Err(diagnostic(site, "E_JOB_VIEWER_QUERY", "all needs one cursor argument that owns the returned page", "declare c: cursor ? and return a page after c; start with null")); }
    91	                Ty::List(Box::new(item), u32::MAX)
    92	            } else { Ty::Object(BTreeMap::from([("data".into(), query.result), ("next".into(), Ty::Cursor.optional())])) };
    93	            Ok((Expr::Builtin { builtin: Builtin::JobQuery { program: Box::new(query.program), cursor, all }, args: vec![Expr::Object(fields)] }, ty))
    94	        })())
    95	    }
    96	}
   360	    }
   361	
   362	    fn live_sum(&self, maintain: &Maintain) -> bool {
   363	        matches!(maintain.kind, MaintainKind::Sum { .. }) && self.schema.table(&maintain.source).is_some_and(|source| source.is_expiring())
   364	    }
   365	
   366	    /// A zero sum is still a group while source rows remain: one signed
   367	    /// contribution may expire before the other. Keep its candidate row so
   368	    /// a scan, snapshot and offline replica can discover the later live sum.
   369	    fn aggregate_source_present(&mut self, maintain: &Maintain, key: &[Value]) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
   370	        // One returned source image and its encoded key (MemoryRows also materializes count_prefix).
   371	        let bytes = super::write_target::row_copy_ceiling(&self.schema.tables[&maintain.source]).saturating_mul(2);
   372	        self.maintain_scratch(bytes, |runner| {
   373	            let source = &runner.schema.tables[&maintain.source];
   374	            let index = maintain.contribution_index();
   375	            if !source.indexes.contains_key(&index) { return Err(RuntimeError::Schema(format!("maintain {} needs its contribution index", maintain.target))); }
   376	            runner.charge_maintain(MeterKind::Examined, 1)?;
   377	            let present = runner.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| runner.tx.count_prefix(allowance, &maintain.source, &index, key, 1))? != 0;
   378	            // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.9 — a local positive witness keeps zero; absence needs complete, currently held source coverage.
   379	            if !present && runner.device && !runner.aggregate_source_complete(maintain, key)? {
   380	                runner.unknown_fact(&maintain.source, &index, key, "zero-sum occupancy");
   381	                return Err(RuntimeError::Prediction("the zero-sum group's source occupancy is not known locally; queued without a predicted row".into()));
   382	            }
   383	            Ok(present)
   384	        })
   385	    }
   386	
   387	    fn aggregate_source_complete(&mut self, maintain: &Maintain, key: &[Value]) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
   388	        let source = &self.schema.tables[&maintain.source];
   389	        if source.sync.as_ref().is_none_or(|sync| sync.horizon.is_some()) || self.schema.maintain_subset_unknown(&maintain.target) { return Ok(false); }
   390	        // The target has the same bare audience keys, but no source horizon.
   391	        if !self.upsert_absence_proven(&maintain.target, "byGroup", &target_group_key(maintain, key))? { return Ok(false); }
   392	        for (name, index) in &source.indexes {
   393	            let values = index.columns().iter().map_while(|column| maintain.by.iter().position(|field| field == column).map(|n| key[n].clone())).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   394	            if self.retained_group(&maintain.source, name, &values)? { return Ok(false); }
   395	        }
   396	        Ok(true)
   397	    }
   398	
   399	    fn apply_aggregate(&mut self, maintain: &Maintain, key: Vec<Value>, change: i128, source_present: Option<bool>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   400	        if self.schema.timelines.iter().any(|t| t.owns(&maintain.target)) {
   401	            return Err(super::timeline::refusal("E_TIMELINE_OWNED", "timeline-owned tables cannot be aggregate targets; maintain a mapped application table instead"));
   402	        }
   403	        let id = maintained_id(&key);
   404	        let target = self.schema.table(&maintain.target).expect("validated");
   405	        self.charge_maintain(MeterKind::Examined, 1)?;
   406	        let existing = self.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| runner.tx.get(allowance, &maintain.target, &id))?;
   407	        let target_key = target_group_key(maintain, &key);
   408	        if self.device && existing.is_none() && !self.upsert_absence_proven(&maintain.target, "byGroup", &target_key)? {
   409	            self.unknown_fact(&maintain.target, "byGroup", &target_key, "aggregate base");
   410	            return Err(RuntimeError::Prediction("the affected aggregate value or its absence is not known locally; queued without a predicted row".into()));
   411	        }
   412	        let old_value = existing.as_ref().and_then(|row| row.field("value").ok()).map(Value::as_int).transpose()?.unwrap_or(0);
   413	        let next = i128::from(old_value) + change;
   414	        if !(I53_MIN as i128..=I53_MAX as i128).contains(&next) {
   415	            return Err(aggregate_overflow(maintain));
   416	        }
   417	        let keep_zero = next == 0 && self.live_sum(maintain) && match source_present {
   418	            Some(present) => present,
   419	            None => self.aggregate_source_present(maintain, &key)?,
   420	        };
   421	        if next == 0 && !keep_zero {
   422	            if let Some(existing) = existing {
   423	                self.refuse_timeline_write(&maintain.target, crate::schema::Effect::Delete)?;
   424	                let entries = self.tx.delete(&maintain.target, target, &existing)?;
   425	                self.charge_maintain(MeterKind::IndexEntries, entries)?;
   426	                self.row_changed(&maintain.target, Some(&existing), None, Origin::Maintained)?;
   427	            }
   428	            self.mirror_column(maintain, &key, 0)?;
   429	            return Ok(());
   430	        }
   431	        let mut row = crate::small_map::collect([("id".to_owned(), Value::Id(id.clone())), ("value".to_owned(), Value::Int(next as i64))]);
   432	        for (field, value) in maintain.group_fields().iter().zip(key.iter().cloned()) {
   433	            row.insert(field.clone(), value);
   434	        }
   435	        // @ref LLP 3000.013.001#3-global-maintained-view-representation — stores index the one logical empty group by a hidden constant.
   436	        if maintain.by.is_empty() { row.insert(GLOBAL.into(), Value::Bool(true)); }
   437	        let row = Value::Row(row.into());
   438	        if existing.as_ref() == Some(&row) { return Ok(()); }
   439	        let entries = self.tx.put(&maintain.target, target, &row, existing.as_ref())?;
   440	        self.charge_maintain(MeterKind::IndexEntries, entries)?;
   441	        self.row_changed(&maintain.target, existing.as_ref(), Some(&row), Origin::Maintained)?;
   442	        self.mirror_column(maintain, &key, next as i64)
   443	    }
   444	}
   445	
   446	pub(super) fn delta(maintain: &Maintain, source: &Value, direction: i64) -> Result<i128, RuntimeError> {
   447	    Ok(match &maintain.kind {
   448	        MaintainKind::Count => i128::from(direction),
   449	        MaintainKind::Sum { of } => i128::from(direction).checked_mul(i128::from(source.field(of)?.as_int()?)).ok_or_else(|| aggregate_overflow(maintain))?,
   450	    })

   662	  delete staff[grant]

   664	"#,

+/// Exact rounded division comes from its own stream, preserving every older
+/// case whose stream does not select the new expression.
+fn rounded_division(case: &mut Case) {
+    let mut rng = Rng::new(case.seed ^ ROUND_DIV_STREAM);
+    if rng.pick(6) != 0 || !matches!(case.program.body, Body::Scalar(_)) { return; }
+    let modes = [Rounding::TowardZero, Rounding::AwayFromZero, Rounding::Floor,
+        Rounding::Ceiling, Rounding::HalfAwayFromZero, Rounding::HalfEven];
+    let dividend = if rng.pick(2) == 0 { Expr::Arg("a".into(), Type::Int) }
+        else { Expr::Int([-7, -5, -1, 0, 1, 5, 7][rng.pick(7) as usize]) };
+    let divisor = if rng.pick(2) == 0 { Expr::Arg("b".into(), Type::Int) }
+        else { Expr::Int([-3, -2, -1, 0, 1, 2, 3][rng.pick(7) as usize]) };
+    let scale = match rng.pick(5) { 0 => None, 1 => Some(0), 2 => Some(2), 3 => Some(6), _ => Some(12) };
+    case.program.body = Body::Scalar(Expr::RoundedDivide(
+        modes[rng.pick(modes.len() as u64) as usize].clone(), scale, Box::new(dividend), Box::new(divisor)));
+}
+

 fn timed_disclosure(case: &mut Case) {

+/-- Truncating quotient/remainder followed by one exact rounding step. The
+model deliberately derives truncation from the existing Euclidean primitive,
+so its oracle is independent of the Rust implementation. -/
+def roundedDiv (rounding : Rounding) (scale : Option Nat) (x y : Int) : EM Value := do
+  if y = 0 then refuse "E_DIV_ZERO"
+  let x := x * (10 : Int) ^ scale.getD 0
+  let qe := Int.ediv x y
+  let re := Int.emod x y
+  let q := if x < 0 ∧ re ≠ 0 then qe + (if y < 0 then -1 else 1) else qe
+  let r := x - q * y

+    let away := q + direction

+      | .towardZero => q
+      | .awayFromZero => away
+      | .floor => if direction < 0 then away else q
+      | .ceiling => if 0 < direction then away else q
+      | .halfAwayFromZero => if Int.natAbs r * 2 ≥ Int.natAbs y then away else q
+      | .halfEven =>
+          if Int.natAbs r * 2 > Int.natAbs y ∨
+              (Int.natAbs r * 2 = Int.natAbs y ∧ Int.emod q 2 ≠ 0)
+          then away else q

     1	//! Mutable syntax traversal shared by planning and final index-name resolution.
     2	use crate::ast::*;
     3	
     4	pub(crate) fn expression(value: &mut Expr, visit: &mut impl FnMut(&mut Expr)) {
     5	    match value {
     6	        Expr::Read(read) | Expr::Exists { read, .. } | Expr::Created { read, .. } => read_children(read, visit),
     7	        Expr::Unary { value, .. } | Expr::Member { base: value, .. } | Expr::Assert { value, .. } | Expr::ShapeUse { base: value, .. } => expression(value, visit),
     8	        Expr::Binary { left, right, .. } => { expression(left, visit); expression(right, visit); }
     9	        Expr::Array { items, .. } | Expr::Call { args: items, .. } => { for item in items { expression(item, visit); } }
    10	        Expr::Object { fields, .. } => { for field in fields { if let Some(value) = &mut field.value { expression(value, visit); } } }
    11	        Expr::Shape { base, fields, .. } => { expression(base, visit); shape(fields, visit); }
    12	        Expr::If { then, condition, otherwise, .. } => { expression(then, visit); expression(condition, visit); expression(otherwise, visit); }
    13	        Expr::Walk { seed, .. } => expression(seed, visit),
    14	        Expr::Write { keys, fields, condition, .. } => {
    15	            for key in keys { expression(&mut key.value, visit); }
    16	            for field in fields { if let Some(value) = &mut field.value { expression(value, visit); } }
    17	            if let Some(value) = condition { expression(value, visit); }
    18	        }
    19	        Expr::Merge(merge) => {
    20	            for value in [&mut merge.matching, &mut merge.filter, &mut merge.cursor].into_iter().flatten() { expression(value, visit); }
    21	            let mut lanes = Vec::new();
    22	            for mut lane in std::mem::take(&mut merge.lanes) {
    23	                if let Some(predicate) = &mut lane.admission { expression(predicate, visit); }
    24	                if let Some((_, read)) = &mut lane.nulls {
    25	                    let mut value = Expr::Read(*read.clone());
    26	                    expression(&mut value, visit);
    27	                    let Expr::Read(planned) = value else { unreachable!("planned null scan") };
    28	                    **read = planned;
    29	                }
    30	                if let Some(value) = &mut lane.distinct { expression(value, visit); }
    31	                if let Some(fields) = &mut lane.project { shape(fields, visit); }
    32	                for value in &mut lane.prefix { expression(value, visit); }
    33	                if let Some((_, value)) = &mut lane.loop_ { expression(value, visit); }
    34	                if let Some(read) = &mut lane.planned {
    35	                    let mut value = Expr::Read(*read.clone());
    36	                    expression(&mut value, visit);
    37	                    match value {
    38	                        Expr::Read(planned) => **read = planned,
    39	                        Expr::Merge(nested) if lane.loop_.is_none() => {
    40	                            for mut child in nested.lanes {
    41	                                child.branch = lane.branch.clone();
    42	                                child.project = lane.project.clone();
    43	                                lanes.push(child);
    44	                            }
    45	                            continue;
    46	                        }
    47	                        _ => unreachable!("a planned lane is a read or an expanded read"),
    48	                    }
    49	                }
    50	                lanes.push(lane);
    51	            }
    52	            merge.lanes = lanes;
    53	        }
    54	        Expr::Name { .. } | Expr::Focal { .. } | Expr::Literal { .. } => {}
    55	    }
    56	    visit(value);
    57	}
    58	
    59	fn read_children(read: &mut ReadExpr, visit: &mut impl FnMut(&mut Expr)) {
    60	    for key in &mut read.keys { expression(&mut key.value, visit); }
    61	    for seek in &mut read.seeks { expression(&mut seek.value, visit); }
    62	    for value in [&mut read.filter, &mut read.matching].into_iter().flatten() { expression(value, visit); }
    63	    if let Some(value) = read.range.as_mut().and_then(|range| range.cursor.as_mut()) { expression(value, visit); }
    64	}
    65	
    66	pub(super) fn shape(fields: &mut [ShapeField], visit: &mut impl FnMut(&mut Expr)) {
    67	    for field in fields { if let ShapeField::Field { value, .. } = field { expression(value, visit); } }
    68	}
    69	
    70	pub(super) fn statements(body: &mut [Stmt], visit: &mut impl FnMut(&mut Expr)) {
    71	    for stmt in body {
    72	        match stmt {
    73	            Stmt::Bind { value, .. } | Stmt::Expr { value, .. } | Stmt::Return { value, .. } => expression(value, visit),
    74	            Stmt::Require { condition, .. } => expression(condition, visit),
    75	            Stmt::Emit { payload, once, .. } => {
    76	                expression(payload, visit);
    77	                if let Some(key) = once { expression(key, visit); }
    78	            }
    79	            Stmt::If { condition, then_body, else_body, .. } => { expression(condition, visit); statements(then_body, visit); statements(else_body, visit); }
    80	            Stmt::As { principal: domain, body, .. } | Stmt::For { domain, body, .. } => { expression(domain, visit); statements(body, visit); }
    81	            Stmt::Break { .. } | Stmt::Continue { .. } => {}
    82	        }
    83	    }
    84	}
    85	
    86	pub(crate) fn document(doc: &mut Document, visit: &mut impl FnMut(&mut Expr)) {
    87	    for program in &mut doc.programs { statements(&mut program.prelude, visit); statements(&mut program.body, visit); }
    88	    for function in &mut doc.functions {
    89	        match &mut function.body { FunctionBody::Expression(value) => expression(value, visit), FunctionBody::Block(body) => statements(body, visit) }
    90	    }
    91	    for decl in &mut doc.shapes { shape(&mut decl.fields, visit); }
    92	}

    39	pub(super) fn reference_rewrite(catalog: &Catalog, info: &TableInfo, expr: &Expr) -> String {
    40	    fn lookup(catalog: &Catalog, table: &str, key: &str) -> String {
    41	        // Aggregate rows are addressed by their group, never by a row id.
    42	        let group = catalog.tables.get(table).filter(|t| t.maintained.is_some())
    43	            .and_then(|t| t.indexes.iter().find(|i| i.name == "byGroup"));
    44	        let key = group.map_or_else(|| format!("id = {key}"), |index|
    45	            index.columns.iter().map(|field| format!("{field} = {field}")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", "));

    47	    }
    48	    fn private_guard(info: &TableInfo, field: &str, row: &str, lines: &mut Vec<String>) {
    49	        if let Some(owner) = info.decl.columns.iter().find(|c| c.name == field && !c.readable_by_audience).and_then(|c| c.written_by.as_ref()) {
    50	            lines.push(format!("require {row}{owner} = viewer else FORBIDDEN"));

    56	        let value = if field == "value" && target.is_some_and(|t| t.maintained.is_some()) { local.into() }

    58	        (value,target.and_then(|t| column_ty(t,field)))

    63	                private_guard(info, name, "", lines);

    68	                if let Some(Ty::Ref(table)) = column_ty(info, image).map(|ty| ty.required()) {

    80	                    named = read.keys.first().and_then(|key| key.column.clone()).filter(|column| column != "id");
    81	                    // @ref LLP 3000.036#18 — preserve the typed positional key; viewer is a principal, never an intrinsic row id.
    82	                    if named.is_none() && key == "viewer" && read.keys.len() == 1 {
    83	                        if let Some(target) = catalog.tables.get(&read.table) {
    84	                            let candidates: Vec<_> = target.indexes.iter().filter(|index| target.total_unique(index) && index.columns.len() == 1
    85	                                && target.columns.get(&index.columns[0]).is_some_and(|ty| ty.kind == TypeKind::Principal)
    86	                                && read.index.as_ref().is_none_or(|name| *name == index.name)).collect();
    87	                            if candidates.len() == 1 { named = Some(candidates[0].columns[0].clone()); }
    88	                        }
    89	                    }
    90	                    (key, Some(Ty::Ref(read.table.clone())))
    91	                } else { render(catalog, info, base, lines) };
    92	                if let Some(Ty::Ref(table)) = ty.map(|ty| ty.required()) {
    93	                    let local = format!("rule_parent_{}", lines.len());
    94	                    let target = match &named { Some(column) => format!("{table}[{column} = {key}]"), None if matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Read(_)) => format!("{table}[{key}]"), None => lookup(catalog, &table, &key) };
    95	                    lines.push(format!("{local} = {target} ! else FORBIDDEN"));
    96	                    if let Some(target) = catalog.tables.get(&table) { private_guard(target, name, &format!("{local}."), lines); }
    97	                    selected(catalog,&table,name,&local)
    98	                } else { (crate::print::expression(expr), None) }
    99	            }
   100	            Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } => {
   101	                let left = render(catalog, info, left, lines).0;
   102	                let right = render(catalog, info, right, lines).0;
   103	                (format!("({left} {op} {right})"), None)
   104	            }
   105	            Expr::Unary { op, value, .. } => (format!("{op} ({})", render(catalog, info, value, lines).0), None),
   106	            Expr::Exists { read, .. } => {
   107	                let keys = read.keys.iter().map(|key| {
   108	                    let value = render(catalog, info, &key.value, lines).0;
   109	                    key.column.as_ref().map_or(value.clone(), |col| format!("{col} = {value}"))
   110	                }).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ");

   113	            }
   114	            _ => (crate::print::expression(expr), None),
   115	        }
   116	    }
   117	    let mut lines = Vec::new();
   118	    let (predicate, _) = render(catalog, info, expr, &mut lines);
   119	    lines.push(format!("require {predicate} else FORBIDDEN"));
   120	    lines.join("\n")
   121	}

   139	pub(super) fn choose_consumer(name: &str, args: &mut [Expr], tables: &BTreeMap<String, TableInfo>, required: &BTreeSet<String>) {
   140	    if !is_consumer(name, args, tables) { return; }
   141	    let field = if matches!(name, "min" | "max") { args.get(1).and_then(intent::column).map(str::to_owned) } else { None };

   159	
   160	pub(super) fn choose(read: &mut ReadExpr, tables: &BTreeMap<String, TableInfo>, required: &BTreeSet<String>) {
   161	    // A page bound makes row order observable even inside count/sum. Explicit
   162	    // authored order/index choices also retain their existing meaning.
   163	    if read.index.is_some() || !read.order_keys.is_empty() || read.range.is_some() || read.matching.is_some() { return; }
   164	    let Some(table) = tables.get(&read.table) else { return; };
   165	    let mut conjuncts = Vec::new();
   166	    if let Some(filter) = &read.filter { intent::split(*filter.clone(), &mut conjuncts); }
   167	    let mut pins = read.keys.iter().filter_map(|key| key.column.clone()).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
   168	    pins.extend(conjuncts.iter().filter_map(|value| intent::equality(value, table).map(|(column, _)| column)));
   169	    // A pinned intrinsic id already bounds the read to one row. Inserting an
   170	    // order column after this prefix would create the invalid (id, col, id).
   171	    if pins.contains("id") { return; }
   172	    let mut ranges = BTreeMap::<String, (bool, bool)>::new();
   173	    let mut unsafe_columns = BTreeSet::new();
   174	    for (column, op, endpoint) in conjuncts.iter().filter_map(intent::comparison)
   175	        .chain(read.seeks.iter().map(|seek| (seek.column.clone(), seek.op.clone(), seek.value.clone()))) {
   176	        if pins.contains(&column) { continue; }
   177	        if !safe_endpoint(&endpoint, required) { unsafe_columns.insert(column); continue; }
   178	        let bound = ranges.entry(column).or_default();
   179	        bound.0 |= op.starts_with('>');
   180	        bound.1 |= op.starts_with('<');
   181	    }
   182	    // Choosing the order makes ordinary planning extract both endpoints. A
   183	    // safe sibling must not hoist another bound out of its short-circuit guard.
   184	    ranges.retain(|column, _| !unsafe_columns.contains(column));
   185	    // Prefer a two-sided window, then a canonical column name. Permuting
   186	    // conjunctions must not churn an automatically chosen index identity.
   187	    let column = ranges.iter().find(|(_, (lower, upper))| *lower && *upper).or_else(|| ranges.first_key_value());
   188	    if let Some((column, _)) = column { read.order_keys.push(column.clone()); }
   189	}
   190	
   191	pub(super) fn safe_endpoint(value: &Expr, required: &BTreeSet<String>) -> bool {
   192	    // These values are present before any row predicate runs. Parameters
   193	    // cannot be reassigned or shadowed. Numeric interval proofs admit only
   194	    // arithmetic that stays within the safe-integer range for every input.
   195	    match value {
   196	        Expr::Literal { value, .. } => !matches!(value, Literal::Null),
   197	        Expr::Name { name, .. } => matches!(name.as_str(), "now" | "viewer") || required.contains(name),
   198	        Expr::Unary { .. } | Expr::Binary { .. } => numeric_interval(value, required).is_some(),
   199	        _ => false,
   200	    }
   201	}
   202	
   203	
   204	fn numeric_interval(value: &Expr, required: &BTreeSet<String>) -> Option<(i128, i128)> {
   205	    let max = i128::from(snapback4_core::value::I53_MAX);
   206	    let (low, high) = match value {
   207	        Expr::Literal { value: Literal::Number(number), .. } => { let n = number.parse().ok()?; (n, n) }
   208	        Expr::Name { .. } if safe_endpoint(value, required) => (-max, max),
   209	        Expr::Unary { op, value, .. } if op == "-" => { let (lo, hi) = numeric_interval(value, required)?; (-hi, -lo) }
   210	        Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } => {
   211	            let (a, b) = numeric_interval(left, required)?;

   218	            }
   219	        }
   220	        _ => return None,
   221	    };

   111	    if length < min as usize {
   112	        return Err(RuntimeError::Input(format!("{label} needs at least {min} {}; got {length}", if min == 1 { "item" } else { "items" })));

   116	
   117	fn describe(value: &Value) -> String {
   118	    match value {
   119	        Value::String(text) if text.chars().count() > 24 => format!("{} characters of text", text.chars().count()),
   120	        Value::String(text) => format!("the text '{text}'"),
   121	        Value::Id(id) => format!("the id '{id}'"),
   122	        Value::Int(n) => format!("the number {n}"),
   123	        Value::Bool(b) => format!("the boolean {b}"),
   124	        Value::Null => "null".into(),
   125	        Value::Bytes(b) => format!("{} bytes", b.len()),
   126	        Value::Array(items) => format!("a list of {}", items.len()),
   127	        Value::Row(_) | Value::Map(_) => "an object".into(),
   128	    }
   129	}
   130	
   131	pub fn validate_text(label: &str, text: &str, min: u32, format: Option<TextFormat>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   132	    let length = text.chars().count();
   133	    if length < min as usize {
   134	        return Err(RuntimeError::Input(format!("{label} needs at least {min} characters; got {length}")));
   135	    }
   136	    if let Some(format) = format
   137	        && !format.matches(text)
   138	    {
   139	        return Err(RuntimeError::Input(match format {
   140	            TextFormat::Handle => format!("{label} must be a handle: lowercase letters, digits and underscores"),
   141	            TextFormat::Url => format!("{label} must be an http or https URL"),
   142	            TextFormat::Email => format!("{label} must be an ASCII email address with one @, a dot-safe local part, and valid domain labels"),
   143	        }));
   144	    }
   145	    Ok(())
   146	}

   148	fn disallowed_control(kind: &ColumnType, value: Option<&Value>) -> Option<u32> {
   149	    match (kind, value) {
   150	        (ColumnType::Optional(inner), Some(value)) => disallowed_control(inner, Some(value)),
   151	        (ColumnType::List { of, .. } | ColumnType::ListRange { of, .. }, Some(Value::Array(values))) => values.iter().find_map(|value| disallowed_control(of, Some(value))),
   152	        (ColumnType::Text { .. }, Some(Value::String(text))) => disallowed_text_control(text),
   153	        _ => None,
   154	    }
   155	}

    96	    // @ref LLP 3000.013#2.2 — post-write presence follows only a stable
    97	    // point key and a read grant whose deciding state the write cannot change.
    98	    pub(super) fn invalidate_after_write(&mut self, expr: &A) {
    99	        let A::Write { op: WriteOp::Update, table, fields, .. } = expr else {
   100	            self.invalidate_points(); return;
   101	        };
   102	        let Some(definition) = self.schema.table(table) else { self.invalidate_points(); return };
   103	        let mut deciding = BTreeSet::new();
   104	        definition.rules.read.fields(&mut deciding);
   105	        let independent = self.row_independent_read_proved(table);
   106	        let stable_read = independent || !fields.iter().any(|field| deciding.contains(&field.name));
   107	        // Maintained columns and self-probes can change indirectly. Lifetimes
   108	        // and through authority retain the conservative transaction barrier.
   109	        let safe = self.ordinary_write_lifetime(table) && stable_read
   110	            && definition.maintained.is_empty()
   111	            && !rule_probes(&definition.rules.read, table)
   112	            && rule_dependencies_stable(self.schema, &definition.rules.read);
   113	        for scope in &mut self.scopes {
   114	            for binding in scope.values_mut() {
   115	                binding.fields.retain(|key, _| match key.first().map(String::as_str) {
   116	                    Some(POINT) => safe && key.get(1) == Some(table) && key.get(2)
   117	                        .and_then(|index| definition.index(index)).is_some_and(|index|
   118	                            !fields.iter().any(|field| index.columns().contains(&field.name)
   119	                                || key.iter().skip(3).any(|part| part.starts_with(&format!("Some({:?}):", field.name))))),
   120	                    Some(maintain_types::AUTHORIZATION) => false,
   121	                    Some(aggregate_facts::NONEMPTY) => false,
   122	                    Some(aggregate_facts::COUNT_CAPTURE) => false,
   123	                    _ => true,
   124	                });
   125	            }
   126	        }
   127	    }
   128	
   129	    pub(super) fn ordinary_write_lifetime(&self, table: &str) -> bool {
   130	        self.schema.table(table).is_some_and(|table| table.expire.is_none()
   131	            && table.not_before.is_none() && table.through.is_none() && table.ephemeral.is_none())
   132	    }
   133	
   134	    pub(super) fn row_independent_read_proved(&self, table: &str) -> bool {
   135	        let Some(info) = self.catalog.tables.get(table) else { return false };
   136	        if self.schema.tables[table].rules.read.is_uniformly_readable() { return true; }
   137	        let Some(rule) = info.decl.rules.iter().find(|rule| rule.effect == ast::Effect::Read) else { return false };
   138	        fn proved(binder: &Binder<'_>, table: &str, value: &A) -> bool {
   139	            if let A::Binary { op, left, right, .. } = value && op == "or" {
   140	                return proved(binder, table, left) || proved(binder, table, right);
   141	            }
   142	            let mut value = value.clone();
   143	            maintain_types::normalize_rule_constants(&mut value);
   144	            let mut row_dependent = false;
   145	            super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut value, &mut |expr| {
   146	                if matches!(expr, A::Focal { .. }) { row_dependent = true; }
   147	                if let A::Call { name, .. } = expr && binder.catalog.functions.contains_key(name) {
   148	                    row_dependent = true;
   149	                }
   150	                if let A::Read(read) | A::Exists { read, .. } | A::Created { read, .. } = expr {
   151	                    row_dependent |= read.table == table || binder.catalog.tables.get(&read.table)
   152	                        .is_none_or(|info| info.maintained.is_some())
   153	                        || binder.schema.table(&read.table).is_none_or(|target| !target.maintained.is_empty() || target.through.is_some());
   154	                }
   155	            });

NOT READY

- **P2 — Non-empty proofs cross focal-row scopes.** At `8ef64d411f:snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/program/aggregate_facts.rs:161–162`, every focal field becomes the same identity (`focal.owner`), without recording its lexical row. The fact consequently lives on the constants anchor. Nested shapes retain that anchor while replacing the focal row (`bind/program.rs:966–970`).

  For public `owners` and `entries(owner, score)` with `byOwner(owner, id)`, this pattern incorrectly narrows the inner minimum:

  ```quarry
  query bad():
    return owners first 2 by .id {
      nested: (owners first 2 by .id {
        low: min(entries[.owner] by byOwner, .score) + 1
      }) if count(entries[.owner] by byOwner) > 0 else []
    }
  ```

  An outer Alice row with entries proves nothing about an inner Bob row without entries, yet `extrema.rs:99,132` treats Bob’s minimum as present. Arithmetic can therefore receive null despite passing static checks. Include the enclosing focal binding’s identity in domain keys and distinguish it from the aggregate filter’s focal row.

Static review of committed objects; no tests executed.
